web/beats.js — 100% lines
   1 /* 檔案用途：手動來源拍點、八拍位置及可取消提示音。所在層：Web 拍點邏輯。主要關聯：main.js、library.js、RECORD_COMPARE.md。 */
   2 /** @param {unknown} input @param {number} durationMs @returns {import('./types').BeatGrid} */
   3 export function validateBeatGrid(input, durationMs) {
   4   const grid = /** @type {import('./types').BeatGrid} */ (input);
   5   if (!grid || typeof grid !== 'object' || typeof grid.bpm !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(grid.bpm) || grid.bpm < 20 || grid.bpm > 300
   6     || !Number.isSafeInteger(grid.firstBeatMs) || grid.firstBeatMs < 0 || grid.firstBeatMs >= durationMs
   7     || ![0, 4, 8].includes(grid.countInBeats)) throw new Error('拍點無效：BPM 須為 20–300，第一拍須在原片內，倒數選秒數、4 或 8 拍。');
   8   // 白名單不帶裝置資訊；可選欄位讓沒有拍點的 v1 備份完全沿用原秒數。
   9   return { bpm: grid.bpm, firstBeatMs: grid.firstBeatMs, countInBeats: grid.countInBeats };
  10 }
  11 /** @param {import('./types').BeatGrid | undefined} grid @param {number} sourceMs */
  12 export function beatPosition(grid, sourceMs) {
  13   if (!grid || !Number.isFinite(sourceMs) || sourceMs < grid.firstBeatMs) return null;
  14   // 只用原片時間；變速、暫停及 seek 不可累加牆鐘，極小容差只處理浮點邊界。
  15   const index = Math.floor((sourceMs - grid.firstBeatMs) * grid.bpm / 60_000 + 1e-9);
  16   return { eight: Math.floor(index / 8) + 1, beat: index % 8 + 1 };
  17 }
  18 /** @param {import('./types').BeatGrid} grid @param {number} rate */
  19 export function countInPlan(grid, rate) {
  20   if (![0.5, 0.75, 1].includes(rate) || ![4, 8].includes(grid.countInBeats)) throw new Error('請先保存拍數倒數及有效錄影速度。');
  21   const intervalMs = 60_000 / grid.bpm / rate;
  22   return { beats: grid.countInBeats, intervalMs, durationMs: grid.countInBeats * intervalMs };
  23 }
  24 /** @param {BaseAudioContext} audio @param {ReturnType<typeof countInPlan>} plan @param {number} startSeconds */
  25 export function scheduleCountIn(audio, plan, startSeconds) {
  26   const nodes = [];
  27   for (let i = 0; i < plan.beats; i++) {
  28     const oscillator = audio.createOscillator(), gain = audio.createGain(), at = startSeconds + i * plan.intervalMs / 1000;
  29     oscillator.frequency.value = i === 0 ? 880 : 660;
  30     gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0, at); gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.12, at + 0.005); gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0, at + 0.06);
  31     // 提示音只往監聽端，不接影片輸出音軌；倒數完成後才啟動錄影與原片。
  32     oscillator.connect(gain).connect(audio.destination); oscillator.start(at); oscillator.stop(at + 0.065);
  33     nodes.push({ oscillator, gain });
  34   }
  35   return () => { for (const { oscillator, gain } of nodes) { try { oscillator.stop(); } catch { /* 已結束仍可清理。 */ } oscillator.disconnect(); gain.disconnect(); } };
  36 }

web/follow-export.js — 100% lines
   1 /* 檔案用途：用既有編碼工具製作帶原片聲音的單側跟隨影片。所在層：本機媒體流程。主要關聯：follow.js、recorder.js、local-audio.js、follow-ui.js。 */
   2 import { load, seek, presentedFrame, record, mediaAction } from './recorder.js';
   3 import { safariAudio, decodeLocalAudio } from './local-audio.js';
   4 import { drawFollow, followSize } from './follow.js';
   5 
   6 /** @param {Blob} file @param {import('./follow').Track} track @param {boolean} mirror @param {AbortSignal} parentSignal @param {(fraction:number)=>void} progress */
   7 export async function exportFollow(file, track, mirror, parentSignal, progress) {
   8   const controller = new AbortController(), signal = controller.signal, cancel = () => controller.abort(parentSignal.reason);
   9   parentSignal.addEventListener('abort', cancel, { once: true }); if (parentSignal.aborted) cancel();
  10   const video = document.createElement('video'), canvas = document.createElement('canvas'), bounded = safariAudio();
  11   video.playsInline = true; video.muted = bounded;
  12   let audio, source, destination, bufferSource, stream, recording, audioStart = 0;
  13   const url = URL.createObjectURL(file), hidden = () => { if (document.hidden) controller.abort(new Error('離開前景，已停止製作跟隨結果；上次結果保留。')); };
  14   document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', hidden);
  15   try {
  16     signal.throwIfAborted();
  17     const durationMs = (track.end - track.start) * 1000;
  18     if (!Number.isFinite(durationMs) || durationMs <= 0 || durationMs > 60_001) throw new Error('單側跟隨結果每次最多 60 秒，請縮短片段後重新框選。');
  19     await load(video, url, signal);
  20     Object.assign(canvas, followSize(video, track));
  21     const context = canvas.getContext('2d', { alpha: false }); if (!context) throw new Error('無法建立跟隨結果畫面。');
  22     audio = new AudioContext();
  23     // resume 可能一直等待原生允許；沿既有事件期限與取消收尾，不讓停止鍵被未完成承諾卡住。
  24     if (audio.state !== 'running') await mediaAction(audio, 'statechange', () => { audio.resume().catch(error => controller.abort(error)); }, signal);
  25     if (audio.state !== 'running') throw new Error('音訊尚未啟動，請再按一次重試。');
  26     destination = audio.createMediaStreamDestination();
  27     if (bounded) {
  28       const buffer = await decodeLocalAudio(file, audio, track.start, durationMs / 1000, signal);
  29       if (buffer) { bufferSource = audio.createBufferSource(); bufferSource.buffer = buffer; bufferSource.connect(destination); }
  30     } else {
  31       // 每次只把這個臨時播放器接到輸出聲路，不碰原片播放器一生只能建立一次的 AudioNode。
  32       source = audio.createMediaElementSource(video); source.connect(destination);
  33     }
  34     await seek(video, track.start, signal);
  35     await Promise.all([presentedFrame(video, signal, frame => !bounded || frame.mediaTime > track.start + 0.001), video.play()]);
  36     video.pause(); await seek(video, track.start, signal, bounded); signal.throwIfAborted();
  37     drawFollow(context, video, track, mirror);
  38     stream = canvas.captureStream(0);
  39     let canvasTrack = /** @type {CanvasCaptureMediaStreamTrack} */ (stream.getVideoTracks()[0]);
  40     if (typeof canvasTrack.requestFrame !== 'function') { stream.getTracks().forEach(t => { t.stop(); }); stream = canvas.captureStream(30); canvasTrack = /** @type {CanvasCaptureMediaStreamTrack} */ (stream.getVideoTracks()[0]); }
  41     if (!bounded || bufferSource) destination.stream.getAudioTracks().forEach(t => { stream.addTrack(t.clone()); });
  42     recording = record(stream, undefined, undefined, { webmOnly: bounded });
  43     recording.done.catch(error => controller.abort(error));
  44     audioStart = audio.currentTime + 0.01; bufferSource?.start(audioStart);
  45     await mediaAction(video, 'playing', () => video.play(), signal);
  46     const began = performance.now();
  47     await /** @type {Promise<void>} */ (new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
  48       let frame, videoFrame, finished = false, lastCapture = -Infinity, lastTime = -1, lastProgress = began;
  49       const finish = error => {
  50         if (finished) return; finished = true; cancelAnimationFrame(frame); if (videoFrame !== undefined) video.cancelVideoFrameCallback(videoFrame);
  51         signal.removeEventListener('abort', aborted); error ? reject(error) : resolve();
  52       };
  53       const aborted = () => finish(signal.reason);
  54       const paint = now => { if (now - lastCapture >= 1000 / 30 - 1) { drawFollow(context, video, track, mirror); canvasTrack.requestFrame?.(); lastCapture = now; } };
  55       const watch = () => {
  56         if (!video.requestVideoFrameCallback || finished) return;
  57         videoFrame = video.requestVideoFrameCallback(() => { if (finished) return; try { paint(performance.now()); watch(); } catch (error) { finish(error); } });
  58       };
  59       const tick = () => {
  60         if (signal.aborted) return aborted();
  61         const elapsed = (video.currentTime - track.start) * 1000, now = performance.now();
  62         // 沿用既有 100ms 聲畫護欄；原生 PCM 時鐘停住或領先時不交付錯位影片。
  63         if (bufferSource && (audio.state !== 'running' || Math.abs((audio.currentTime - audioStart) * 1000 - elapsed) > 100)) return finish(new Error('音訊與畫面偏移超過 100ms，未完成跟隨影片。'));
  64         if (elapsed >= durationMs - 20 || video.ended && elapsed >= durationMs - 100) return finish();
  65         if (video.paused || video.ended) return finish(new Error('原片播放提前中斷，跟隨結果未完成。'));
  66         if (elapsed > lastTime + 1) { lastTime = elapsed; lastProgress = now; }
  67         if (now - lastProgress > 1000 || now - began > durationMs + 10_000) return finish(new Error('原片播放停滯，跟隨結果未完成。'));
  68         try { paint(now); progress(Math.max(0, Math.min(1, elapsed / durationMs))); } catch (error) { return finish(error); }
  69         frame = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  70       };
  71       signal.addEventListener('abort', aborted, { once: true }); if (signal.aborted) aborted(); else { watch(); tick(); }
  72     }));
  73     video.pause(); if (bufferSource) { bufferSource.stop(); bufferSource.disconnect(); bufferSource.buffer = null; }
  74     const blob = await recording.stop(); signal.throwIfAborted();
  75     const validation = document.createElement('video'), resultUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob); validation.muted = true; validation.playsInline = true;
  76     try {
  77       await load(validation, resultUrl, signal);
  78       if (Math.abs(validation.duration * 1000 - durationMs) > 100 || validation.videoWidth !== canvas.width || validation.videoHeight !== canvas.height) throw new Error('跟隨影片的片長或尺寸不符，上次結果保留。');
  79     } finally { validation.removeAttribute('src'); validation.load(); URL.revokeObjectURL(resultUrl); }
  80     signal.throwIfAborted(); progress(1); return blob;
  81   } finally {
  82     parentSignal.removeEventListener('abort', cancel); document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', hidden);
  83     video.pause(); if (bufferSource) { try { bufferSource.stop(); } catch { /* 尚未排程或已停止仍須斷線。 */ } bufferSource.disconnect(); bufferSource.buffer = null; }
  84     if (recording) await recording.stop().catch(() => {});
  85     stream?.getTracks().forEach(t => { t.stop(); }); source?.disconnect(); destination?.stream.getTracks().forEach(t => { t.stop(); }); destination?.disconnect();
  86     if (audio && audio.state !== 'closed') await audio.close().catch(() => {});
  87     video.removeAttribute('src'); video.load(); URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  88   }
  89 }

web/follow-metrics.js — 100% lines
   1 /* 檔案用途：彙總本頁人物分析耗時，不保存來源或影格。所在層：本機診斷。主要關聯：follow-ui.js、person-worker.js。 */
   2 const rounded = value => Math.round(value * 1000) / 1000;
   3 /** @param {number[]} values */
   4 export function distribution(values) {
   5   const sorted = values.filter(value => Number.isFinite(value) && value >= 0).sort((a, b) => a - b), count = sorted.length;
   6   if (!count) return { count: 0, totalMs: 0, meanMs: null, p50Ms: null, p95Ms: null };
   7   const total = sorted.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0);
   8   // 使用 nearest-rank；只在結束後排序，不讓逐格量測本身增加排序工作。
   9   return { count, totalMs: rounded(total), meanMs: rounded(total / count), p50Ms: rounded(sorted[Math.ceil(count * .5) - 1]), p95Ms: rounded(sorted[Math.ceil(count * .95) - 1]) };
  10 }
  11 /** @param {{now:()=>number,timeOrigin:number}} [clock] */
  12 export function followMeasurements(clock = performance) {
  13   /** @type {any[]} */
  14   const reports = [], previous = [];
  15   let sequence = 0;
  16   return { reports,
  17     /** @param {{side:string,source:string,start:number,end:number,box:import('./follow').Box,sampleHz:number,plannedFrames:number,preparation:{loadMs:number,firstSeekMs:number}}} config */
  18     start(config) {
  19       const started = clock.now();
  20       /** @type {Record<'seek'|'bitmap'|'roundTrip'|'detect',number[]>} */
  21       const samples = { seek: [], bitmap: [], roundTrip: [], detect: [] };
  22       let finished = false;
  23       const entries = new Set();
  24       const probe = {
  25         samples, processedFrames: 0, workerCreatedNow: 0, workerCreatedAt: 0,
  26         workerAttempts: 0, compatibilityFallbacks: 0,
  27         /** @type {'simd'|'nosimd'|null} */ runtime: null,
  28         /** @type {number|null} */ analysisCompletedMs: null,
  29         /** @type {number|null} */ resultMs: null,
  30         /** @type {number|null} */ workerConstructorMs: null,
  31         /** @type {number|null} */ workerStartupMs: null,
  32         /** @type {number|null} */ scriptImportMs: null,
  33         /** @type {number|null} */ modelInitMs: null,
  34         endAnalysis() {
  35           // 製片約需片段長度；先停分析碼表，才能分清辨識速度與整體等待。
  36           if (probe.analysisCompletedMs === null) probe.analysisCompletedMs = Math.max(0, clock.now() - started);
  37         },
  38         recordWorker(timing) {
  39           if (!timing) return;
  40           // 回退包含兩次冷啟動；只累加回覆真正帶來的初始化數字，不把失敗成本抹掉。
  41           for (const key of ['scriptImportMs', 'modelInitMs']) if (Number.isFinite(timing[key]) && timing[key] >= 0) probe[key] = rounded((probe[key] ?? 0) + timing[key]);
  42           if (timing.runtime === 'simd' || timing.runtime === 'nosimd') probe.runtime = timing.runtime;
  43           if (Number.isFinite(timing.detectMs) && timing.detectMs >= 0) samples.detect.push(timing.detectMs);
  44           const startup = timing.enteredAt - probe.workerCreatedAt;
  45           const entry = `${probe.workerAttempts}:${timing.enteredAt}`;
  46           // Window／Worker 的 timeOrigin 要轉到同一時基；睡眠或精度差造成不一致時留空，不捏造零成本。
  47           if (!entries.has(entry) && Number.isFinite(startup) && startup >= 0 && startup <= clock.now() - probe.workerCreatedNow + 1) { entries.add(entry); probe.workerStartupMs = rounded((probe.workerStartupMs ?? 0) + startup); }
  48         },
  49         /** @param {'complete'|'cancelled'|'failed'} outcome @param {number} trackedFrames */
  50         finish(outcome, trackedFrames) {
  51           if (finished) return null; finished = true;
  52           const totalFlowMs = Math.max(0, clock.now() - started), totalMs = probe.analysisCompletedMs ?? totalFlowMs, clipDurationMs = (config.end - config.start) * 1000;
  53           const stages = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(samples).map(([key, values]) => [key, distribution(values)]));
  54           // 比對鍵只活在有界記憶體，不進報告；不能為了辨識同一片而雜湊整支私人影片。
  55           const key = JSON.stringify([config.side, config.source, config.start, config.end, config.box, config.sampleHz, probe.runtime]);
  56           const last = outcome === 'complete' ? previous.findLast(item => item.key === key) : undefined;
  57           const report = {
  58             schemaVersion: 2, sequence: ++sequence, side: config.side, outcome, sampleHz: config.sampleHz,
  59             clipDurationMs: rounded(clipDurationMs), plannedFrames: config.plannedFrames,
  60             processedFrames: probe.processedFrames, trackedFrames, totalAnalysisMs: rounded(totalMs),
  61             totalFlowMs: rounded(totalFlowMs), resultMs: probe.resultMs === null ? null : rounded(probe.resultMs),
  62             framesPerSecond: totalMs ? rounded(probe.processedFrames / totalMs * 1000) : null,
  63             realtimeFactor: totalMs ? rounded(clipDurationMs / totalMs) : null,
  64             preparation: { loadMs: rounded(config.preparation.loadMs), firstSeekMs: rounded(config.preparation.firstSeekMs) },
  65             worker: { constructionMs: probe.workerConstructorMs === null ? null : rounded(probe.workerConstructorMs), startupMs: probe.workerStartupMs, scriptImportMs: probe.scriptImportMs, modelInitMs: probe.modelInitMs, firstRoundTripMs: samples.roundTrip.length ? rounded(samples.roundTrip[0]) : null, runtime: probe.runtime, attempts: probe.workerAttempts, fallbacks: probe.compatibilityFallbacks },
  66             stages,
  67             sameInputRun: outcome === 'complete' ? (last?.report.sameInputRun || 0) + 1 : null,
  68             comparison: last ? { previousSequence: last.report.sequence, differenceMs: rounded(totalMs - last.report.totalAnalysisMs), previousTotalMs: last.report.totalAnalysisMs } : null,
  69           };
  70           reports.push(report); if (reports.length > 10) reports.shift();
  71           if (outcome === 'complete') { previous.push({ key, report }); if (previous.length > 10) previous.shift(); }
  72           return report;
  73         },
  74       };
  75       return probe;
  76     },
  77   };
  78 }

  11 export function createFollowUI(videos, options) {

  20   let metricsUrl;
  21   function showMetrics(report) {

  23     const ms = value => value === null ? '未取得' : `${value.toFixed(3)} ms`;
  24     const row = (label, stage) => `${label}：${stage.count} 次；平均 ${ms(stage.meanMs)}／P50 ${ms(stage.p50Ms)}／P95 ${ms(stage.p95Ms)}`;

  32       row('逐次定位', report.stages.seek), row('擷取影格', report.stages.bitmap), row('模型推論', report.stages.detect), row('Worker 等待（含初始化與推論）', report.stages.roundTrip),
  33       report.comparison ? `同來源／片段／選框第 ${report.sameInputRun} 次，比上次 ${ms(report.comparison.differenceMs)}。` : '尚無相同來源、片段及選框的完成輪可比較。',
  34       '各段可能包含或重疊，不能全部相加；再次分析仍建立新 Worker／模型。',
  35     ];
  36     document.getElementById('follow-metrics-report')?.replaceChildren(lines.join('\n'));

  41     if (previous) URL.revokeObjectURL(previous);
  42   }
  43   const inputs = /** @type {HTMLInputElement[]} */ ([...dialog.querySelectorAll('input[type=number]')]);
  44   const sides = /** @type {Side[]} */ (['reference', 'self']);
  45   /** @type {Record<Side,FollowState>} */
  46   const states = { reference: { track: null, enabled: false, revision: 0 }, self: { track: null, enabled: false, revision: 0 } };
  47   /** @type {{side:Side,video:HTMLVideoElement,source:string,start:number,end:number,box:import('./follow').Box,trigger:HTMLElement|null,measuring:boolean,preparation:{loadMs:number,firstSeekMs:number}}|null} */
  48   let draft = null;
  49   let analyzing = false, cancelAnalysis = () => {};
  50   /** @type {{x:number,y:number,id:number}|null} */
  51   let pointer = null;

  53   const button = side => /** @type {HTMLButtonElement} */ (document.getElementById(`${side}-follow`));

  55   const clear = side => /** @type {HTMLButtonElement} */ (document.getElementById(`${side}-follow-clear`));
  56   const note = side => /** @type {HTMLElement} */ (document.getElementById(`${side}-follow-status`));
  57   const resultVideo = side => /** @type {HTMLVideoElement} */ (document.getElementById(`${side}-follow-result`));
  58   const resultStatus = side => /** @type {HTMLElement} */ (document.getElementById(`${side}-follow-result-status`));

  61   async function makeResult(side, track, mirror, signal, showProgress) {
  62     const file = options.file(side); if (!file) throw new Error('找不到這一側的原片，請重新選片。');
  63     for (const key of sides) videos[key].pause();
  64     let last = -1;
  65     return exportFollow(file, track, mirror, signal, fraction => {
  66       const percent = Math.floor(fraction * 100); if (percent === last) return; last = percent;
  67       showProgress(`正在製作${side === 'reference' ? '老師' : '自己'}的單側跟隨影片 ${percent}%；約需片段長度，可隨時取消。`);
  68     });
  69   }
  70   function publishResult(side, blob, source, mirror) {
  71     const state = states[side], previous = state.result?.url, url = URL.createObjectURL(blob), video = resultVideo(side), link = download(side);
  72     state.result = { url, source, mirror, revision: state.revision };
  73     video.pause(); video.src = url; video.hidden = false; video.load();
  74     link.href = url; link.download = `dance-${side}-follow.${extension(blob.type)}`; link.hidden = false;
  75     // 新檔已獨立驗證才能換掉前次結果；失敗／取消不會執行到這裡。
  76     if (previous) URL.revokeObjectURL(previous);
  77     // 兩個入口都在成功交換後通知全頁，避免殘留舊錯誤，也不搶先覆蓋失敗／取消原因。
  78     options.status(`${side === 'reference' ? '老師' : '自己'}跟隨影片已完成，可播放或匯出；原片與筆記仍保留。`);
  79   }
  80   function paint() {
  81     if (!draft || !context) return;
  82     const { video, box } = draft;
  83     context.drawImage(video, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
  84     const x = box.x * canvas.width, y = box.y * canvas.height, width = box.width * canvas.width, height = box.height * canvas.height;
  85     context.fillStyle = '#0008'; context.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, y); context.fillRect(0, y + height, canvas.width, canvas.height - y - height);
  86     context.fillRect(0, y, x, height); context.fillRect(x + width, y, canvas.width - x - width, height);
  87     context.strokeStyle = '#65e8c2'; context.lineWidth = 3; context.strokeRect(x, y, width, height);
  88   }
  89   function setBox(box) {
  90     if (!draft) return;
  91     draft.box = validBox(box);
  92     for (const input of inputs) input.value = String(Math.round(draft.box[input.name] * 1000) / 10);
  93     paint();
  94   }
  95   function closeDraft() {
  96     const trigger = draft?.trigger;
  97     if (pointer && canvas.hasPointerCapture(pointer.id)) canvas.releasePointerCapture(pointer.id);
  98     pointer = null;
  99     if (draft) { draft.video.pause(); draft.video.removeAttribute('src'); draft.video.load(); }
 100     draft = null; if (dialog.open) dialog.close(); if (trigger) options.focus(trigger);
 101   }

 108     for (const side of sides) { resultVideo(side).pause(); if (!event.persisted && states[side].result) URL.revokeObjectURL(states[side].result.url); }
 109   });
 110   window.addEventListener('pagehide', event => { if (!event.persisted && metricsUrl) URL.revokeObjectURL(metricsUrl); });
 111   for (const input of inputs) input.addEventListener('change', () => {

 114     catch (error) { message.textContent = error instanceof Error ? error.message : '選框範圍不正確。'; setBox(draft.box); }
 115   });
 116   const point = event => {
 117     const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
 118     return { x: Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (event.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width)), y: Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (event.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height)) };
 119   };
 120   canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', event => {
 121     if (!draft || analyzing || event.button !== 0) return;
 122     pointer = { ...point(event), id: event.pointerId }; canvas.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId); event.preventDefault();
 123   });
 124   const move = event => {
 125     if (!pointer || pointer.id !== event.pointerId || !draft || analyzing) return;
 126     const next = point(event), width = Math.abs(next.x - pointer.x), height = Math.abs(next.y - pointer.y);
 127     if (width >= 0.02 && height >= 0.02) setBox({ x: Math.min(pointer.x, next.x), y: Math.min(pointer.y, next.y), width, height });
 128   };
 129   canvas.addEventListener('pointermove', move);
 130   canvas.addEventListener('pointerup', event => { move(event); if (pointer?.id === event.pointerId) { canvas.releasePointerCapture(event.pointerId); pointer = null; } });
 131   canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel', () => { pointer = null; });
 132   canvas.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
 133     if (!draft || analyzing || !['ArrowLeft', 'ArrowRight', 'ArrowUp', 'ArrowDown'].includes(event.key)) return;
 134     event.preventDefault(); const box = { ...draft.box }, horizontal = ['ArrowLeft', 'ArrowRight'].includes(event.key), positive = ['ArrowRight', 'ArrowDown'].includes(event.key);
 135     const key = event.shiftKey ? horizontal ? 'width' : 'height' : horizontal ? 'x' : 'y';
 136     box[key] = Math.max(event.shiftKey ? 0.02 : 0, box[key] + (positive ? 0.01 : -0.01)); setBox(box);
 137   });
 138   for (const side of sides) {
 139     button(side).addEventListener('click', () => options.operate(async signal => {
 140       closeDraft(); const source = options.source(side), [start, end] = options.range(side);
 141       if (!source) throw new Error('請先選好兩側影片。');
 142       const video = document.createElement('video'); video.muted = true; video.playsInline = true; video.preload = 'auto';
 143       const trigger = button(side);
 144       const measuring = metricsEnabled.checked;
 145       try {
 146         // 分析播放器不掛在畫面上；等解碼 seeked 即可，不能等待不存在的螢幕呈現通知。
 147         let began = measuring ? performance.now() : 0;
 148         await load(video, source, signal); const loadMs = measuring ? performance.now() - began : 0;
 149         began = measuring ? performance.now() : 0;
 150         await seek(video, start, signal); const firstSeekMs = measuring ? performance.now() - began : 0;
 151         draft = { side, video, source, start, end, box: { x: 0.2, y: 0.05, width: 0.6, height: 0.9 }, trigger, measuring, preparation: { loadMs, firstSeekMs } };
 152         canvas.width = 640; canvas.height = Math.round(640 * video.videoHeight / video.videoWidth);

 155         setBox(draft.box); dialog.showModal(); canvas.focus();
 156       } catch (error) { video.removeAttribute('src'); video.load(); throw error; }
 157     }));
 158     toggle(side).addEventListener('change', () => { states[side].enabled = toggle(side).checked; options.changed(); update(true); });
 159     clear(side).addEventListener('click', () => { states[side].track = null; states[side].enabled = false; states[side].revision++; options.changed(); update(true); });
 160     for (const type of ['play', 'pause', 'ended']) resultVideo(side).addEventListener(type, () => {
 161       if (!resultVideo(side).paused) {
 162         if (options.busy()) resultVideo(side).pause();
 163         else { for (const other of sides) { videos[other].pause(); if (other !== side) resultVideo(other).pause(); } }
 164       }

 168       const state = states[side], source = options.source(side), track = state.track, mirror = options.mirror(side);
 169       if (!source || state.source !== source || !track) throw new Error('請先框選這一側並完成分析。');
 170       const blob = await makeResult(side, track, mirror, signal, text => { resultStatus(side).textContent = text; });
 171       signal.throwIfAborted(); if (options.source(side) !== source) throw new Error('來源已變更，原結果保留。');
 172       publishResult(side, blob, source, mirror); options.focus(resultVideo(side));
 173     }));
 174   }
 175   confirm.addEventListener('click', () => options.operate(async parentSignal => {
 176     if (!draft || analyzing) return;
 177     const current = draft, controller = new AbortController(), signal = controller.signal;
 178     const sampleHz = 8, count = Math.ceil((current.end - current.start) * sampleHz);
 179     const probe = current.measuring ? measurements.start({ side: current.side, source: current.source, start: current.start, end: current.end, box: current.box, sampleHz, plannedFrames: count + 1, preparation: current.preparation }) : null;
 180     /** @type {'complete'|'cancelled'|'failed'} */
 181     let outcome = 'failed';
 182     let trackedFrames = 0;
 183     const aborted = () => controller.abort(parentSignal.reason);
 184     parentSignal.addEventListener('abort', aborted, { once: true }); if (parentSignal.aborted) aborted();
 185     analyzing = true; cancelAnalysis = () => controller.abort(); controls.disabled = true; confirm.disabled = true;
 186     let worker, compatibility = false;
 187     const createWorker = () => {
 188       const began = probe ? performance.now() : 0;
 189       if (probe) { probe.workerCreatedNow = began; probe.workerCreatedAt = performance.timeOrigin + began; }
 190       worker = new Worker('/person-worker.js');
 191       if (probe) { probe.workerConstructorMs = (probe.workerConstructorMs ?? 0) + performance.now() - began; probe.workerAttempts++; }
 192     };
 193     try {
 194       createWorker();
 195       const detect = async search => {
 196         signal.throwIfAborted();
 197         // 只看選框／目標附近，避免團體寬畫面把人物縮得太小而漏偵測；座標再映回完整原片。
 198         const width = search.width * current.video.videoWidth, height = search.height * current.video.videoHeight;
 199         const began = probe ? performance.now() : 0;
 200         const bitmap = await createImageBitmap(current.video, search.x * current.video.videoWidth, search.y * current.video.videoHeight, width, height, { resizeWidth: Math.round(640 * width / Math.max(width, height)), resizeHeight: Math.round(640 * height / Math.max(width, height)) });
 201         probe?.samples.bitmap.push(performance.now() - began);
 202         if (signal.aborted) { bitmap.close(); signal.throwIfAborted(); }
 203         return /** @type {Promise<import('./follow').Box[]>} */ (new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
 204           const requested = probe ? performance.now() : 0;
 205           let settled = false;
 206           const finish = (error, boxes) => { if (settled) return; settled = true; probe?.samples.roundTrip.push(performance.now() - requested); clearTimeout(timer); signal.removeEventListener('abort', abort); if (worker) { worker.onmessage = null; worker.onerror = null; } error ? reject(error) : resolve(boxes); };
 207           const abort = () => finish(signal.reason, null);
 208           const timer = setTimeout(() => finish(new Error('人物分析逾時，請縮短片段後重試。'), null), 30_000);
 209           signal.addEventListener('abort', abort, { once: true });
 210           const reply = ({ data }) => {
 211             if (data.retryCompatibility) {
 212               if (settled || signal.aborted || compatibility || !data.bitmap) { data.bitmap?.close(); finish(signal.aborted ? signal.reason : new Error('人物相容初始化失敗，原結果保留。'), null); return; }
 213               probe?.recordWorker(data.timing); if (probe) { probe.compatibilityFallbacks++; probe.runtime = null; }
 214               // 停掉半初始化 runtime 後才建新 Worker；沿用原偵測期限與同一張影格，不重取樣。
 215               worker.onmessage = null; worker.onerror = null; worker.terminate(); worker = undefined; compatibility = true;
 216               try { createWorker(); worker.onmessage = reply; worker.onerror = failed; worker.postMessage({ bitmap: data.bitmap, measure: !!probe, compatibility }, [data.bitmap]); }
 217               catch (error) { data.bitmap.close(); finish(error, null); }
 218               return;
 219             }
 220             probe?.recordWorker(data.timing); if (probe && !data.error) probe.processedFrames++;
 221             finish(data.error ? new Error(data.error) : null, data.boxes?.map(box => ({ x: search.x + box.x * search.width, y: search.y + box.y * search.height, width: box.width * search.width, height: box.height * search.height })));
 222           };
 223           const failed = () => finish(new Error('無法啟動本機人物分析，請確認應用資源完整。'), null);
 224           worker.onmessage = reply; worker.onerror = failed;
 225           try { worker.postMessage({ bitmap, measure: !!probe, compatibility }, [bitmap]); }
 226           catch (error) { bitmap.close(); finish(error, null); }
 227         }));
 228       };
 229       let first, previous, lastSeen = current.start, held = 0;
 230       /** @type {import('./follow').Sample[]} */
 231       const samples = [];
 232       for (let index = 0; index <= count; index++) {
 233         const time = Math.min(current.end, current.start + index / sampleHz);
 234         const began = probe ? performance.now() : 0;
 235         await seek(current.video, Math.min(time, current.video.duration - 0.02), signal);
 236         probe?.samples.seek.push(performance.now() - began);
 237         const search = previous ? validBox({ x: previous.x - previous.width * 0.75, y: previous.y - previous.height * 0.2, width: previous.width * 2.5, height: previous.height * 1.4 }) : current.box;
 238         const person = matchPerson(await detect(search), previous || current.box);
 239         if (!person && !first) throw new Error('選框中未找到明確人物，請只框住一個人的完整身體後重試。');
 240         if (person) { first ||= person; previous = person; lastSeen = time; }
 241         else { held++; if (time - lastSeen > 0.75) throw new Error(`在 ${time.toFixed(2)} 秒無法確定同一個人，請縮短片段或重新框選。`); }
 242         samples.push({ time, ...followBox(current.box, first, previous) });
 243         trackedFrames++;
 244         message.textContent = `只在本機分析 ${Math.round(index / count * 100)}%（${time.toFixed(2)} 秒）；可隨時取消。`;
 245       }
 246       signal.throwIfAborted();
 247       if (options.source(current.side) !== current.source) throw new Error('來源已更換，請重新框選。');
 248       const track = { start: current.start, end: current.end, samples: smoothTrack(samples), held }, mirror = options.mirror(current.side);
 249       // 推論已結束就釋放模型，製作影片不需同時占用另一份 WASM 記憶體。
 250       worker.terminate(); worker = undefined;
 251       probe?.endAnalysis();
 252       const resultBegan = probe ? performance.now() : 0;
 253       let blob;
 254       // 製片失敗或取消也保留部分耗時，但仍由原成功交換點保全上次結果。
 255       try { blob = await makeResult(current.side, track, mirror, signal, text => { message.textContent = text; }); }
 256       finally { if (probe) probe.resultMs = performance.now() - resultBegan; }
 257       signal.throwIfAborted(); if (options.source(current.side) !== current.source) throw new Error('來源已變更，請重新框選。');
 258       // 路徑與可播放影片完整成功後一起交換；取消或製作失敗保留上次整組結果。
 259       const state = states[current.side]; state.source = current.source; state.track = track; state.enabled = true; state.revision++;
 260       publishResult(current.side, blob, current.source, mirror);
 261       closeDraft(); options.changed(); options.focus(resultVideo(current.side));
 262       outcome = 'complete';
 263     } catch (caught) {
 264       if (signal.aborted) outcome = 'cancelled';
 265       const error = caught instanceof Error ? caught : new Error(String(caught));
 266       message.textContent = error.name === 'AbortError' ? '已取消分析，原片與上次成功的追蹤保留；可以調整選框再試。' : `${error.message} 上次成功的追蹤保留。`;
 267       throw error;
 268     } finally {
 269       worker?.terminate(); parentSignal.removeEventListener('abort', aborted); analyzing = false; cancelAnalysis = () => {}; controls.disabled = false; confirm.disabled = false;
 270       showMetrics(probe?.finish(outcome, trackedFrames));
 271     }
 272   }));
 273   function update(available) {
 274     for (const side of sides) {
 275       const state = states[side], source = options.source(side);
 276       if (state.source !== source) { state.source = source; state.track = null; state.enabled = false; state.revision++; }
 277       const track = state.track;
 278       const result = state.result, current = result && track && result.source === source && result.revision === state.revision && result.mirror === options.mirror(side);
 279       const busy = options.busy(); refresh(side).disabled = !track || state.source !== source || busy;
 280       refresh(side).classList.toggle('primary', !!track && !!result && !current);
 281       download(side).textContent = `匯出${result && !current ? '上次' : ''}${side === 'reference' ? '老師' : '自己'}跟隨影片`;
 282       if (busy) resultVideo(side).pause(); resultVideo(side).controls = !busy;
 283       resultStatus(side).textContent = !result ? '框選完成後，這裡會顯示可獨立播放與匯出的跟隨影片。' : current ? '這是目前選框與鏡像的單側結果，以原速播放並保留該側原片聲音。請匯出另存；結果只留本頁。' : '這是先前影片或設定的結果，原檔保留。請重新框選，或按「更新跟隨結果」套用目前鏡像，再匯出。';
 284       button(side).disabled = !available || options.busy(); toggle(side).disabled = !track || options.busy(); clear(side).disabled = !track || options.busy(); toggle(side).checked = state.enabled;
 285       note(side).textContent = track ? `${state.enabled ? '已開啟跟隨' : '跟隨已暫停'} · ${track.start.toFixed(2)}–${track.end.toFixed(2)} 秒${track.held ? `；${track.held} 個分析點暫用前次位置，請預覽檢查。` : '；請預覽確認全段都跟對人。'} 設定只留本頁，匯出影片會保留裁切。` : '尚未框選，使用完整畫面。';
 286     }
 287   }
 288   return {
 289     update,
 290     playing() { return sides.some(side => !resultVideo(side).paused); },
 291     pauseResults() { for (const side of sides) resultVideo(side).pause(); },
 292     /** @param {Side} side */
 293     track(side) { const state = states[side]; return state.enabled && state.source === options.source(side) && videos[side].getAttribute('src') === state.source ? state.track : null; },
 294     signature() { return sides.map(side => [states[side].enabled, states[side].revision]); },
 295     assertCoverage() {
 296       for (const side of sides) {
 297         const state = states[side]; if (!state.enabled || !state.track || state.source !== options.source(side)) continue;
 298         const [start, end] = options.range(side);
 299         if (start < state.track.start - 0.001 || end > state.track.end + 0.001) throw new Error(`${label(side)}的比較區間超出已分析範圍，請重新框選分析，或關閉該側跟隨。`);
 300       }
 301     },
 302   };
 303 }

web/follow.js — 100% lines
   1 /* 檔案用途：人物選框、身分連續性與可重播裁切路徑。所在層：純畫面邏輯。主要關聯：follow-ui.js、main.js、person-worker.js。 */
   2 import { contain } from './timeline.js';
   3 const clamp = (value, min, max) => Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value));
   4 /** @typedef {{x:number,y:number,width:number,height:number}} Box */
   5 /** @typedef {Box & {time:number}} Sample */
   6 /** @typedef {{start:number,end:number,samples:Sample[],held:number}} Track */
   7 
   8 /** @param {Box} box @returns {Box} */
   9 export function validBox(box) {
  10   if (!Object.values(box).every(Number.isFinite) || box.width < 0.02 || box.height < 0.02) throw new Error('請框住完整身體，選框不能太小。');
  11   const width = clamp(box.width, 0.02, 1), height = clamp(box.height, 0.02, 1);
  12   return { x: clamp(box.x, 0, 1 - width), y: clamp(box.y, 0, 1 - height), width, height };
  13 }
  14 /** @param {Box} a @param {Box} b */
  15 export function overlap(a, b) {
  16   const intersection = Math.max(0, Math.min(a.x + a.width, b.x + b.width) - Math.max(a.x, b.x)) * Math.max(0, Math.min(a.y + a.height, b.y + b.height) - Math.max(a.y, b.y));
  17   return intersection / (a.width * a.height + b.width * b.height - intersection);
  18 }
  19 /** @param {Box[]} people @param {Box} previous */
  20 export function matchPerson(people, previous) {
  21   const ranked = people.map(box => {
  22     const distance = Math.hypot(box.x + box.width / 2 - previous.x - previous.width / 2, box.y + box.height / 2 - previous.y - previous.height / 2);
  23     const ratio = box.height / previous.height;
  24     return { box, score: overlap(box, previous) - distance, distance, ratio };
  25   }).filter(item => item.distance < Math.max(0.12, previous.height * 0.45) && item.ratio > 0.5 && item.ratio < 2).sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);
  26   // 多人交錯時寧可要求重選；單純挑最高分會把鏡中影像或旁人當成原目標。
  27   if (!ranked.length || ranked[0].score < 0.05 || ranked[1] && ranked[0].score - ranked[1].score < 0.08) return null;
  28   return ranked[0].box;
  29 }
  30 /** @param {Box} selection @param {Box} first @param {Box} person */
  31 export function followBox(selection, first, person) {
  32   const scale = clamp(person.height / first.height, 0.5, 2);
  33   const width = Math.min(1, selection.width * scale), height = Math.min(1, selection.height * scale);
  34   return validBox({ x: person.x + person.width / 2 + (selection.x + selection.width / 2 - first.x - first.width / 2) * scale - width / 2,
  35     y: person.y + person.height / 2 + (selection.y + selection.height / 2 - first.y - first.height / 2) * scale - height / 2, width, height });
  36 }
  37 /** @param {Track} track @param {number} time @returns {Box} */
  38 export function cropAt(track, time) {
  39   const samples = track.samples;
  40   let lo = 0, hi = samples.length - 1;
  41   while (lo < hi) { const mid = Math.floor((lo + hi) / 2); if (samples[mid].time < time) lo = mid + 1; else hi = mid; }
  42   const a = samples[Math.max(0, lo - 1)], b = samples[lo], fraction = clamp((time - a.time) / (b.time - a.time || 1), 0, 1);
  43   return validBox({ x: a.x + (b.x - a.x) * fraction, y: a.y + (b.y - a.y) * fraction, width: a.width + (b.width - a.width) * fraction, height: a.height + (b.height - a.height) * fraction });
  44 }
  45 /** @param {Sample[]} samples */
  46 export function smoothTrack(samples) {
  47   // 離線先算路徑，使用前後相同權重平滑，避免只用上一格造成追蹤持續落後。
  48   return samples.map((sample, index) => {
  49     const nearby = samples.slice(Math.max(0, index - 1), index + 2);
  50     const mean = key => nearby.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item[key], 0) / nearby.length;
  51     return { time: sample.time, ...validBox({ x: mean('x'), y: mean('y'), width: mean('width'), height: mean('height') }) };
  52   });
  53 }
  54 /** @param {HTMLVideoElement} video @param {Track | null} track */
  55 export function sourceCrop(video, track) {
  56   const box = track ? cropAt(track, video.currentTime) : { x: 0, y: 0, width: 1, height: 1 };
  57   return { x: box.x * video.videoWidth, y: box.y * video.videoHeight, width: box.width * video.videoWidth, height: box.height * video.videoHeight };
  58 }
  59 /** @param {HTMLVideoElement} video @param {Track} track */
  60 export function followSize(video, track) {
  61   const first = track.samples[0], width = first.width * video.videoWidth, height = first.height * video.videoHeight;
  62   // 保留選框比例與原始細節，長邊最多 1280；偶數尺寸避免原生 H.264 編碼拒絕奇數邊長。
  63   const scale = Math.min(1, 1280 / Math.max(width, height));
  64   return { width: Math.max(2, Math.round(width * scale / 2) * 2), height: Math.max(2, Math.round(height * scale / 2) * 2) };
  65 }
  66 /** @param {CanvasRenderingContext2D} context @param {HTMLVideoElement} video @param {Track} track @param {boolean} mirror */
  67 export function drawFollow(context, video, track, mirror) {
  68   const { width, height } = context.canvas, crop = sourceCrop(video, track), rect = contain(crop.width, crop.height, width, height);
  69   context.fillStyle = '#080c0e'; context.fillRect(0, 0, width, height); context.save();
  70   try {
  71     // 鏡像在人物裁切之後，選中的人不會因左右翻轉而換成另一個位置。
  72     context.translate(mirror ? width : 0, 0); context.scale(mirror ? -1 : 1, 1);
  73     context.drawImage(video, crop.x, crop.y, crop.width, crop.height, rect.x, rect.y, rect.width, rect.height);
  74   } finally { context.restore(); }
  75 }

   1 /* 檔案用途：本機筆記與可攜格式的信任邊界。所在層：純資料邏輯。主要關聯：DATA_FORMAT.md、storage.js、main.js。 */
   2 import { validateMarkerRange, MAX_COMPARISON_MS } from './timeline.js';
   3 import { validateBeatGrid } from './beats.js';
   4 export const tables = /** @type {const} */ (['videos', 'assets', 'markers', 'takes', 'recipes', 'exports', 'moves']);
   5 /** @returns {import('./types').Library} */
   6 export function newLibrary() {
   7   return { format: 'dance-stamp', schemaVersion: 2, kind: 'backup', exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(), sourceLibraryId: crypto.randomUUID(), videos: [], assets: [], markers: [], takes: [], recipes: [], exports: [], moves: [] };
   8 }
   9 function check(ok, message = '備份格式不正確，沒有匯入任何資料。') { if (!ok) throw new Error(message); }
  10 const id = value => typeof value === 'string' && /^[\da-f]{8}-[\da-f]{4}-[\da-f]{4}-[\da-f]{4}-[\da-f]{12}$/i.test(value);
  11 const integer = value => Number.isSafeInteger(value) && value >= 0;
  12 const text = (value, limit = 500) => typeof value === 'string' && value.length <= limit;
  13 export const moveStatuses = { wanted: '想練', practicing: '練習中', learned: '會了' };
  14 const courseDate = value => typeof value === 'string' && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value) && new Date(value).toISOString().slice(0, 10) === value;
  15 function rejectDangerous(value) {
  16   if (!value || typeof value !== 'object') return;
  17   for (const key of Object.keys(value)) {
  18     check(!['__proto__', 'constructor', 'prototype'].includes(key));
  19     rejectDangerous(value[key]);
  20   }
  21 }
  22 /** @returns {import('./types').OverlayPresentation | undefined} */
  23 function exportPresentation(value) {
  24   if (value === undefined) return undefined;
  25   // 輸出外觀是資料白名單；拒絕無限值與越界位移，不將外部物件直接存入。
  26   check(value && value.layout === 'overlay' && Number.isFinite(value.opacity) && value.opacity >= 0 && value.opacity <= 1);
  27   const layer = input => {
  28     check(input && Number.isFinite(input.scale) && input.scale >= 0.25 && input.scale <= 4);
  29     check(Number.isFinite(input.x) && Math.abs(input.x) <= 2 && Number.isFinite(input.y) && Math.abs(input.y) <= 2);
  30     return { scale: input.scale, x: input.x, y: input.y };
  31   };
  32   return { layout: 'overlay', opacity: value.opacity, reference: layer(value.reference), self: layer(value.self) };
  33 }
  34 /** @returns {import('./types').Library} */
  35 export function validateBackup(input) {
  36   check(input && typeof input === 'object');
  37   rejectDangerous(input);
  38   check(input.format === 'dance-stamp' && [1, 2].includes(input.schemaVersion) && ['backup', 'share'].includes(input.kind), '不支援這個備份版本或種類，沒有改動本機資料。');
  39 
  40   check(id(input.sourceLibraryId) && text(input.exportedAt) && Number.isFinite(Date.parse(input.exportedAt)));
  41   for (const table of tables) check(Array.isArray(input[table]) && input[table].length <= 10_000);
  42   // v1 從未支援動作，不能猜測舊草案欄位或把新版資料偷偷降級。
  43   if (input.schemaVersion === 1) check(input.moves.length === 0, 'v1 不支援動作庫，請保留原備份。');
  44   const maps = Object.fromEntries(tables.map(table => {
  45     const map = new Map();
  46     for (const row of input[table]) { check(row && id(row.id) && !map.has(row.id)); map.set(row.id, row); }
  47     return [table, map];
  48   }));
  49   const out = newLibrary(); out.kind = input.kind; out.sourceLibraryId = input.sourceLibraryId; out.exportedAt = input.exportedAt;
  50   for (const v of input.videos) {
  51     check(text(v.title) && integer(v.durationMs) && v.durationMs > 0 && integer(v.revision) && v.revision > 0);
  52     const classification = {};
  53     // 舊分享中的合法分類也須保留，不能升 v2 時把使用者明確加入的欄位丟掉。
  54     for (const field of ['teacher', 'danceStyle']) if (v[field] !== undefined) { check(text(v[field], 200)); classification[field] = v[field]; }
  55     if (v.recordedDate !== undefined) { check(courseDate(v.recordedDate)); classification.recordedDate = v.recordedDate; }
  56     out.videos.push({ id: v.id, title: v.title, durationMs: v.durationMs, revision: v.revision, ...classification, ...(v.beatGrid === undefined ? {} : { beatGrid: validateBeatGrid(v.beatGrid, v.durationMs) }) });
  57   }
  58   for (const move of input.moves) {
  59     check(text(move.name) && move.name.trim().length > 0 && text(move.danceStyle, 200));
  60     check(Object.hasOwn(moveStatuses, move.status) && integer(move.revision) && move.revision > 0);
  61     out.moves.push({ id: move.id, name: move.name, danceStyle: move.danceStyle, status: move.status, revision: move.revision });
  62   }
  63   for (const a of input.assets) {
  64     check(['reference', 'take', 'vs'].includes(a.kind) && integer(a.byteLength) && text(a.mimeType, 200));
  65     check(a.videoId === undefined || maps.videos.has(a.videoId));
  66     check(a.durationMs === undefined || (integer(a.durationMs) && a.durationMs > 0));
  67     check(a.originalName === undefined || text(a.originalName));
  68     out.assets.push({ id: a.id, ...(a.videoId ? { videoId: a.videoId } : {}), kind: a.kind, byteLength: a.byteLength, mimeType: a.mimeType, ...(a.durationMs ? { durationMs: a.durationMs } : {}), ...(a.originalName === undefined ? {} : { originalName: a.originalName }), mediaIncluded: false });
  69   }
  70   for (const m of input.markers) {
  71     check(maps.videos.has(m.videoId));
  72     validateMarkerRange(m.startMs, m.endMs, maps.videos.get(m.videoId).durationMs);
  73     check(text(m.title) && text(m.note, 20_000) && integer(m.revision) && m.revision > 0);
  74     if (input.schemaVersion === 2) check(m.moveId === undefined || maps.moves.has(m.moveId));
  75     out.markers.push({ id: m.id, videoId: m.videoId, startMs: m.startMs, ...(m.endMs !== undefined ? { endMs: m.endMs } : {}), title: m.title, note: m.note, revision: m.revision, ...(input.schemaVersion === 2 && m.moveId !== undefined ? { moveId: m.moveId } : {}) });
  76   }
  77   for (const t of input.takes) {
  78     check(maps.videos.has(t.referenceVideoId) && maps.assets.get(t.cameraAssetId)?.kind === 'take');
  79     check(integer(t.referenceStartMs) && integer(t.referenceEndMs) && t.referenceEndMs > t.referenceStartMs && t.referenceEndMs <= maps.videos.get(t.referenceVideoId).durationMs);
  80     // take 也代表既有雙片配對，備份必須容納合法長比較；新相機另由 captureDuration 限制。
  81     check([0.5, 0.75, 1].includes(t.rate) && (t.referenceEndMs - t.referenceStartMs) / t.rate <= MAX_COMPARISON_MS);
  82     check(['ready', 'interrupted', 'failed', 'recording'].includes(t.state) && text(t.createdAt) && Number.isFinite(Date.parse(t.createdAt)));
  83     let captureTiming;
  84     if (t.captureTiming !== undefined) {
  85       check(t.captureTiming && typeof t.captureTiming === 'object' && integer(t.captureTiming.recorderRequestedMs));
  86       const timing = t.captureTiming;
  87       for (const key of ['recorderStartedMs', 'referencePlayResolvedMs', 'referenceAtPlayMs']) check(timing[key] === undefined || integer(timing[key]));
  88       check(timing.referenceAtPlayMs === undefined || timing.referenceAtPlayMs <= maps.videos.get(t.referenceVideoId).durationMs);
  89       // 單調時間只供觀測，不推導影格同步；白名單排除裝置與原始錯誤資訊。
  90       captureTiming = { recorderRequestedMs: timing.recorderRequestedMs, ...Object.fromEntries(['recorderStartedMs', 'referencePlayResolvedMs', 'referenceAtPlayMs'].filter(key => timing[key] !== undefined).map(key => [key, timing[key]])) };
  91     }
  92     out.takes.push({ id: t.id, referenceVideoId: t.referenceVideoId, cameraAssetId: t.cameraAssetId, referenceStartMs: t.referenceStartMs, referenceEndMs: t.referenceEndMs, rate: t.rate, state: t.state === 'recording' ? 'interrupted' : t.state, createdAt: t.createdAt, ...(captureTiming ? { captureTiming } : {}), ...(t.beatGrid === undefined ? {} : { beatGrid: validateBeatGrid(t.beatGrid, maps.videos.get(t.referenceVideoId).durationMs) }) });
  93   }
  94   for (const r of input.recipes) {
  95     check(maps.takes.has(r.takeId) && Number.isSafeInteger(r.offsetMs) && typeof r.referenceMirror === 'boolean' && typeof r.selfMirror === 'boolean');
  96     check(r.layout === 'side-by-side' && r.audioSource === 'reference' && integer(r.revision) && r.revision > 0);
  97     out.recipes.push({ id: r.id, takeId: r.takeId, offsetMs: r.offsetMs, referenceMirror: r.referenceMirror, selfMirror: r.selfMirror, layout: r.layout, audioSource: r.audioSource, revision: r.revision });
  98   }
  99   for (const e of input.exports) {
 100     check(maps.recipes.has(e.recipeId) && maps.assets.get(e.assetId)?.kind === 'vs' && integer(e.recipeRevision) && e.recipeRevision > 0);
 101     check(text(e.mimeType, 200) && ['ready', 'interrupted', 'failed'].includes(e.state) && text(e.createdAt) && Number.isFinite(Date.parse(e.createdAt)));
 102     out.exports.push({ id: e.id, recipeId: e.recipeId, recipeRevision: e.recipeRevision, assetId: e.assetId, mimeType: e.mimeType, state: e.state, createdAt: e.createdAt, ...(e.presentation === undefined ? {} : { presentation: exportPresentation(e.presentation) }) });
 103   }
 104   // 白名單重新建立物件，絕不把路徑、object URL 或任意外部欄位帶進資料庫。
 105   // 容量屬於可攜內容；讀回補上的媒體狀態不能擋住合法筆記與救援。外部原檔仍由 parseBackup 先檢查 bytes。
 106   check(new Blob([JSON.stringify(out)]).size <= 10 * 1024 * 1024, '筆記資料超過 10 MiB，請先保存備份。');
 107   return out;
 108 }
 109 export function parseBackup(source) {
 110   check(new Blob([source]).size <= 10 * 1024 * 1024, '備份超過 10 MiB。');
 111   let input;
 112   try { input = JSON.parse(source); } catch { throw new Error('備份不是有效 JSON。'); }
 113   return validateBackup(input);
 114 }
 115 /** @param {import('./types').Library} data */
 116 export function migrateLibrary(data) {
 117   // 只在記憶體升版，保留本機媒體狀態；第一次成功交易才與新編輯一起提交。
 118   validateBackup(data);
 119   return { ...structuredClone(data), schemaVersion: 2 };
 120 }
 121 /** @param {import('./types').Library} data @param {{keyword?: string, danceStyle?: string, teacher?: string, from?: string, to?: string, status?: string}} filters */
 122 export function searchLibrary(data, filters = {}) {
 123   // ponytail: 有上限的本機資料直接掃描，避免維護第二份離線索引與 migration。
 124   const fold = value => (value || '').normalize('NFKC').toLocaleLowerCase();
 125   const tokens = fold(filters.keyword).trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
 126   const matches = values => tokens.every(token => fold(values.join(' ')).includes(token));
 127   const includes = (value, query) => fold(value).includes(fold(query));
 128   const videos = new Map(data.videos.map(v => [v.id, v]));
 129   const moves = new Map(data.moves.map(m => [m.id, m]));
 130   const markersByVideo = new Map(), markersByMove = new Map();
 131   for (const marker of data.markers) {
 132     for (const [map, key] of /** @type {const} */ ([[markersByVideo, marker.videoId], [markersByMove, marker.moveId]])) {
 133       const group = map.get(key) || []; group.push(marker); map.set(key, group);
 134     }
 135   }
 136   const videoScope = v => includes(v.teacher, filters.teacher) && (!filters.from || (v.recordedDate && v.recordedDate >= filters.from)) && (!filters.to || (v.recordedDate && v.recordedDate <= filters.to));
 137   const courseText = v => [v.title, v.teacher, v.danceStyle, v.recordedDate];
 138   const markerMatches = (marker, move) => {
 139     const v = videos.get(marker.videoId);
 140     return v && videoScope(v) && (includes(v.danceStyle, filters.danceStyle) || includes(move?.danceStyle, filters.danceStyle)) && matches([...courseText(v), marker.title, marker.note, move?.name, move?.danceStyle]);
 141   };
 142   const moveResults = data.moves.flatMap(move => {
 143     if (filters.status && move.status !== filters.status) return [];
 144     const markers = (markersByMove.get(move.id) || []).filter(m => markerMatches(m, move));
 145     const standalone = !filters.teacher && !filters.from && !filters.to && includes(move.danceStyle, filters.danceStyle) && matches([move.name, move.danceStyle]);
 146     return markers.length || standalone ? [{ move, markers }] : [];
 147   });
 148   const videoResults = data.videos.filter(v => {
 149     if (!videoScope(v)) return false;
 150     const markers = markersByVideo.get(v.id) || [];
 151     return (!filters.status && includes(v.danceStyle, filters.danceStyle) && matches(courseText(v))) || markers.some(m => (!filters.status || moves.get(m.moveId)?.status === filters.status) && markerMatches(m, moves.get(m.moveId)));
 152   });
 153   return { videos: videoResults, moves: moveResults };
 154 }
 155 /** @param {import('./types').Library} data @param {import('./types').Marker[]} markers */
 156 export function practiceHistory(data, markers) {
 157   // 歷史來自既有 take 的老師區間；不新增易失聯的 take→marker 外鍵，也不改寫錄影配方。
 158   const byVideo = new Map();
 159   for (const marker of markers) { const group = byVideo.get(marker.videoId) || []; group.push(marker); byVideo.set(marker.videoId, group); }
 160   return data.takes.filter(take => (byVideo.get(take.referenceVideoId) || []).some(m => m.endMs === undefined ? m.startMs >= take.referenceStartMs && m.startMs < take.referenceEndMs : m.startMs < take.referenceEndMs && m.endMs > take.referenceStartMs)).sort((a, b) => Date.parse(b.createdAt) - Date.parse(a.createdAt) || a.id.localeCompare(b.id));
 161 }

 163 /** @param {import('./types').Library} local @param {import('./types').ShareSelection} selection */
 164 export function createShare(local, selection) {
 165   const selected = (rows, ids) => {
 166     const keys = new Set(ids);
 167     check([...keys].every(id => rows.some(row => row.id === id)), '分享選取已改變，請重新選擇。');
 168     return rows.filter(row => keys.has(row.id));
 169   };
 170   const markers = selected(local.markers, selection.markerIds), artifacts = selected(local.exports, selection.exportIds);
 171   const recipes = local.recipes.filter(r => selection.takeIds.includes(r.takeId) || artifacts.some(e => e.recipeId === r.id));
 172   const takes = selected(local.takes, [...selection.takeIds, ...recipes.map(r => r.takeId)]);
 173   const videos = selected(local.videos, [...selection.videoIds, ...markers.map(m => m.videoId), ...takes.map(t => t.referenceVideoId)]);
 174   check(videos.length > 0, '請至少選一份影片筆記或片段。');
 175   check(takes.every(t => recipes.some(r => r.takeId === t.id)), '選取的練習缺少配方，請先修復。');
 176   const assets = local.assets.filter(a => a.kind === 'reference' && videos.some(v => v.id === a.videoId) || takes.some(t => t.cameraAssetId === a.id) || artifacts.some(e => e.assetId === a.id));
 177   const out = newLibrary(); out.kind = 'share'; out.sourceLibraryId = local.sourceLibraryId;
 178   // 不能先展開整個物件再刪幾欄：未來動作庫、節拍或裝置欄位也必須預設不外流。
 179   out.videos = videos.map(v => ({ id: v.id, title: v.title, durationMs: v.durationMs, revision: v.revision,
 180     ...Object.fromEntries(['teacher', 'danceStyle', 'recordedDate'].filter(key => selection.fields.includes(key) && v[key] !== undefined).map(key => [key, v[key]])) }));
 181   out.markers = markers.map(m => ({ id: m.id, videoId: m.videoId, startMs: m.startMs, ...(m.endMs === undefined ? {} : { endMs: m.endMs }), title: m.title, note: selection.fields.includes('note') ? m.note : '', revision: m.revision }));
 182   out.assets = assets.map(a => ({ id: a.id, ...(a.videoId ? { videoId: a.videoId } : {}), kind: a.kind, byteLength: a.byteLength, mimeType: a.mimeType, ...(a.durationMs ? { durationMs: a.durationMs } : {}),
 183     ...(selection.fields.includes('originalName') && a.originalName !== undefined ? { originalName: a.originalName } : {}), mediaIncluded: false }));
 184   // v1 必填的建立時間用固定佔位值；不透露練習日期，也不讓每次匯出都製造內容衝突。
 185   const hiddenDate = '1970-01-01T00:00:00.000Z';
 186   out.takes = takes.map(t => ({ id: t.id, referenceVideoId: t.referenceVideoId, cameraAssetId: t.cameraAssetId, referenceStartMs: t.referenceStartMs, referenceEndMs: t.referenceEndMs, rate: t.rate, state: t.state, createdAt: hiddenDate }));
 187   out.recipes = recipes.map(r => ({ id: r.id, takeId: r.takeId, offsetMs: r.offsetMs, referenceMirror: r.referenceMirror, selfMirror: r.selfMirror, layout: r.layout, audioSource: r.audioSource, revision: r.revision }));
 188   out.exports = artifacts.map(e => ({ id: e.id, recipeId: e.recipeId, recipeRevision: e.recipeRevision, assetId: e.assetId, mimeType: e.mimeType, state: e.state, createdAt: hiddenDate, ...(e.presentation === undefined ? {} : { presentation: exportPresentation(e.presentation) }) }));
 189   return validateBackup(out);
 190 }
 191 /** @param {import('./types').Library} local @param {import('./types').Library} incoming */
 192 export function mergeBackup(local, incoming) {
 193   incoming = validateBackup(incoming);
 194   check(incoming.kind === 'backup', '分享檔須先預覽並重建 ID，不能直接合併。');
 195   const next = migrateLibrary(local);
 196 
 197   const normalizedLocal = validateBackup(local);
 198   // 以整份來源記錄的對照表保證重匯入冪等；同 ID 衝突直接拒絕，保留全部本機資料。
 199   for (const table of tables) {
 200     const existing = new Map(normalizedLocal[table].map(row => /** @type {const} */ ([row.id, row])));
 201     for (const row of incoming[table]) {
 202       const old = existing.get(row.id);
 203       if (old && JSON.stringify(old) !== JSON.stringify(row)) throw new Error('備份與本機有相同 ID 的不同內容；請保留原檔，改用另一個瀏覽器資料夾還原。');
 204       if (!old) (/** @type {Array<{id: string, state?: string, storageKind?: string}>} */ (next[table])).push(table === 'assets' ? { ...row, state: 'missing', storageKind: 'external' } : row);
 205     }
 206   }
 207   validateBackup(next);
 208   return next;
 209 }

 211 // 同一份關聯表用於依賴閉包與 ID 重寫，避免副本把 take／輸出連到本機另一支影片。
 212 const backupRelations = { videos: {}, assets: { videoId: 'videos' }, markers: { videoId: 'videos', moveId: 'moves' }, takes: { referenceVideoId: 'videos', cameraAssetId: 'assets' }, recipes: { takeId: 'takes' }, exports: { recipeId: 'recipes', assetId: 'assets' }, moves: {} };
 213 const rowKey = (table, id) => table + ':' + id;
 214 const canonical = row => JSON.stringify(row);
 215 /** @param {import('./types').Recipe[]} recipes */
 216 function recipeSettingsByTake(recipes) {
 217   const groups = new Map();
 218   for (const { id: _id, takeId, ...settings } of recipes) {
 219     const group = groups.get(takeId) || []; group.push(canonical(settings)); groups.set(takeId, group);
 220   }
 221   for (const group of groups.values()) group.sort();
 222   return groups;
 223 }
 224 function rewriteBackupRow(row, table, ids) {
 225   const next = { ...row, id: ids[table].get(row.id) || row.id };
 226   for (const [field, parent] of Object.entries(backupRelations[table])) if (row[field] !== undefined) next[field] = ids[parent].get(row[field]) || row[field];
 227   return next;
 228 }
 229 async function backupUUID(value, signal) {
 230   signal?.throwIfAborted();
 231   if (!crypto.subtle) throw new Error('安全匯入副本需要受信任的 HTTPS 或本機預覽；原資料保留。');
 232   const bytes = new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(value)))).slice(0, 16);
 233   signal?.throwIfAborted();
 234   // 只對筆記描述建立可重現的 UUIDv8，不讀影片內容，也不把 hash 當身分或授權證明。
 235   bytes[6] = (bytes[6] & 15) | 128; bytes[8] = (bytes[8] & 63) | 128;
 236   const hex = [...bytes].map(byte => byte.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
 237   return [hex.slice(0, 8), hex.slice(8, 12), hex.slice(12, 16), hex.slice(16, 20), hex.slice(20)].join('-');
 238 }
 239 /** @param {import('./types').Library} local @param {import('./types').Library} source @param {AbortSignal} [signal] */
 240 export async function planBackupImport(local, source, signal) {
 241   const existingData = validateBackup(local), incoming = validateBackup(source);
 242   const shared = incoming.kind === 'share';
 243   // 分享永遠重建 ID，連空庫或寄回原作者也不能信任寄件者的本機身分。
 244   const empty = tables.every(table => local[table].length === 0), foreign = shared || !empty && local.sourceLibraryId !== incoming.sourceLibraryId;
 245   const ids = Object.fromEntries(tables.map(table => [table, new Map()]));
 246   if (foreign) {
 247     for (const table of tables) for (const row of incoming[table]) ids[table].set(row.id, await backupUUID([shared ? 'dance-stamp-share-v1' : 'dance-stamp-import-v1', incoming.sourceLibraryId, table, row.id], signal));
 248     for (const table of tables) for (const row of incoming[table]) Object.assign(row, rewriteBackupRow(row, table, ids));
 249   }
 250   const existing = Object.fromEntries(tables.map(table => [table, new Map(existingData[table].map(row => /** @type {const} */ ([row.id, row])))]));
 251   const conflicts = [];
 252   const forks = new Set();
 253   for (const table of tables) for (const row of incoming[table]) {
 254     const old = existing[table].get(row.id);
 255     if (old && canonical(old) !== canonical(row)) { conflicts.push({ table, id: row.id }); forks.add(rowKey(table, row.id)); }
 256   }
 257   // 示範媒體變更也要分開筆記主體，否則同一 video 會出現兩個不同的 reference。
 258   let changed = true;
 259   while (changed) {
 260     changed = false;
 261     const fork = (table, id) => { const key = rowKey(table, id); if (!forks.has(key)) { forks.add(key); changed = true; } };
 262     for (const asset of incoming.assets) if (asset.kind === 'reference' && asset.videoId && forks.has(rowKey('assets', asset.id))) fork('videos', asset.videoId);
 263     for (const artifact of incoming.exports) if (forks.has(rowKey('exports', artifact.id))) fork('assets', artifact.assetId);
 264     // 開啟入口以 take 選配方；只複製 recipe 會讓新校正永遠被第一份舊配方遮住。
 265     for (const recipe of incoming.recipes) if (forks.has(rowKey('recipes', recipe.id))) fork('takes', recipe.takeId);
 266     for (const table of tables) for (const row of incoming[table]) {
 267       if (Object.entries(backupRelations[table]).some(([field, parent]) => row[field] !== undefined && forks.has(rowKey(parent, row[field])))) fork(table, row.id);
 268     }
 269   }
 270   const referenceGroups = new Map();
 271   for (const asset of incoming.assets) if (asset.kind === 'reference' && asset.videoId) {
 272     const group = referenceGroups.get(asset.videoId) || []; group.push(asset); referenceGroups.set(asset.videoId, group);
 273   }
 274   for (const group of referenceGroups.values()) group.sort((a, b) => a.id < b.id ? -1 : a.id > b.id ? 1 : 0);
 275   const records = Object.fromEntries(tables.map(table => [table, new Map(incoming[table].map(row => /** @type {const} */ ([row.id, row])))]));
 276   const exportGroups = new Map(), videoSignatures = new Map();
 277   for (const artifact of incoming.exports) { const group = exportGroups.get(artifact.assetId) || []; group.push(artifact); exportGroups.set(artifact.assetId, group); }
 278   for (const group of exportGroups.values()) group.sort((a, b) => a.id < b.id ? -1 : a.id > b.id ? 1 : 0);
 279   const videoSignature = id => {
 280     // 共用的示範描述只 hash 一次，避免大型備份中每個輸出重複序列化整份來源。
 281     if (!videoSignatures.has(id)) videoSignatures.set(id, backupUUID(['dance-stamp-origin-v1', records.videos.get(id), referenceGroups.get(id) || []], signal));
 282     return videoSignatures.get(id);
 283   };
 284   const copies = Object.fromEntries(tables.map(table => [table, new Map()]));
 285   const recipeGroups = recipeSettingsByTake(incoming.recipes), existingRecipes = recipeSettingsByTake(existingData.recipes);
 286   // move 是 marker 的父項；先配置動作副本 ID，marker 的穩定 ID 才包含正確關聯。
 287   for (const table of ['moves', ...tables.filter(table => table !== 'moves')]) {
 288     const reserved = new Set(incoming[table].map(row => row.id));
 289     for (const row of incoming[table]) {
 290       if (!forks.has(rowKey(table, row.id))) continue;
 291       const rewritten = rewriteBackupRow(row, table, copies);
 292       let referenceFingerprint = table === 'videos' ? await videoSignature(row.id) : [];
 293       // 相同 take 的不同備份校正需要不同副本；不靠 recipe 的新 ID 反推，避免循環相依。
 294       if (table === 'takes') referenceFingerprint = recipeGroups.get(row.id) || [];
 295       if (table === 'assets' && 'kind' in row && row.kind === 'vs') {
 296         const origins = [];
 297         // 複製配方不能誤取本機舊 VS；描述含來源版本，沒有複製影片 bytes。
 298         for (const artifact of exportGroups.get(row.id) || []) {
 299           const recipe = records.recipes.get(artifact.recipeId), take = records.takes.get(recipe.takeId);
 300           origins.push([artifact, recipe, take, await videoSignature(take.referenceVideoId), records.assets.get(take.cameraAssetId)]);
 301         }
 302         referenceFingerprint = origins;
 303       }
 304       let assigned = false;
 305       // 已匯入的副本若再被編輯，尋找下一個穩定 ID；原件及編輯版都不能覆寫。
 306       for (let counter = 0; counter <= existing[table].size + incoming[table].length; counter++) {
 307         const id = await backupUUID(['dance-stamp-copy-v1', incoming.sourceLibraryId, table, rewritten, referenceFingerprint, counter], signal);
 308         if (reserved.has(id)) continue;
 309         const old = existing[table].get(id);
 310         if (old && canonical(old) !== canonical({ ...rewritten, id })) continue;
 311         // 已匯入的校正若被編輯，原 take 也要保留；不可再往同一 take 塞第二份不可選的 recipe。
 312         if (old && table === 'takes' && canonical(existingRecipes.get(id) || []) !== canonical(recipeGroups.get(row.id) || [])) continue;
 313         copies[table].set(row.id, id); reserved.add(id); assigned = true; break;
 314       }
 315       if (!assigned) throw new Error('無法安全配置副本 ID；原資料保留，沒有匯入。');
 316     }
 317   }
 318   for (const table of tables) for (const row of incoming[table]) Object.assign(row, rewriteBackupRow(row, table, copies));
 319   const additions = Object.fromEntries(tables.map(table => [table, incoming[table].filter(row => !existing[table].has(row.id)).length]));
 320   const added = Object.values(additions).reduce((sum, count) => sum + count, 0);
 321   // 只有完成 ID／衝突規劃的資料才能走低階合併，避免其他呼叫者誤信分享檔的原 ID。
 322   incoming.kind = 'backup';
 323   const data = mergeBackup(local, incoming);
 324   if (empty && !shared) data.sourceLibraryId = incoming.sourceLibraryId;
 325   signal?.throwIfAborted();
 326   return { data, additions, added, conflicts, copied: forks.size, differentSource: foreign, skipped: tables.reduce((sum, table) => sum + incoming[table].length, 0) - added };
 327 }

 329 /** @param {import('./types').Video[]} videos */
 330 export function videoLabels(videos) {
 331   const counts = new Map(), seen = new Map();
 332   for (const video of videos) counts.set(video.title, (counts.get(video.title) || 0) + 1);
 333   return new Map(videos.map(video => {
 334     const index = (seen.get(video.title) || 0) + 1; seen.set(video.title, index);
 335     return [video.id, counts.get(video.title) > 1 ? video.title + '（第' + index + '份）' : video.title];
 336   }));
 337 }

 339 /** @param {import('./types').Library} local @param {'copy'|'take'|'export'|'video'|'move'} kind @param {string} id */
 340 export function planDeletion(local, kind, id) {
 341   validateBackup(local);
 342   const data = structuredClone(local), candidates = new Set(), locks = new Set();
 343   const table = ({ copy: 'assets', take: 'takes', export: 'exports', video: 'videos', move: 'moves' })[kind];
 344   if (!table || !data[table].some(row => row.id === id)) throw new Error('找不到要移除的資料；原資料保留。');
 345   if (kind === 'move') {
 346     // 移除動作只是解除分類，不能刪掉跨課程共用的標記、配方或原片。
 347     data.moves = data.moves.filter(move => move.id !== id);
 348     for (const marker of data.markers) if (marker.moveId === id) { delete marker.moveId; marker.revision++; }
 349   } else if (kind === 'copy') {
 350     const asset = data.assets.find(a => a.id === id);
 351     if (!asset) throw new Error('媒體描述已失聯；原資料保留。');
 352     Object.assign(asset, { state: 'missing', storageKind: 'external', mediaIncluded: false });
 353     data.takes.filter(t => t.cameraAssetId === id || asset.videoId === t.referenceVideoId).forEach(t => { locks.add(t.id); });
 354   } else {
 355     const removedTakes = new Set(data.takes.filter(t => kind === 'take' ? t.id === id : kind === 'video' && t.referenceVideoId === id).map(t => t.id));
 356     const removedRecipes = new Set(data.recipes.filter(r => removedTakes.has(r.takeId)).map(r => r.id));
 357     for (const t of data.takes) if (removedTakes.has(t.id)) { candidates.add(t.cameraAssetId); locks.add(t.id); }
 358     for (const a of data.exports) if ((kind === 'export' && a.id === id) || removedRecipes.has(a.recipeId)) candidates.add(a.assetId);
 359     data.exports = data.exports.filter(a => !(kind === 'export' && a.id === id) && !removedRecipes.has(a.recipeId));
 360     data.recipes = data.recipes.filter(r => !removedRecipes.has(r.id));
 361     data.takes = data.takes.filter(t => !removedTakes.has(t.id));
 362     if (kind === 'video') {
 363       data.assets.filter(a => a.videoId === id).forEach(a => { candidates.add(a.id); });
 364       data.videos = data.videos.filter(v => v.id !== id); data.markers = data.markers.filter(m => m.videoId !== id);
 365     }
 366     // 多個配方可共用原片；只刪失去所有關聯的媒體，不能順手清掉其他 take 的副本。
 367     const videos = new Set(data.videos.map(v => v.id));
 368     const used = new Set([...data.takes.map(t => t.cameraAssetId), ...data.exports.map(a => a.assetId), ...data.assets.filter(a => a.videoId && videos.has(a.videoId)).map(a => a.id)]);
 369     data.assets = data.assets.filter(a => !candidates.has(a.id) || used.has(a.id));
 370   }
 371   validateBackup(data);
 372   const assets = new Set(data.assets.map(a => a.id)), takes = new Set(data.takes.map(t => t.id));
 373   const mediaIds = kind === 'copy' ? [id] : local.assets.filter(a => !assets.has(a.id)).map(a => a.id);
 374   const takeIds = local.takes.filter(t => !takes.has(t.id)).map(t => t.id);
 375   // chunks 以相機 asset ID 為 key，不是 take ID；共用原片仍被引用時不得清理其分段。
 376   const chunkIds = kind === 'copy' ? [] : local.assets.filter(a => a.kind === 'take' && !assets.has(a.id)).map(a => a.id);
 377   const counts = Object.fromEntries(['videos', 'markers', 'takes', 'recipes', 'exports'].map(name => [name, local[name].length - data[name].length]));
 378   return { data, mediaIds, takeIds, chunkIds, locks: [...locks].sort(), counts, kind, id };
 379 }
 380 /** @param {import('./types').Library} data @param {Set<string>} stored @param {Set<string>} session */
 381 export function mediaInventory(data, stored, session) {
 382   const names = videoLabels(data.videos), owners = new Map(), takes = new Map(data.takes.map(t => [t.id, t])), recipes = new Map(data.recipes.map(r => [r.id, r]));
 383   const addOwner = (assetId, videoId) => { const group = owners.get(assetId) || new Set(); group.add(videoId); owners.set(assetId, group); };
 384   for (const asset of data.assets) if (asset.videoId) addOwner(asset.id, asset.videoId);
 385   for (const take of data.takes) addOwner(take.cameraAssetId, take.referenceVideoId);
 386   for (const artifact of data.exports) {
 387     const recipe = recipes.get(artifact.recipeId), take = recipe && takes.get(recipe.takeId);
 388     if (take) addOwner(artifact.assetId, take.referenceVideoId);
 389   }
 390   const interrupted = new Set(data.takes.filter(t => t.state === 'interrupted' || t.state === 'recording').map(t => t.cameraAssetId));
 391   return data.assets.map(asset => {
 392     const ids = [...(owners.get(asset.id) || [])], labels = ids.slice(0, 3).map(id => names.get(id));
 393     const owner = labels.join('、') + (ids.length > 3 ? '等 ' + ids.length + ' 份筆記' : '');
 394     // 只依實際媒體 key／已確認的本次 File 判斷可用，不相信備份中的 ready 宣告。
 395     const availability = stored.has(asset.id) ? 'stored' : session.has(asset.id) ? 'session' : 'missing';
 396     return { asset, owner: owner || '未關聯的媒體', availability, interrupted: interrupted.has(asset.id) };
 397   });
 398 }
