web/local-audio.js — 100% lines
   1 /* 檔案用途：有界讀取本機 MP4 的 AAC 片段，避開 Safari 元素來源漏音。所在層：媒體。主要關聯：main.js、timeline.js、RECORD_COMPARE.md。 */
   2 import { MAX_COMPARISON_MS } from './timeline.js';
   3 // ponytail: 只支援單軌 AAC-LC 的一般 MP4；其餘格式明確拒絕，之後有實片需求才擴充 demux。
   4 export const audioBudget = Object.freeze({ metadata: 8 * 1024 * 1024, samples: 250_000, packet: 16 * 1024, batch: 8, seconds: MAX_COMPARISON_MS / 1000, sampleRate: 48_000, channels: 2 });
   5 const unsupported = () => new Error('Safari 音訊相容合成目前支援一般 MP4 的單一 AAC-LC 音軌（最多雙聲道、48kHz）。此片格式或資源超出範圍，請改用 Chrome；原片與前次 VS 保留。');
   6 const require = value => { if (!value) throw unsupported(); };
   7 export function safariAudio(userAgent = navigator.userAgent) {
   8   return /AppleWebKit/.test(userAgent) && !/Chrome|Chromium|Edg|OPR|Android/.test(userAgent);
   9 }
  10 const u32 = (v, p) => v.getUint32(p);
  11 const u64 = (v, p) => { const n = Number(v.getBigUint64(p)); require(Number.isSafeInteger(n)); return n; };
  12 const text = (v, p) => String.fromCharCode(...new Uint8Array(v.buffer, v.byteOffset + p, 4));
  13 function boxes(v, start = 0, end = v.byteLength) {
  14   const result = [];
  15   while (start < end) {
  16     require(result.length < 4096 && end - start >= 8);
  17     let size = u32(v, start), header = 8;
  18     if (size === 1) { require(end - start >= 16); size = u64(v, start + 8); header = 16; }
  19     if (size === 0) size = end - start;
  20     require(size >= header && size <= end - start);
  21     result.push({ type: text(v, start + 4), start: start + header, end: start + size }); start += size;
  22   }
  23   return result;
  24 }
  25 function one(list, type, optional = false) {
  26   const found = list.filter(b => b.type === type);
  27   require(found.length === 1 || (optional && found.length === 0)); return found[0];
  28 }
  29 function children(v, b) { return boxes(v, b.start, b.end); }
  30 function need(b, length) { require(b.end - b.start >= length); return b.start; }
  31 function table(v, b, stride, extra = 0) {
  32   const p = need(b, 8 + extra), count = u32(v, p + 4 + extra);
  33   require(v.getUint8(p) === 0 && count <= audioBudget.samples && b.end - p === 8 + extra + count * stride);
  34   return { p: p + 8 + extra, count };
  35 }
  36 async function read(file, start, size, signal) {
  37   signal.throwIfAborted(); require(size <= audioBudget.metadata && start >= 0 && start + size <= file.size);
  38   const data = await file.slice(start, start + size).arrayBuffer(); signal.throwIfAborted();
  39   require(data.byteLength === size); return new DataView(data);
  40 }
  41 function descriptor(v, p, end) {
  42   require(p + 2 <= end); const tag = v.getUint8(p++); let length = 0, byte;
  43   for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) { require(p < end); byte = v.getUint8(p++); length = length * 128 + (byte & 127); if (!(byte & 128)) break; }
  44   require(!(byte & 128) && p + length <= end); return { tag, start: p, end: p + length };
  45 }
  46 function aacConfig(v, stsd) {
  47   const p = need(stsd, 8); require(u32(v, p) === 0 && u32(v, p + 4) === 1);
  48   const entry = one(boxes(v, p + 8, stsd.end), 'mp4a'); need(entry, 28);
  49   // QuickTime v1/v2、外部資料參照與加密 entry 另有語意；不能猜它們的封包位置。
  50   require(v.getUint16(entry.start + 8) === 0 && v.getUint16(entry.start + 6) === 1);
  51   const esds = one(boxes(v, entry.start + 28, entry.end), 'esds'); need(esds, 4);
  52   require(u32(v, esds.start) === 0);
  53   const es = descriptor(v, esds.start + 4, esds.end); require(es.tag === 3 && es.end - es.start >= 3 && v.getUint8(es.start + 2) === 0);
  54   const config = descriptor(v, es.start + 3, es.end); require(config.tag === 4 && config.end - config.start >= 13 && v.getUint8(config.start) === 0x40 && v.getUint8(config.start + 1) === 0x15);
  55   const asc = descriptor(v, config.start + 13, config.end); require(asc.tag === 5 && asc.end - asc.start >= 2 && asc.end - asc.start <= 16);
  56   const bits = v.getUint16(asc.start), rates = [96000, 88200, 64000, 48000, 44100, 32000, 24000, 22050, 16000, 12000, 11025, 8000, 7350];
  57   const rate = rates[(bits >> 7) & 15], channels = (bits >> 3) & 15;
  58   // 排除 SBR、PCE、960-frame 與擴充設定；解碼後仍逐包檢查，不能只信容器聲道數。
  59   require(bits >> 11 === 2 && rate >= 8000 && rate <= audioBudget.sampleRate && channels >= 1 && channels <= audioBudget.channels && (bits & 7) === 0);
  60   const description = new Uint8Array(v.buffer, v.byteOffset + asc.start, asc.end - asc.start).slice();
  61   require(description.length === 2 || (description.length === 5 && description[2] === 0x56 && description[3] === 0xe5 && description[4] === 0));
  62   return { codec: 'mp4a.40.2', sampleRate: rate, numberOfChannels: channels, description };
  63 }
  64 function timescale(v, b) {
  65   const p = need(b, 24), version = v.getUint8(p); require(version <= 1); if (version) need(b, 36);
  66   const result = u32(v, p + (version ? 20 : 12)); require(result > 0); return result;
  67 }
  68 
  69 /** @param {Blob} file @param {AbortSignal} signal */
  70 export async function readAudioTrack(file, signal) {
  71   // 只跳過 mdat；不把長影片或未知大小的 moov 整檔讀進記憶體。
  72   let moov, count = 0; const media = [];
  73   for (let pos = 0; pos < file.size;) {
  74     require(++count <= 4096 && file.size - pos >= 8);
  75     const h = await read(file, pos, Math.min(16, file.size - pos), signal), type = text(h, 4);
  76     let size = u32(h, 0), header = 8;
  77     if (size === 1) { require(h.byteLength >= 16); size = u64(h, 8); header = 16; }
  78     if (!size) size = file.size - pos;
  79     require(size >= header && size <= file.size - pos && !['moof', 'mfra'].includes(type));
  80     if (type === 'moov') { require(!moov && size <= audioBudget.metadata); moov = await read(file, pos + header, size - header, signal); }
  81     if (type === 'mdat') { require(media.length < 4096); media.push({ start: pos + header, end: pos + size }); }
  82     pos += size;
  83   }
  84   require(moov); const v = moov, root = boxes(v); require(!root.some(b => b.type === 'mvex'));
  85   const tracks = root.filter(b => b.type === 'trak'); require(tracks.length > 0 && tracks.length <= 16);
  86   const audio = tracks.filter(track => {
  87     const mdia = children(v, one(children(v, track), 'mdia')), handler = one(mdia, 'hdlr'); need(handler, 12);
  88     return text(v, handler.start + 8) === 'soun';
  89   });
  90   require(audio.length <= 1); if (!audio.length) return null;
  91   const track = children(v, audio[0]), mdia = children(v, one(track, 'mdia')), scale = timescale(v, one(mdia, 'mdhd'));
  92   const minf = children(v, one(mdia, 'minf')), stbl = children(v, one(minf, 'stbl')), stsd = one(stbl, 'stsd'), config = aacConfig(v, stsd);
  93   require(scale === config.sampleRate && !stbl.some(b => ['ctts', 'stz2', 'senc', 'saiz', 'saio'].includes(b.type)));
  94   // 只接受自包含資料，不可依外部 URL 讀取來源或將壞 offset 當音訊。
  95   const dref = one(children(v, one(minf, 'dinf')), 'dref'), dr = need(dref, 8);
  96   require(u32(v, dr + 4) === 1); const url = one(boxes(v, dr + 8, dref.end), 'url '); require(url.end - url.start === 4 && u32(v, url.start) === 1);
  97   let shift = 0, audioStart = 0, audioEnd = Infinity;
  98   const edts = one(track, 'edts', true);
  99   if (edts) {
 100     const elst = one(children(v, edts), 'elst'), p = need(elst, 8), version = v.getUint8(p); require(version <= 1);
 101     const n = u32(v, p + 4), stride = version ? 20 : 12; require(n >= 1 && n <= 2 && elst.end - p === 8 + n * stride);
 102     const movieScale = timescale(v, one(root, 'mvhd')); let delay = 0;
 103     for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
 104       const q = p + 8 + i * stride, duration = version ? u64(v, q) : u32(v, q), at = version ? Number(v.getBigInt64(q + 8)) : v.getInt32(q + 4);
 105       require(Number.isSafeInteger(at) && u32(v, q + stride - 4) === 0x10000);
 106       if (at === -1) { require(i === 0 && n === 2); delay = duration / movieScale; }
 107       else { require(at >= 0 && i === n - 1); shift = delay - at / scale; audioStart = delay; audioEnd = delay + duration / movieScale; }
 108     }
 109   }
 110   const sz = one(stbl, 'stsz'), sp = need(sz, 12), fixed = u32(v, sp + 4), n = u32(v, sp + 8);
 111   require(u32(v, sp) === 0 && n > 0 && n <= audioBudget.samples && sz.end - sp === 12 + (fixed ? 0 : n * 4) && fixed <= audioBudget.packet);
 112   const sizes = new Uint32Array(n), offsets = new Float64Array(n), times = new Float64Array(n + 1);
 113   for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { sizes[i] = fixed || u32(v, sp + 12 + i * 4); require(sizes[i] > 0 && sizes[i] <= audioBudget.packet); }
 114   const stts = table(v, one(stbl, 'stts'), 8); let index = 0, time = 0;
 115   for (let i = 0; i < stts.count; i++) {
 116     const q = stts.p + i * 8, repeat = u32(v, q), delta = u32(v, q + 4); require(repeat > 0 && index + repeat <= n && delta > 0 && delta <= 1024);
 117     for (let j = 0; j < repeat; j++) { require(delta === 1024 || index === n - 1); times[index++] = time / scale + shift; time += delta; }
 118   }
 119   require(index === n); times[n] = time / scale + shift;
 120   const co = one(stbl, 'stco', true), large = one(stbl, 'co64', true); require(!!co !== !!large);
 121   const chunks = table(v, co || large, co ? 4 : 8), sc = table(v, one(stbl, 'stsc'), 12);
 122   require(chunks.count > 0 && sc.count > 0); index = 0; let run = 0;
 123   for (let i = 0; i < sc.count; i++) {
 124     const q = sc.p + i * 12, first = u32(v, q); require((i ? first > u32(v, q - 12) : first === 1) && first <= chunks.count && u32(v, q + 4) > 0 && u32(v, q + 8) === 1);
 125   }
 126   for (let chunk = 1; chunk <= chunks.count; chunk++) {
 127     if (run + 1 < sc.count && chunk === u32(v, sc.p + (run + 1) * 12)) run++;
 128     const perChunk = u32(v, sc.p + run * 12 + 4); require(index + perChunk <= n);
 129     let offset = co ? u32(v, chunks.p + (chunk - 1) * 4) : u64(v, chunks.p + (chunk - 1) * 8);
 130     const begin = offset;
 131     for (let j = 0; j < perChunk; j++) { offsets[index] = offset; offset += sizes[index++]; }
 132     require(media.some(b => begin >= b.start && offset <= b.end));
 133   }
 134   require(index === n);
 135   return { config, sizes, offsets, times, audioStart, audioEnd: Math.min(audioEnd, times[n]) };
 136 }
 137 
 138 // timeout 與取消涵蓋原生解碼等待；close() 才能真正取消 queue，不能只有 Promise.race。
 139 export async function audioReady(promise, signal) {
 140   signal.throwIfAborted();
 141   return await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
 142     const abort = () => finish(signal.reason), timer = setTimeout(() => finish(new Error('音訊準備逾時，請重試；原片與前次 VS 保留。')), 10_000);
 143     const finish = (error, value) => { clearTimeout(timer); signal.removeEventListener('abort', abort); error ? reject(error) : resolve(value); };
 144     signal.addEventListener('abort', abort, { once: true }); promise.then(value => finish(null, value), finish);
 145   });
 146 }

 148 /** @param {Blob} file @param {AudioContext} context @param {number} start @param {number} duration @param {AbortSignal} signal */
 149 export async function decodeLocalAudio(file, context, start, duration, signal) {
 150   require(Number.isFinite(start) && start >= 0 && Number.isFinite(duration) && duration > 0 && duration <= audioBudget.seconds);
 151   let decoder, buffer, failure;
 152   /** @type {(() => void) | undefined} */
 153   let wake;
 154   const cancel = () => { if (decoder && decoder.state !== 'closed') decoder.close(); buffer = null; };
 155   signal.throwIfAborted(); signal.addEventListener('abort', cancel, { once: true });
 156   try {
 157     const track = await readAudioTrack(file, signal); if (!track) return null;
 158     require(globalThis.AudioDecoder && globalThis.EncodedAudioChunk);
 159     const support = await audioReady(AudioDecoder.isConfigSupported(track.config), signal); require(support.supported); signal.throwIfAborted();
 160     const rate = track.config.sampleRate, channels = track.config.numberOfChannels, frames = Math.ceil(duration * rate);
 161     buffer = context.createBuffer(channels, frames, rate);
 162     let outputs = 0, submitted = 0;
 163     decoder = new AudioDecoder({
 164       output(data) {
 165         try {
 166           if (signal.aborted || failure) return;
 167           require(data.sampleRate === rate && data.numberOfChannels === channels && data.numberOfFrames > 0 && data.numberOfFrames <= 1024);
 168           // 前置封包只供解碼器暖機；空 edit 尚未結束前，不能把被丟棄的來源音訊寫進靜音區。
 169           const position = Math.round((data.timestamp / 1e6 - start) * rate), from = Math.max(0, -position, Math.round((track.audioStart - start) * rate) - position), to = Math.min(data.numberOfFrames, frames - position, Math.round((track.audioEnd - start) * rate) - position);
 170           if (to > from) for (let channel = 0; channel < channels; channel++) {
 171             const pcm = new Float32Array(data.numberOfFrames); data.copyTo(pcm, { planeIndex: channel, format: 'f32-planar' });
 172             buffer.getChannelData(channel).set(pcm.subarray(from, to), position + from);
 173           }
 174           outputs++;
 175         } catch (error) { failure = error; }
 176         finally { data.close(); wake?.(); }
 177       },
 178       error(error) { failure = error; wake?.(); },
 179     });
 180     decoder.configure(track.config);
 181     const { times, sizes, offsets } = track;
 182     let first = 0; while (first < sizes.length && times[first + 1] <= start) first++;
 183     // AAC 需要前置封包恢復重疊窗；取來源時間戳裁入固定片段，不能用固定裝置延遲冒充校正。
 184     first = Math.max(0, first - 2);
 185     for (let i = first; i < sizes.length && times[i] < start + duration; i++) {
 186       signal.throwIfAborted(); if (failure) throw failure;
 187       const packet = await read(file, offsets[i], sizes[i], signal);
 188       decoder.decode(new EncodedAudioChunk({ type: 'key', timestamp: Math.round(times[i] * 1e6), data: packet })); submitted++;
 189       // 中途 flush 會破壞 Safari 的 AAC 重疊窗；等真正 PCM 交付才補包，仍最多八包未交付。
 190       // decodeQueueSize 只計控制佇列，不能代替尚在 codec 內的輸出；訂閱後再查一次避免漏掉交付。
 191       if (submitted - outputs >= audioBudget.batch) {
 192         try {
 193           await audioReady(new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
 194             wake = () => { if (failure) reject(failure); else if (submitted - outputs < audioBudget.batch) resolve(undefined); };
 195             wake();
 196           }), signal);
 197         } finally { wake = undefined; }
 198         if (failure) throw failure;
 199       }
 200     }
 201     await audioReady(decoder.flush(), signal); signal.throwIfAborted(); if (failure) throw failure;
 202     // AAC 每包 1024 格；兩包暖機與首尾跨界也須計入，不能殘留舊 60 秒封包上限。
 203     require(outputs === submitted && submitted <= Math.ceil(audioBudget.seconds * audioBudget.sampleRate / 1024) + 3);
 204     return buffer;
 205   } catch (error) {
 206     buffer = null; if (signal.aborted) throw signal.reason;
 207     if (error instanceof Error && /Safari|逾時/.test(error.message)) throw error;
 208     throw unsupported();
 209   } finally { signal.removeEventListener('abort', cancel); if (decoder && decoder.state !== 'closed') decoder.close(); }
 210 }

  98 function focusControl(element) {
  99   if (!(element instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
 100   // 先等 fieldset 重新啟用；直接 focus 停用中的輸入不會生效。
 101   if (active) { operationFocus = element; return; }
 102   /** @type {HTMLElement | null} */
 103   let target = element;
 104   if (!element.isConnected) {
 105     const key = element.dataset.focusKey;
 106     target = key ? document.querySelector(`[data-focus-key="${CSS.escape(key)}"]`) : null;
 107     if (!target && key?.startsWith('asset:')) target = document.querySelector(`[data-asset-id="${CSS.escape(key.split(':')[1])}"] button`) || $('refresh-media');
 108     target ||= key?.startsWith('marker:') ? $('mark') : $('library');
 109   }
 110   const modal = document.querySelector('dialog[open]');
 111   if (target && !target.matches(':disabled') && target.getClientRects().length && (!modal || modal.contains(target))) target.focus();
 112 }

 114 function urlFor(key, blob) {
 115   const previous = urls.get(key);
 116   const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
 117   urls.set(key, url);
 118   if (previous) URL.revokeObjectURL(previous);
 119   return url;
 120 }
 121 /** @param {Blob} blob @param {string} prefix @param {boolean} [output] */
 122 function mediaFilename(blob, prefix, output = false) {
 123   const originalSuffix = blob instanceof File ? blob.name.match(/\.(mp4|mov|webm|mkv|m4v)$/i)?.[1]?.toLowerCase() : undefined;
 124   // 原片下載沿用真實容器；未知格式保留 bytes 為 bin，不冒充轉碼。
 125   const suffix = output ? extension(blob.type) : originalSuffix || (blob.type.includes('quicktime') ? 'mov' : blob.type.startsWith('video/mp4') ? 'mp4' : blob.type.startsWith('video/webm') ? 'webm' : blob.type.includes('matroska') ? 'mkv' : 'bin');
 126   return `${prefix}.${suffix}`;
 127 }

 214   $('adjusted-side-status').textContent = !available || $('adjusted-side-layout').disabled ? $('overlay-layout-status').textContent : '左右各自調大小與位置，試著讓兩邊的人物一樣大、靠近中間。';
 215   if (available) {
 216     // 只認已提交成功的來源 URL；壞檔與取消回復舊片時，不能順便丟掉使用者的對齊。
 217     const sources = `${urls.get('reference')}\n${urls.get('self')}`;
 218     if (sources !== overlaySources) { overlaySources = sources; resetOverlayLayout(false); resetAdjustedSide(false); }
 219   }
 220   $('export-controls').disabled = !available || !!active || storageUnconfirmed();
 221   if (available && !active) { const value = recipe(); drawOverlay(value); drawAdjustedSide(value); }
 222 }
 223 function updateSourceTimelines() {
 224   for (const entry of sourceTimelines) {
 225     const duration = entry.duration() / 1000;
 226     const disabled = !duration || !!active || storageUnconfirmed() || $('compare').getAttribute('aria-busy') === 'true';
 227     entry.slider.disabled = disabled;
 228     for (const button of entry.buttons.querySelectorAll('button')) button.disabled = disabled;
 229     entry.slider.max = String(duration || 1);
 230     const time = Math.max(0, Math.min(duration, entry.video.currentTime || 0));
 231     entry.slider.value = String(time); entry.time.value = `${time.toFixed(3)} 秒`; entry.slider.setAttribute('aria-valuetext', `${time.toFixed(3)} 秒`);
 232     const start = entry.start();
 233     entry.chosen.textContent = !duration ? '尚未選片' : start < 0 ? `原片從 0 秒開始；比較偏移 ${start.toFixed(3)} 秒` : `已設起點 ${start.toFixed(3)} 秒`;
 234     // 刻度只隨來源長度重建；播放位置更新不能每次換掉滑桿或鍵盤焦點。
 235     if (entry.ruler.dataset.duration !== String(duration)) {
 236       entry.ruler.dataset.duration = String(duration); entry.ruler.replaceChildren();
 237       for (let i = 0; i <= 4; i++) {
 238         const tick = document.createElement('span'); tick.textContent = `${Number((duration * i / 4).toFixed(2))} 秒`; entry.ruler.append(tick);
 239       }
 240     }
 241   }
 242 }
 243 function locateSource(entry, seconds) {
 244   if (entry.slider.disabled || active) return;
 245   // 共同比較的偏移只能明確設起點時改；此處只讓使用者在原片中找同一個動作。
 246   stopPractice(); entry.video.pause();
 247   try { entry.video.currentTime = Math.max(0, Math.min(entry.duration() / 1000, seconds)); updateSourceTimelines(); }
 248   catch { status('影片尚未就緒，請重新選片後再定位；原片與起點保留。'); }
 249 }
 250 for (const entry of sourceTimelines) {
 251   entry.slider.addEventListener('input', () => locateSource(entry, Number(entry.slider.value)));
 252   for (const button of entry.buttons.querySelectorAll('button')) button.addEventListener('click', () => locateSource(entry, entry.video.currentTime + Number(button.dataset.seek)));
 253   for (const name of ['timeupdate', 'seeked']) entry.video.addEventListener(name, updateSourceTimelines);
 254 }
 255 async function operation(task) {
 256   if (active) return;
 257   if (storageUnconfirmed()) { showStorageRecovery(); return; }
 258   const controller = new AbortController(), trigger = document.activeElement;
 259   operationTrigger = trigger; operationFocus = null;
 260   active = controller; updateStopControl();
 261   for (const id of /** @type {const} */ (['controls', 'compare', 'practice', 'library-panel'])) $(id).setAttribute('aria-busy', 'true');
 262   for (const id of /** @type {const} */ (['inventory-controls', 'import-controls', 'relink-controls', 'delete-controls'])) $(id).disabled = true;
 263   $('share-controls').disabled = true;
 264   $('existing-mode').disabled = true;
 265   for (const id of /** @type {const} */ (['reference-start', 'reference-end', 'self-start'])) $(id).disabled = true;
 266   stopPractice();
 267   $('controls').disabled = true; $('compare').disabled = true;
 268   reference.controls = false; self.controls = false;
 269   updateOverlayControls();
 270   $('practice').disabled = true;
 271   for (const el of $('library-panel').querySelectorAll('button, input, select')) (/** @type {HTMLButtonElement | HTMLInputElement | HTMLSelectElement} */ (el)).disabled = true;
 272   try {
 273     await ready; await comparisonSave;
 274     // 等待期間也可能轉為未確認；已排隊的換片不能越過封鎖，否則仍會清掉當次自拍。
 275     if (storageUnconfirmed()) throw new Error(storageUnconfirmedMessage);
 276     await task(controller.signal);
 277   }
 278   catch (caught) { const error = caught instanceof Error ? caught : new Error(String(caught));
 279     releaseCamera();
 280     status(storageUnconfirmed() ? storageUnconfirmedMessage : error.name === 'AbortError' ? comparisonPlayback ? '兩片已一起暫停；可繼續播放或拖動共同時間線。' : '已停止，原片與上次成功的輸出仍保留。' : `${error.message} 原片仍保留，可重試。`);
 281     // 原生錯誤可能含檔名／裝置資訊；可帶走的診斷只留分類，詳細說明留在本機狀態。
 282     diagnostics.lastError = storageUnconfirmed() ? 'storage-unconfirmed' : error.name === 'AbortError' ? 'cancelled' : error.name === 'QuotaExceededError' ? 'storage-full' : 'operation-failed';
 283   } finally {
 284     reference.pause(); self.pause();
 285     if (monitor) monitor.gain.value = 1;
 286     if (audio?.state === 'running') await audio.suspend().catch(() => {});
 287     active = null; comparisonPlayback = null; $('controls').disabled = false; $('pause-comparison').hidden = true;
 288     // 比較結束先收起暫停控制，再恢復練習速度，避免下一次播放沿用合成設定。
 289     applyPracticeSettings(); drawPractice();
 290     reference.controls = true; self.controls = true;
 291     for (const id of /** @type {const} */ (['controls', 'compare', 'practice', 'library-panel'])) $(id).setAttribute('aria-busy', 'false');
 292     updateControls(); report();
 293     const current = document.activeElement, requested = operationFocus;
 294     operationTrigger = null; operationFocus = null;
 295     // 明確下一步且仍停在觸發控制時也要換焦；使用者已移到停止鍵等處則不搶回。
 296     if (current === document.body || current === document.documentElement || (requested && (current === trigger || current?.closest('dialog') === requested.closest('dialog') && current?.closest('dialog')))) focusControl(requested || trigger);
 297   }
 298 }
 299 function abortIfNeeded(signal) { signal.throwIfAborted(); }
 300 /** @returns {Promise<void>} */
 301 function wait(ms, signal) {
 302   return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
 303     const aborted = () => { clearTimeout(timer); reject(signal.reason); };
 304     const timer = setTimeout(() => { signal.removeEventListener('abort', aborted); resolve(); }, ms);
 305     signal.addEventListener('abort', aborted, { once: true });
 306     if (signal.aborted) aborted();
 307   });
 308 }
 309 /** @param {Partial<import('./types').Comparison>} [overrides] */
 310 function recipe(overrides = {}) {
 311   const value = { startMs: Math.round(Number($('start').value) * 1000), endMs: Math.round(Number($('end').value) * 1000), rate: Number($('rate').value), offsetMs: Math.round(Number($('offset').value) * 1000), selfDurationMs,
 312     referenceMirror: $('reference-mirror').checked, selfMirror: $('self-mirror').checked, ...overrides };
 313   if (!referenceDurationMs || value.endMs > referenceDurationMs) throw new Error('片段終點超過示範影片長度。');
 314   // 右片未載入時只驗設定；真正共同區間等實片載入後才計算。
 315   comparison({ ...value, selfDurationMs: selfDurationMs || MAX_COMPARISON_MS });
 316   return value;
 317 }
 318 function mirrors() {
 319   // 只翻轉媒體層，避免連控制文字一起反向；取景與輸出刻意使用不同欄位。
 320   preview.classList.toggle('mirror', $('preview-mirror').checked);
 321   // 原生 video 控制不翻轉；真正的鏡像比較以 canvas 與同一份配方呈現。
 322   if (referenceDurationMs && selfDurationMs && !active) {
 323     try { draw(recipe()); } catch { /* 尚未填好配方時保留上一幀。 */ }
 324   }
 325 }
 326 async function prepareAudio() {
 327   if (!audio) {
 328     audio = new AudioContext();
 329     monitor = audio.createGain();
 330     // Safari 直接播放原片；一旦掛上元素來源便無法還原原生聲路，會再次觸發開頭漏音。
 331     if (!localAudio) {
 332       audioSource = audio.createMediaElementSource(reference);
 333       audioDestination = audio.createMediaStreamDestination();
 334       // 同一個 video 一生只能掛一個來源節點；重試匯出時重用，避免第二次無聲或拋錯。
 335       audioSource.connect(monitor).connect(audio.destination);
 336       audioSource.connect(audioDestination);
 337     }
 338   }
 339   await audio.resume();
 340   if (audio.state !== 'running') throw new Error('音訊尚未啟動，請再次點按重試。');
 341 }
 342 async function countIn(grid, rate, signal) {
 343   const plan = countInPlan(grid, rate), start = audio.currentTime + 0.05;
 344   const cancel = scheduleCountIn(audio, plan, start), deadline = performance.now() + plan.durationMs + 2050;
 345   try {
 346     while (audio.currentTime < start + plan.durationMs / 1000) {
 347       abortIfNeeded(signal);
 348       if (audio.state !== 'running' || performance.now() > deadline) throw new Error('拍數倒數中斷，請重新點按錄影。');
 349       // 聲音與文字跟同一音訊時鐘；不逐拍累加 setTimeout，避免慢速倒數越來越偏。
 350       const passed = Math.max(0, (audio.currentTime - start) * 1000);
 351       status(`${Math.max(1, plan.beats - Math.floor(passed / plan.intervalMs))} 拍後開始（${rate}×），請就定位。`);
 352       await wait(30, signal);
 353     }
 354   } finally { cancel(); }
 355 }
 356 function resetOverlayLayout(repaint = true) {
 357   for (const [input, output] of overlayLayoutFields) {
 358     $(input).value = $(input).defaultValue; $(output).textContent = `${$(input).value}%`;
 359   }
 360   if (repaint && !overlay.hidden) drawOverlay(recipe());
 361 }
 362 function resetAdjustedSide(repaint = true) {
 363   for (const [input, output] of adjustedSideFields) {
 364     $(input).value = $(input).defaultValue; $(output).textContent = `${$(input).value}%`;
 365   }
 366   if (repaint && !adjustedSide.hidden) drawAdjustedSide(recipe());
 367 }
 368 /** @param {import('./types').OverlayPresentation['reference']} layer */
 369 function drawVideoLayer(targetContext, video, width, height, mirror, layer, alpha = 1, anchor = 0.5) {
 370   if (video.readyState < 2) return;
 371   // 所有試看先使用同一人物裁切，再做各框自己的縮放與鏡像，避免新框退回未跟隨的原畫面。
 372   const crop = sourceCrop(video, following.track(video === reference ? 'reference' : 'self'));
 373   const rect = contain(crop.width, crop.height, width, height);
 374   targetContext.save();
 375   // 平移在鏡像／縮放之外，向右仍是向右；失敗也還原，避免下一幀累加變換。
 376   try {
 377     targetContext.globalAlpha = alpha;
 378     targetContext.translate((width - rect.width) * anchor + rect.width / 2 + layer.x * width, height / 2 + layer.y * height);
 379     targetContext.scale(layer.scale * (mirror ? -1 : 1), layer.scale);
 380     targetContext.drawImage(video, crop.x, crop.y, crop.width, crop.height, -rect.width / 2, -rect.height / 2, rect.width, rect.height);
 381   } finally { targetContext.restore(); }
 382 }
 383 function drawOverlay(value, target = overlay, targetContext = overlayContext, presentation = overlayPresentation()) {
 384   const width = target.width, height = target.height;
 385   targetContext.fillStyle = '#080c0e'; targetContext.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);
 386   // ponytail: 重用兩個已解碼的 video，不新增播放器或時鐘，疊影才能跟共同時間線一起停與走。
 387   // 自己當底圖、老師最後半透明覆蓋，避免不透明自拍把老師完全擋住。
 388   for (const [video, mirror, alpha, layer] of [
 389     [self, value.selfMirror, 1, presentation.self],
 390     [reference, value.referenceMirror, presentation.opacity, presentation.reference],
 391   ]) {
 392     drawVideoLayer(targetContext, video, width, height, mirror, layer, alpha);
 393   }
 394 }
 395 function drawAdjustedSide(value) {
 396   if (adjustedSide.hidden) return;
 397   const target = adjustedSideContext, values = adjustedSideFields.map(([input]) => Number($(input).value) / 100);
 398   // 未調整側沿用原並排像素，避免直式／鏡像另畫的原生色彩捨入，也省去重複影片繪圖。
 399   target.drawImage(canvas, 0, 0);
 400   // 共用已解碼的兩片與疊影變換；各裁在自己的半框，不能把老師拖到右邊蓋住自己。
 401   for (const [video, x, mirror, index] of [[reference, 0, value.referenceMirror, 0], [self, 640, value.selfMirror, 3]]) {
 402     const [scale, dx, dy] = values.slice(index, index + 3);
 403     if (scale === 1 && dx === 0 && dy === 0) continue;
 404     target.save();
 405     try {
 406       target.beginPath(); target.rect(x, 48, 640, 672); target.clip(); target.translate(x, 48);
 407       target.fillStyle = '#080c0e'; target.fillRect(0, 0, 640, 672);
 408       drawVideoLayer(target, video, 640, 672, mirror, { scale, x: dx, y: dy }, 1, x === 0 ? 1 : 0);
 409     } finally { target.restore(); }
 410   }
 411 }
 412 function draw(value, withOverlay = true) {
 413   context.fillStyle = '#080c0e'; context.fillRect(0, 0, 1280, 720);
 414   for (const [video, x, mirror, label] of [[reference, 0, value.referenceMirror, '示範'], [self, 640, value.selfMirror, '我']]) {
 415     if (video.readyState < 2) continue;
 416     const crop = sourceCrop(video, following.track(video === reference ? 'reference' : 'self'));
 417     const rect = contain(crop.width, crop.height, 640, 672);
 418     // 全身等比保留，留白移到外側；只翻影片本身，鏡像後仍須貼著共同中線。
 419     const imageX = x === 0 ? 640 - rect.width : 0;
 420     context.save(); context.translate(x + imageX + (mirror ? rect.width : 0), 48); context.scale(mirror ? -1 : 1, 1);
 421     context.drawImage(video, crop.x, crop.y, crop.width, crop.height, 0, rect.y, rect.width, rect.height); context.restore();
 422     context.fillStyle = '#e8f2ed'; context.font = '24px sans-serif'; context.fillText(label, x + 22, 33);
 423   }
 424   // 合成時只畫輸出來源，額外試看框不增加編碼期間的繪圖負擔。
 425   if (withOverlay) { drawOverlay(value); drawAdjustedSide(value); }
 426 }
 427 async function align(value, elapsedMs, signal, waitForFrame = false) {
 428   reference.pause(); self.pause();
 429   // 慢速音高處理曾讓成檔聲音早於畫面；預覽與輸出共用自然變調，結束後才恢復練習音高。
 430   if ('preservesPitch' in reference) reference.preservesPitch = false;
 431   const target = positions(value, elapsedMs);
 432   await Promise.all([seek(reference, target.referenceMs / 1000, signal, waitForFrame), seek(self, target.selfMs / 1000, signal, waitForFrame)]);
 433   draw(value);
 434 }
 435 async function timeline(value, signal, exporting, captureFrame = () => {}, startOutput = () => {}, outputAudioTime = () => NaN, render = () => draw(value, !exporting)) {
 436   const { durationMs } = comparison(value);
 437   const first = positions(value, 0);
 438   reference.playbackRate = value.rate; self.playbackRate = 1; self.muted = true;
 439   const starting = new AbortController(), cancelStart = () => starting.abort(signal.reason);
 440   signal.addEventListener('abort', cancelStart, { once: true }); if (signal.aborted) cancelStart();
 441   try {
 442     // Safari 先看到真正前進的來源影格，回零後的 seek 才會產生新的目標影格通知。
 443     const frames = exporting ? [presentedFrame(reference, starting.signal, frame => !localAudio || frame.mediaTime > first.referenceMs / 1000 + 0.001), presentedFrame(self, starting.signal, frame => !localAudio || frame.mediaTime > first.selfMs / 1000 + 0.001)] : [];
 444     await Promise.all([...frames, Promise.resolve().then(() => reference.play()), Promise.resolve().then(() => self.play())]);
 445     // 先喚醒解碼與音訊，再回到配方起點；不能靠錄晚一點而省略自拍開頭。
 446     if (exporting) await align(value, 0, starting.signal, localAudio);
 447     // 錄製先接受輸入再啟播；不能等依賴後續影格的 start 通知，否則彼此等待。
 448     abortIfNeeded(signal); await startOutput(); abortIfNeeded(signal);
 449     if (exporting) await Promise.all([reference.play(), self.play()]);
 450   } finally { starting.abort(); signal.removeEventListener('abort', cancelStart); }
 451   const began = performance.now();
 452   let maxDriftMs = 0, maxRenderGapMs = 0, lastFrameAt = began, lastCaptureAt = began - 1000 / 30, lastProgress = began, lastElapsed = 0;
 453   await /** @type {Promise<void>} */ (new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
 454     let frame, finished = false;
 455     const videoFrames = new Map();
 456     const finish = error => {
 457       if (finished) return;
 458       finished = true; cancelAnimationFrame(frame); signal.removeEventListener('abort', aborted);
 459       for (const [video, id] of videoFrames) video.cancelVideoFrameCallback(id);
 460       videoFrames.clear(); error ? reject(error) : resolve();
 461     };
 462     const paint = now => {
 463       if (now - lastCaptureAt >= 1000 / 30 - 1) {
 464         // 每輪只畫選定的輸出佈局，兩種影片沿用同一時鐘與編碼護欄。
 465         render(); captureFrame();
 466         lastCaptureAt = now - ((now - lastCaptureAt) % (1000 / 30));
 467       }
 468     };
 469     const watchVideo = video => {
 470       if (!video.requestVideoFrameCallback || finished) return;
 471       const id = video.requestVideoFrameCallback(() => {
 472         if (finished || signal.aborted) return;
 473         try { paint(performance.now()); watchVideo(video); } catch (error) { finish(error); }
 474       });
 475       videoFrames.set(video, id);
 476     };
 477     // 影片元素捲出可見區時仍須消費實際影格更新；只看 currentTime 不代表像素已換新。
 478     // rAF 保持共同時鐘與輸出節奏，影格通知則讓兩側解碼內容持續進入合成。
 479     watchVideo(reference); watchVideo(self);
 480     const aborted = () => finish(signal.reason);
 481     signal.addEventListener('abort', aborted, { once: true });
 482     const tick = () => {
 483       if (signal.aborted) return aborted();
 484       const elapsedMs = (reference.currentTime * 1000 - first.referenceMs) / value.rate;
 485       // BufferSource 不會跟著影片等 play 或停滯；沿用 G2 的 100ms 限制，超標先拒絕，不能保存錯位影片。
 486       const audioElapsedMs = outputAudioTime();
 487       if (exporting && !Number.isNaN(audioElapsedMs) && Math.abs(audioElapsedMs - elapsedMs) > 100) return finish(new Error('音訊與畫面偏移超過 100ms，未完成合成；請降低負載後重試。'));
 488       if (elapsedMs >= durationMs - 20 || (reference.ended && elapsedMs >= durationMs - 100)) return finish();
 489       if (reference.ended || self.ended || reference.paused || self.paused) return finish(new Error('播放提前中斷，未完成合成。'));
 490       const now = performance.now();
 491       maxRenderGapMs = Math.max(maxRenderGapMs, now - lastFrameAt); lastFrameAt = now;
 492       if (elapsedMs > lastElapsed + 1) { lastProgress = now; lastElapsed = elapsedMs; }
 493       if (now - lastProgress > 1000 || now - began > durationMs + 10_000) return finish(new Error('播放停滯，請降低負載後重試。'));
 494       const driftMs = self.currentTime * 1000 - (first.selfMs + elapsedMs);
 495       maxDriftMs = Math.max(maxDriftMs, Math.abs(driftMs));
 496       // 小偏差用速率輕調，不逐幀 seek；大偏差停止輸出，避免把壞同步標成成功。
 497       self.playbackRate = Math.max(0.95, Math.min(1.05, 1 - driftMs / 2000));
 498       if (exporting && Math.abs(driftMs) > 250 && now - began > 500) return finish(new Error('兩側偏移超過 250ms，請重新校正／降低負載。'));
 499       try { paint(now); } catch (error) { return finish(error); }
 500       $('progress').value = Math.max(0, elapsedMs / durationMs);
 501       $('scrub').value = String(Math.max(0, elapsedMs / 1000));
 502       frame = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
 503     };
 504     frame = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
 505   }));
 506   reference.pause(); self.pause(); self.playbackRate = 1;
 507   $('progress').value = 1;
 508   return { elapsedMs: Math.round(performance.now() - began), observedMaxDriftMs: Math.round(maxDriftMs), maxRenderGapMs: Math.round(maxRenderGapMs) };
 509 }
 510 /** @param {Blob} file @param {HTMLVideoElement} video @param {string} key @param {AbortSignal} [signal] @param {(selection: {file: Blob, duration: number}) => Promise<void>} [beforeCommit] */
 511 async function loadCandidate(file, video, key, signal, beforeCommit = async _selection => {}) {
 512   const previous = urls.get(key), previousTime = video.currentTime, previousRate = video.playbackRate;
 513   let nextUrl;
 514   try {
 515     if (!file.size) throw new Error('這是空檔案，請重新選取。');
 516     nextUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file);
 517     const duration = await load(video, nextUrl, signal);
 518     signal?.throwIfAborted();
 519     // metadata 寫入失敗時也要還原舊影片；不能先換掉畫面再發現配方無法保存。
 520     await beforeCommit({ file, duration });
 521     signal?.throwIfAborted();
 522     // 新片解碼成功才釋放舊 URL；取消或壞檔仍能回到原先位置，不破壞筆記綁定。
 523     if (previous) URL.revokeObjectURL(previous);
 524     urls.set(key, nextUrl);
 525     return duration;
 526   } catch (caught) {
 527     if (nextUrl) {
 528       URL.revokeObjectURL(nextUrl);
 529       if (previous) {
 530         try { const duration = await load(video, previous); await seek(video, Math.min(previousTime, duration / 1000)); }
 531         catch { /* 解碼資源不足仍保留舊 URL 與筆記，供重新選檔，不以重置解決。 */ }
 532         video.playbackRate = previousRate;
 533       } else { video.removeAttribute('src'); video.load(); }
 534     }
 535     throw caught;
 536   }
 537 }
 538 async function chooseFile(input, video, key, signal, beforeCommit = async _selection => {}) {
 539   try {
 540     const selected = input.files?.[0]; if (!selected) return null;
 541     const file = await normalizedVideo(selected);
 542     const duration = await loadCandidate(file, video, key, signal, beforeCommit);
 543     const picker = key === 'reference' ? $('reference-picker') : $('self-picker');
 544     // 解碼與資料提交成功才收合；壞檔、取消或保存失敗要留在原選片位置重試。
 545     const label = key === 'reference' ? $('reference-selection') : $('self-selection');
 546     label.textContent = selected.name; label.dataset.source = video.getAttribute('src') || '';
 547     picker.open = false;
 548     focusControl(picker.querySelector('summary'));
 549     return { file, duration };
 550   } finally { input.value = ''; }
 551 }

 566 $('reference-file').addEventListener('change', () => operation(async signal => {

 568   if (takeBlob && !pendingLinkId && !confirm(comparisonOnly ? '更換示範影片前，請先下載目前的 VS。確定更換？' : '更換示範前，請先下載自拍與 VS。確定改選？')) { $('reference-file').value = ''; return; }

 584   }

 586   $('start').value = '0'; $('end').value = String(selected.duration / 1000);

 590   self.pause(); self.removeAttribute('src'); self.load();
 591   const previous = urls.get('self'); if (previous) URL.revokeObjectURL(previous); urls.delete('self');
 592   currentVideoId = videoId; sessionMedia.set(assetId, selected.file);
 593   $('video-title').value = requireCurrentVideo().title; renderLibrary();

 595 }));
 596 $('self-file').addEventListener('change', () => operation(async signal => {
 597   if (!referenceDurationMs || !currentVideoId) throw new Error('請先載入並連結示範影片。');
 598   if (takeBlob && !pendingTakeId && !confirm(comparisonOnly ? '確定更換練習影片？已匯出的 VS 仍會保留。' : '請先下載上一段自拍。確定載入另一段？')) { $('self-file').value = ''; return; }
 599   releaseCamera();
 600   let savedTake;
 601   const selected = await chooseFile($('self-file'), self, 'self', signal, async candidate => {
 602     if (pendingTakeId) return;
 603     const value = recipe({ rate: 1, offsetMs: 0, selfDurationMs: candidate.duration });
 604     comparison(value);
 605     const assetId = crypto.randomUUID();
 606     // 外部影片直接建立 ready 配對；不經相機 recording／chunks，也不自動寫入 Blob。

 615   if (pendingTakeId) { $('confirm-take').hidden = false; status('請播放預覽自拍原片，再確認連結到待恢復的 take。'); return; }
 616   activeTakeId = savedTake.id; $('rate').value = '1'; $('offset').value = '0';
 617   sessionMedia.set(savedTake.cameraAssetId, selected.file);
 618   status(comparisonOnly ? '兩片已載入。設定同一個動作的起點，再一起預覽或輸出 VS。原片不會上傳。' : '兩片已載入，原片只在本次開啟。各自選起點，或直接共同預覽；需要重開自動取回時再保存副本。'); updateIntersection(); draw(recipe());
 619 }));

 625 $('new-take').addEventListener('click', () => operation(async () => {
 626   if (takeBlob && !confirm('請先下載目前自拍與 VS。準備新一段後，已保存的舊 take 仍會保留，確定繼續？')) { releaseCamera(); status('已取消準備下一段，原自拍仍保留。'); return; }

 628   const old = urls.get('self'); if (old) { URL.revokeObjectURL(old); urls.delete('self'); }
 629   selfDurationMs = 0; takeBlob = null; activeTakeId = null; pendingTakeId = null; $('confirm-take').hidden = true; $('offset').value = '0';

 631 }));
 632 $('camera').addEventListener('click', () => operation(async signal => {
 633   recipe();
 634   existingMode = false;
 635   if (!isSecureContext || !navigator.mediaDevices?.getUserMedia) throw new Error('相機需要受信任的 HTTPS 或此電腦的 localhost。');
 636   releaseCamera(); status('請在瀏覽器中允許相機；不會要求麥克風。');
 637   const facing = $('camera-facing').value;
 638   if (!['user', 'environment'].includes(facing)) throw new Error('請選擇前或後鏡頭。');
 639   // 後鏡頭缺少時明確失敗，不悄悄改用前鏡頭；桌面 webcam 仍可用預設前鏡頭。
 640   const pending = navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ video: { facingMode: facing === 'environment' ? { exact: 'environment' } : { ideal: 'user' }, width: { ideal: 1280 }, height: { ideal: 720 }, frameRate: { ideal: 30 } }, audio: false }).catch(error => { throw signal.aborted ? signal.reason : cameraError(error, facing); });
 641   const stream = await /** @type {Promise<MediaStream>} */ (new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
 642     const cancelled = () => reject(signal.reason);
 643     signal.addEventListener('abort', cancelled, { once: true });
 644     pending.then(value => {
 645       signal.removeEventListener('abort', cancelled);
 646       if (signal.aborted) { value.getTracks().forEach(track => { track.stop(); }); reject(signal.reason); }
 647       else resolve(value);
 648     }, error => { signal.removeEventListener('abort', cancelled); reject(error); });
 649     if (signal.aborted) cancelled();
 650   }));
 651   // 權限對話框不能被 AbortController 關閉；遲到的串流仍要立即停止，不能漏開相機。
 652   if (signal.aborted) { stream.getTracks().forEach(track => { track.stop(); }); abortIfNeeded(signal); }
 653   camera = stream;
 654   const watch = new AbortController(); cameraWatch = watch;
 655   try {
 656     await mediaAction(preview, 'loadeddata', () => {
 657       preview.srcObject = stream; preview.hidden = false; $('preview-frame').hidden = false; self.hidden = true;
 658       return preview.play();
 659     }, signal);
 660     const interrupted = () => { if (camera === stream) { stop(); status('相機畫面已中斷，請重新開啟；已錄到的部分會保留。'); } };
 661     const track = stream.getVideoTracks()[0], width = preview.videoWidth, height = preview.videoHeight;
 662     track.addEventListener('ended', interrupted, { once: true, signal: watch.signal });
 663     track.addEventListener('mute', interrupted, { once: true, signal: watch.signal });
 664     preview.addEventListener('resize', () => { if (camera === stream && (preview.videoWidth !== width || preview.videoHeight !== height)) interrupted(); }, { signal: watch.signal });
 665     const cameraSettings = stream.getVideoTracks()[0].getSettings();
 666     // 裝置 label／deviceId 可能識別個人設備；診斷只留下必要能力數字。
 667     diagnostics.camera = Object.fromEntries(['width', 'height', 'frameRate', 'facingMode'].map(k => [k, cameraSettings[k]]));
 668     mirrors(); status(`相機已就緒${cameraSettings.facingMode ? '' : '（裝置未回報鏡頭方向，請自行確認畫面）'}。請把全身與腳步放在框內，再點倒數錄影。`);
 669   } catch (caught) { const error = caught instanceof Error ? caught : new Error(String(caught)); releaseCamera(); throw error; }
 670 }));
 671 $('capture').addEventListener('click', () => operation(async signal => {
 672   if (!camera) throw new Error('請先開啟相機。');
 673   if (beatDirty) throw new Error('請先保存或取消拍點編輯，再開始錄影。');
 674   const value = recipe(), expectedDurationMs = captureDuration(value);
 675   // 確認框的取消也會關閉預覽，避免使用者退出重錄後相機仍持續開啟。
 676   if (takeBlob && !confirm('請先下載目前自拍與 VS。重錄會建立新 take，已保存的舊段仍會保留，確定繼續？')) { releaseCamera(); status('已取消新錄影，原自拍與已保存的舊段都保留。'); return; }
 677   const info = /** @type {import('./types').CaptureInfo} */ ({ rate: value.rate, interrupted: true }); diagnostics.capture = info;
 678   let recording, take, partialBlob, captureIntegrityError, captureBuffer, captureSource, captureGain, captureAudioAt = NaN, stopRequested = false, chunkQueue = Promise.resolve(), chunkError, persistenceError, chunkIndex = 0;
 679   const muted = reference.muted;
 680   try {
 681     take = await beginTake(value);
 682     // 與恢復操作共用原生鎖，避免另一分頁把尚在錄製的 take 當成中斷接管。
 683     await withTakeLock(take.id, async () => {
 684       try {
 685         await prepareAudio();
 686         if (localAudio) {
 687           // Safari 切回音高保持會回跳；示範維持音高設定並靜音，只從同一原片準備本輪有界聲音。
 688           reference.muted = true;
 689           if ('preservesPitch' in reference && !reference.preservesPitch) reference.preservesPitch = true;
 690           if (!muted && reference.volume > 0) captureBuffer = await decodeLocalAudio(currentReferenceFile, audio, value.startMs / 1000, (value.endMs - value.startMs) / 1000, signal);
 691         }
 692         await seek(reference, value.startMs / 1000, signal);
 693         if (localAudio) {
 694           // 先用非原速喚醒真實影格再回 A；否則原速長段在下一次改速播放仍可能退回關鍵影格。
 695           const warming = new AbortController(), cancelWarm = () => warming.abort(signal.reason);
 696           const timer = setTimeout(() => warming.abort(new Error('示範畫面準備逾時，請重新錄影。')), 2000);
 697           signal.addEventListener('abort', cancelWarm, { once: true });
 698           if (signal.aborted) cancelWarm();
 699           try {
 700             reference.playbackRate = 2;
 701             // 回 A 後可能收到前輪 17 秒的舊通知；位置真的前進且影格符合本輪 2× 的原 100ms 容差才算暖機。
 702             await Promise.all([presentedFrame(reference, warming.signal, frame => frame.mediaTime > value.startMs / 1000 + 0.001 && reference.currentTime > value.startMs / 1000 + 0.001 && Math.abs(frame.mediaTime - reference.currentTime) * 1000 / 2 <= 100), reference.play()]);
 703             reference.pause();
 704             await seek(reference, value.startMs / 1000, warming.signal);
 705           } finally { reference.pause(); warming.abort(); clearTimeout(timer); signal.removeEventListener('abort', cancelWarm); }
 706         }
 707         reference.playbackRate = value.rate;
 708         if (take.beatGrid?.countInBeats) await countIn(take.beatGrid, value.rate, signal);
 709         else for (let n = Number($('countdown').value); n > 0; n--) { status(`${n} 秒後開始，請就定位。`); await wait(1000, signal); }
 710         abortIfNeeded(signal);
 711         info.recorderRequestedMs = Math.round(performance.now());
 712         recording = record(camera, blob => {
 713           chunkQueue = chunkQueue.then(() => saveChunk(take.cameraAssetId, chunkIndex++, blob)).catch(error => {
 714             chunkError = error;
 715             // 舊 take 的遲到寫入失敗不能中止下一個操作；仍保留該 take 的 chunks 供恢復。
 716             if (active?.signal === signal && !signal.aborted) { active.abort(new Error('錄影片段保存失敗，請先下載原片。')); stop(); }
 717           });
 718         }, startedMs => { info.recorderStartedMs = startedMs; });
 719         // 原生也可能提早成功 stop；只有本輪明確要求的停止才可進完整錄影收尾。
 720         recording.done.then(() => {
 721           if (!stopRequested && active?.signal === signal && !signal.aborted) {
 722             captureIntegrityError = new Error('錄影未完成：錄製提早停止。未完成原片可另存；舊自拍與 VS 保留。');
 723             active.abort(captureIntegrityError); stop();
 724           }
 725         }, error => { if (active?.signal === signal && !signal.aborted) { active.abort(error); stop(); } });
 726         if (captureBuffer) {
 727           captureSource = audio.createBufferSource(); captureGain = audio.createGain();
 728           captureSource.buffer = captureBuffer; captureSource.playbackRate.value = value.rate;
 729           captureGain.gain.value = reference.volume;
 730           captureSource.connect(captureGain).connect(audio.destination);
 731         }
 732         const began = performance.now();
 733         await reference.play();
 734         info.referencePlayResolvedMs = Math.round(performance.now()); info.referenceAtPlayMs = Math.round(reference.currentTime * 1000); info.interrupted = false;
 735         if (captureSource) {
 736           // 錄影仍先於示範；等原生啟播接受後才供聲，避免聲音先跑，並沿用既有 10ms 排程而非裝置補償值。
 737           try { captureAudioAt = audio.currentTime + 0.01; captureSource.start(captureAudioAt); }
 738           catch (error) { captureIntegrityError = error instanceof Error ? error : new Error('示範聲音無法啟動。'); throw captureIntegrityError; }
 739         }
 740         status('錄影中。示範到片段終點會停止，也可隨時按停止。');
 741         const checkCaptureAudio = () => {
 742           // 聲音與示範沿同一 A／r 映射；時鐘超過原 100ms 就保留中斷原片，不能把錯位錄影標成功。
 743           // PCM 播完後 context 時鐘仍前進；只界定真正已播出的聲音位置，不改示範或 B 的讀值。
 744           if (captureSource && (audio.state !== 'running' || Math.abs((reference.currentTime * 1000 - value.startMs) / value.rate - Math.min((audio.currentTime - captureAudioAt) * 1000, expectedDurationMs)) > 100)) {
 745             captureIntegrityError = new Error('示範聲音中斷或不同步，請重新錄影。'); throw captureIntegrityError;
 746           }
 747         };
 748         while (reference.currentTime * 1000 < value.endMs && !reference.ended) {
 749           if (performance.now() - began > 62_000) throw new Error('播放／錄影逾時。');
 750           if (reference.paused) throw new Error('示範播放中斷。');
 751           checkCaptureAudio();
 752           await wait(30, signal);
 753           renderBeatPosition();
 754         }
 755         // 原生播放可在主執行緒忙碌時跨過 B；收尾再查一次，避免最後一段聲音中斷被迴圈條件略過。
 756         checkCaptureAudio();
 757       } finally {
 758         reference.pause();
 759         // 先停聲再等編碼／保存尾段；取消或失敗也不能留下播放中的本輪音訊。
 760         if (captureSource) { try { captureSource.stop(); } catch { /* 尚未排程也必須斷線。 */ } captureSource.disconnect(); captureSource.buffer = null; }
 761         captureGain?.disconnect(); captureBuffer = null;
 762         if (recording) {
 763           // 跨過 B 不代表聲音完整；診斷也須保留本輪已確定的完整性錯誤。
 764           info.interrupted = signal.aborted || !!captureIntegrityError || reference.currentTime * 1000 < value.endMs - 100;
 765           try {
 766             // 必須先標記，再 stop／關相機；否則正常完成的 done 也會被誤當提早停止。
 767             stopRequested = true;
 768             const stopped = recording.stop(); releaseCamera();
 769             const blob = await stopped; partialBlob = blob;
 770             info.mime = blob.type;
 771             const durationMs = await loadCandidate(blob, self, 'self', undefined, candidate => {
 772               info.durationMs = candidate.duration;
 773               if (captureIntegrityError) throw captureIntegrityError;
 774               // 沿原 media-feasibility 的 expected−500ms 下限；驗證前不能撤銷舊播放器／下載或清 chunks。
 775               if (!info.interrupted && candidate.duration <= expectedDurationMs - 500) {
 776                 info.interrupted = true;
 777                 captureIntegrityError = new Error('錄影未完成：原片長度不足。未完成原片可另存；舊自拍與 VS 保留。');
 778                 throw captureIntegrityError;
 779               }
 780               // EOF 的聲音時鐘有終點，須另守住原生未四捨五入片長，不能把晚停的長片當成功。
 781               if (!info.interrupted && captureSource && reference.ended && Math.abs(self.duration * 1000 - expectedDurationMs) > 100) {
 782                 info.interrupted = true;
 783                 captureIntegrityError = new Error('錄影未完成：原片長度不符。未完成原片可另存；舊自拍與 VS 保留。');
 784                 throw captureIntegrityError;
 785               }
 786               return Promise.resolve();
 787             });
 788             download('take-download', blob, 'dance-self');
 789             // 新原片解碼成功才切換關聯；倒數取消或壞檔不能把舊畫面綁到新 take。
 790             takeBlob = blob; selfDurationMs = durationMs; activeTakeId = take.id; pendingTakeId = null; $('confirm-take').hidden = true; $('offset').value = '0';
 791             await chunkQueue;
 792             info.interrupted ||= signal.aborted || !!chunkError;
 793             const captureTiming = info.recorderRequestedMs === undefined ? undefined : { recorderRequestedMs: info.recorderRequestedMs, ...Object.fromEntries(['recorderStartedMs', 'referencePlayResolvedMs', 'referenceAtPlayMs'].filter(key => info[key] !== undefined).map(key => [key, info[key]])) };
 794             try { await finalizeTake(take, blob, durationMs, info.interrupted, captureTiming); }
 795             catch (error) { persistenceError = error instanceof Error ? error : new Error('儲存發生錯誤。'); }
 796 
 797           } finally {
 798             releaseCamera();
 799             // 編碼或讀回失敗也要持鎖等尾塊落盤，否則另一分頁會恢復尚未收齊的片段。
 800             await chunkQueue;
 801           }
 802         } else releaseCamera();
 803       }
 804       if (persistenceError) throw persistenceError;
 805       if (chunkError) throw new Error('錄影片段落盤曾失敗；已保留可下載原片，請先下載。');
 806       status('自拍已錄好。先下載原片，再校正與合成。'); updateIntersection();
 807     });
 808   } catch (error) {
 809     // 失敗原片另給下載，不能為了保全新片覆蓋舊自拍連結；chunks 留待明確恢復。
 810     if (partialBlob && takeBlob !== partialBlob) { download('partial-download', partialBlob, 'dance-self-incomplete'); partialAssetId = take?.cameraAssetId; }
 811     if (take) await markStoppedTake(take, recording ? 'interrupted' : 'failed').catch(() => { diagnostics.lastError = '錄影狀態無法更新；原片與 chunks 保留，請下載原片後重開。'; });
 812     if (captureIntegrityError) throw captureIntegrityError;
 813     if (persistenceError) throw new Error(`自拍無法保存在此瀏覽器，請立即下載自拍原片。${persistenceError.message}`);
 814     throw error;
 815   } finally { reference.muted = muted; releaseCamera(); await chunkQueue; }
 816 }));
 817 function updateIntersection() {
 818   try {
 819     const value = recipe(), range = comparison(value);
 820     following.assertCoverage();
 821     $('scrub').max = String(range.durationMs / 1000); $('scrub').value = String(Math.min(Number($('scrub').value), range.durationMs / 1000));
 822     $('intersection').textContent = `共同長度 ${(range.durationMs / 1000).toFixed(2)} 秒；老師 ${((value.startMs + value.rate * range.lowerMs) / 1000).toFixed(3)} 秒／我 ${((range.lowerMs + value.offsetMs) / 1000).toFixed(3)} 秒起跳，示範 ${value.rate}×。${range.durationMs < (value.endMs - value.startMs) / value.rate ? '片尾不足，只輸出兩片都有畫面的區間。' : ''}`;
 823     $('export').disabled = false;
 824     if (outputBlob) $('output-status').textContent = outputRecipe === recipeSignature(value) ? `這支${$('export-layout').value === 'overlay' ? '疊影' : '左右並排'}影片對應目前的比較設定。` : '這是先前設定的 VS，原檔仍保留；若要目前設定的畫面，請重新匯出。';
 825   } catch (caught) { const error = caught instanceof Error ? caught : new Error(String(caught)); $('intersection').textContent = error.message; $('export').disabled = true; }
 826   updateOverlayControls(); updateSourceTimelines();
 827 }

 830 $('overlay-opacity').addEventListener('input', () => {
 831   $('overlay-opacity-value').textContent = `${$('overlay-opacity').value}%`;
 832   if (!overlay.hidden) drawOverlay(recipe());
 833   updateIntersection();
 834 });

 836   $(output).textContent = `${$(input).value}%`;
 837   if (!overlay.hidden) drawOverlay(recipe());
 838   updateIntersection();
 839 });
 840 $('overlay-layout-reset').addEventListener('click', () => { resetOverlayLayout(); updateIntersection(); });
 841 for (const [input, output] of adjustedSideFields) $(input).addEventListener('input', () => {

 843   // 試看不寫配方，也不改原並排、疊影與匯出，方便同頁比較效果。

 848 $('scrub').addEventListener('change', () => operation(async signal => { await align(recipe(), Number($('scrub').value) * 1000, signal); status('已定位兩側畫面。'); }));
 849 // 兩區各有清楚入口，但共用操作鎖與時間映射，不能同時啟動兩套音訊或播放迴圈。
 850 for (const [id, label] of /** @type {const} */ ([['play', '左右比較'], ['overlay-play', '疊影比較']])) $(id).addEventListener('click', () => {
 851   if (active && comparisonPlayback === id) { stop(); return; }
 852   return operation(async signal => {
 853     following.assertCoverage();
 854     const value = recipe(), durationMs = comparison(value).durationMs;
 855     // 沿用共同時間線續播；片尾才回頭，暫停不重設起點或另建播放器。
 856     const startMs = Number($('scrub').value) * 1000;
 857     await saveCurrentRecipe(value, signal); await prepareAudio(); await align(value, startMs >= durationMs - 20 ? 0 : startMs, signal);
 858     comparisonPlayback = id; $('pause-comparison').hidden = false; updateOverlayControls();
 859     status(`共同預覽中：${label}；再按同一按鈕可暫停，之後從原位置繼續。`);
 860     diagnostics.preview = await timeline(value, signal, false);
 861     // 最後一幀可能略早於片尾；完成後明確標到終點，下次按播放才會從頭開始。
 862     $('scrub').value = String(durationMs / 1000); status('預覽結束，可調整對齊或輸出。');
 863   });
 864 });
 865 $('export').addEventListener('click', () => operation(async signal => {
 866   following.assertCoverage();
 867   const value = recipe(), presentation = currentPresentation();
 868   // 操作鎖固定本輪可見度與位置；直接以輸出尺寸繪製，避免放大低解析預覽。
 869   const render = () => presentation ? drawOverlay(value, canvas, context, presentation) : draw(value, false);
 870   let stream, recording, blob, buffer, bufferSource, destination, audioStartedAt;
 871   const muted = reference.muted;
 872   await saveCurrentRecipe(value, signal);
 873   try {
 874     await prepareAudio();
 875     if (localAudio) {
 876       status('正在準備 Safari 相容音訊，可隨時取消；原片保留。');
 877       const first = positions(value, 0);
 878       buffer = await decodeLocalAudio(currentReferenceFile, audio, first.referenceMs / 1000, comparison(value).durationMs * value.rate / 1000, signal);
 879       reference.muted = true;
 880     }
 881     await align(value, 0, signal); abortIfNeeded(signal);
 882     monitor.gain.value = 0;
 883     /** @type {CanvasCaptureMediaStreamTrack | undefined} */
 884     let canvasTrack;
 885     status('正在準備比較畫面，請保持此頁在前景。');
 886     // 兩側真正呈現後才建立輸出流，不裁首尾或硬填音訊延遲來掩蓋啟播差。
 887     const measured = await timeline(value, signal, true, () => canvasTrack?.requestFrame?.(), async () => {
 888       render();
 889       // ponytail: 前景即時合成免轉碼套件；通過 M0 實測後才評估更快的編碼路徑。
 890       // 明確在畫面畫好後請求擷取，避免慢速素材與自動擷取計時器互相錯開而留住舊畫面。
 891       stream = canvas.captureStream(0);
 892       canvasTrack = /** @type {CanvasCaptureMediaStreamTrack} */ (stream.getVideoTracks()[0]);
 893       if (typeof canvasTrack.requestFrame !== 'function') {
 894         stream.getTracks().forEach(track => { track.stop(); });
 895         stream = canvas.captureStream(30); canvasTrack = /** @type {CanvasCaptureMediaStreamTrack} */ (stream.getVideoTracks()[0]);
 896       }
 897       if (localAudio) {
 898         // 每輪自己的目的端與一次性來源都由 finally 釋放；無音軌就保持真正無聲，不猜測壞解碼為靜音。
 899         if (buffer) {
 900           destination = audio.createMediaStreamDestination(); bufferSource = audio.createBufferSource();
 901           bufferSource.buffer = buffer; bufferSource.playbackRate.value = value.rate; bufferSource.connect(destination);
 902           destination.stream.getAudioTracks().forEach(track => { stream.addTrack(track.clone()); });
 903         }
 904         // start 事件可依賴後續影格，不能等它才啟播；start() 已成功回傳才啟動音畫，仍用原偏移護欄拒絕失步。
 905         recording = record(stream, undefined, undefined, { webmOnly: true });
 906         // 10ms 只給原生音訊排程留前置；不是量測後硬填的裝置補償，來源零點與片長都不改。
 907         audioStartedAt = audio.currentTime + 0.01;
 908         bufferSource?.start(audioStartedAt);
 909       } else {
 910         audioDestination.stream.getAudioTracks().forEach(track => { stream.addTrack(track.clone()); });
 911         recording = record(stream);
 912       }
 913       recording.done.catch(error => { if (active?.signal === signal && !signal.aborted) active.abort(error); });
 914       status('合成中，請保持此頁在前景。取消後仍可重試。');
 915     }, () => !bufferSource ? NaN : audio.state === 'running' ? Math.max(0, (audio.currentTime - audioStartedAt) * 1000) : Infinity, render);
 916     blob = await recording.stop();
 917     diagnostics.export = { ...measured, mime: blob.type, bytes: blob.size, requestedWidth: 1280, requestedHeight: 720, recipe: value, ...(presentation ? { presentation } : {}) };
 918     const temporary = URL.createObjectURL(blob), validationVideo = document.createElement('video');
 919     validationVideo.muted = true; validationVideo.playsInline = true;
 920     // 用獨立元素驗證新檔，取消或壞檔不能破壞上一個成功輸出的播放器。
 921     try {
 922       diagnostics.export.decodedDurationMs = await load(validationVideo, temporary, signal);
 923       // 可播放不代表錄完整；沿原 100ms 覆蓋容差，確認共同片長後才能替換舊 VS 並保存新檔。
 924       const rawDurationMs = validationVideo.duration * 1000;
 925       if (!Number.isFinite(rawDurationMs) || Math.abs(rawDurationMs - comparison(value).durationMs) > 100) throw new Error('匯出片長與共同區間不符，未完成合成；原片與前次 VS 保留。');
 926     }
 927     finally { validationVideo.removeAttribute('src'); validationVideo.load(); URL.revokeObjectURL(temporary); }
 928     abortIfNeeded(signal);
 929     outputBlob = blob; outputAssetId = null; outputRecipe = recipeSignature(value); download('output-download', blob, presentation ? 'dance-overlay' : 'dance-vs');
 930     $('output-download').textContent = presentation ? '下載疊影影片' : '下載 VS 影片';
 931     $('output-status').textContent = `這支${$('export-layout').value === 'overlay' ? '疊影' : '左右並排'}影片對應目前的比較設定。`;
 932     output.pause(); output.src = urls.get('output-download'); output.hidden = false;
 933     $('share').disabled = false;
 934     try {
 935       await persistExport(blob, diagnostics.export.decodedDurationMs, presentation);
 936       status('VS 影片已成檔並保存在此瀏覽器。請下載，離開本站後重新播放驗證。');
 937     } catch (caught) { const error = caught instanceof Error ? caught : new Error(String(caught));
 938       if (storageUnconfirmed()) $('output-status').textContent = 'VS 已成檔可下載；這次保存狀態未確認。';
 939       status(storageUnconfirmed() ? `VS 已成檔可下載。${storageUnconfirmedMessage}` : `VS 已成檔但無法保存到此瀏覽器：${error.message} 請立即下載；原片仍保留。`);
 940     }
 941   } finally {
 942     reference.pause(); self.pause();
 943     reference.muted = muted;
 944     // 取消、背景或原生錯誤立即停聲並放開 PCM，不能等編碼收尾才釋放來源。
 945     if (bufferSource) { try { bufferSource.stop(); } catch { /* 尚未排程也需斷線。 */ } bufferSource.disconnect(); bufferSource.buffer = null; }
 946     buffer = null;
 947     destination?.stream.getTracks().forEach(track => { track.stop(); }); destination?.disconnect();
 948     if (recording) await recording.stop().catch(() => {});
 949     stream?.getTracks().forEach(track => { track.stop(); });
 950     // 共用擷取畫布在成檔後還原左右預覽；否則疊影會留在標示左右的框內。
 951     draw(value);
 952   }
 953 }));
 954 $('share').addEventListener('click', async () => {
 955   if (!outputBlob) return;
 956   const file = new File([outputBlob], $('output-download').download, { type: outputBlob.type });
 957   if (!navigator.canShare?.({ files: [file] })) { status('此瀏覽器無法分享影片檔，請改用下載。'); return; }
 958   try { await navigator.share({ files: [file] }); status('已交給系統分享；請到目的端核對保存結果。'); }
 959   catch (caught) { const error = caught instanceof Error ? caught : new Error(String(caught)); status(error.name === 'AbortError' ? '已取消分享，影片仍保留。' : '分享失敗，影片仍可下載或重試。'); }
 960 });

 962 $('stop').addEventListener('click', () => {
 963   const working = !!active; stop();

 965   if (!comparisonOnly) status('已要求停止，正在保留已錄到的資料。');
 966   else if (!working) status('已暫停。');
 967 });

 981 // 更新與離頁共用草稿判斷，不能讓「立即更新」繞過原本的保存保護。
 982 function hasUnsavedWork() {
 983   const video = state?.data.videos.find(value => value.id === currentVideoId);
 984   const metadataDirty = video && /** @type {const} */ ([['title', 'video-title'], ['teacher', 'video-teacher'], ['danceStyle', 'video-style'], ['recordedDate', 'video-date']]).some(([field, id]) => $(id).value !== (video[field] || ''));
 985   return storageUnconfirmed() || active || beatDirty || moveDirty || metadataDirty || !$('marker-form').hidden || $('import-dialog').open || $('media-dialog').open || $('delete-dialog').open || $('share-dialog').open;
 986 }
 987 window.addEventListener('beforeunload', e => { if (hasUnsavedWork()) { e.preventDefault(); e.returnValue = ''; } });

 990 function currentVideo() { return state.data.videos.find(v => v.id === currentVideoId); }
 991 function requireCurrentVideo() { const video = currentVideo(); if (!video) throw new Error('影片筆記已失聯，請重新開啟。'); return video; }
 992 function beatLabel(grid, sourceMs) {
 993   const position = beatPosition(grid, sourceMs);
 994   return position ? `第 ${position.eight} 組八拍 · 第 ${position.beat} 拍` : grid ? '第一拍之前' : '';
 995 }
 996 function renderBeatPosition() {
 997   const grid = state && currentVideo()?.beatGrid;
 998   // 秒數顯示已取整到毫秒，拍號也用同一快照，避免邊界時畫面時間與拍號互相矛盾。
 999   const sourceMs = Math.round(reference.currentTime * 1000);
1000   $('beat-position').textContent = grid && referenceDurationMs ? `原片 ${(sourceMs / 1000).toFixed(3)} 秒 · ${beatLabel(grid, sourceMs)}` : '尚未設定拍點或影片未連結；仍可使用秒數與秒倒數。';
1001 }
1002 function resetBeatEditor() {
1003   const video = state && currentVideo(), grid = video?.beatGrid;
1004   beatEditorVideoId = currentVideoId; beatDirty = false;
1005   $('beat-bpm').value = grid ? String(grid.bpm) : ''; $('beat-first').value = grid ? String(grid.firstBeatMs / 1000) : '';
1006   $('beat-count-in').value = String(grid?.countInBeats || 0);
1007   $('beat-status').textContent = grid ? `已保存：原速 ${grid.bpm} BPM，第一拍 ${grid.firstBeatMs / 1000} 秒；${grid.countInBeats ? `${grid.countInBeats} 拍提示音倒數` : '使用秒倒數'}。` : '未設定拍點；使用秒數與秒倒數。';
1008   renderBeatPosition();
1009 }
1010 for (const id of /** @type {const} */ (['beat-bpm', 'beat-first', 'beat-count-in'])) $(id).addEventListener('input', () => {
1011   beatDirty = true; $('beat-status').textContent = '拍點輸入尚未保存，現有拍點與倒數設定仍保留。';
1012 });
1013 $('beat-set-first').addEventListener('click', () => {
1014   if (active || !referenceDurationMs) return;
1015   $('beat-first').value = reference.currentTime.toFixed(3); beatDirty = true; $('beat-status').textContent = '第一拍位置尚未保存。';
1016 });
1017 $('beat-cancel').addEventListener('click', () => { if (!active) resetBeatEditor(); });
1018 for (const id of /** @type {const} */ (['beat-save', 'beat-clear'])) $(id).addEventListener('click', () => operation(async signal => {
1019   const video = requireCurrentVideo();
1020   if (beatEditorVideoId !== video.id) throw new Error('拍點草稿不屬於目前影片，請取消編輯後重試。');
1021   let grid;
1022   if (id === 'beat-save') {
1023     if (!$('beat-bpm').value.trim() || !$('beat-first').value.trim()) throw new Error('請填寫 BPM 與第一拍原片秒數；不設定時可取消或移除拍點。');
1024     const firstMs = Number($('beat-first').value) * 1000;
1025     if (!Number.isFinite(firstMs) || firstMs < 0 || firstMs >= video.durationMs) throw new Error('第一拍須在原片內，不能是負數或超出片長。');
1026     grid = validateBeatGrid({ bpm: Number($('beat-bpm').value), firstBeatMs: Math.round(firstMs), countInBeats: Number($('beat-count-in').value) }, video.durationMs);
1027   }
1028   // 只提交 metadata；quota、取消或另一分頁衝突時，舊設定與表單草稿都還在。
1029   await saveData(data => { const row = data.videos.find(v => v.id === video.id); if (grid) row.beatGrid = grid; else delete row.beatGrid; row.revision++; }, undefined, signal);
1030   resetBeatEditor(); status(grid ? '拍點已保存，影片與比較配方不變。' : '已移除拍點，仍可使用秒數與秒倒數。');
1031 }));
1032 // UI 入口明確傳自己的 signal；不能借用 active，否則停止會連帶取消錄影保全或別次比較佇列。
1033 async function saveData(change, mediaChange, signal) {
1034   const next = structuredClone(state.data); change(next); validateBackup(next);
1035   state = await writeLibrary(next, state.revision, mediaChange, signal);
1036   $('storage-status').textContent = '已保存在此瀏覽器。JSON 備份及影片請另行下載。';
1037   renderLibrary();
1038 }

1040 function linkedMime(previous, file) {
1041   // 相同容器保留備份已有的 codec 描述；File.type 沒有 codec 不能當成已驗證它變了。
1042   return previous.split(';')[0] === file.type.split(';')[0] ? previous : file.type;
1043 }

1047   focusControl($('restore'));

1049 }

1059 }

1065 function closeMediaDialog(abort = true) {

1070   const url = urls.get('relink-preview'); if (url) URL.revokeObjectURL(url); urls.delete('relink-preview');
