1075 function sessionFile(id) {
1076   if (sessionMedia.has(id)) return sessionMedia.get(id);
1077   if (currentVideoId && currentReferenceFile && state.data.assets.some(a => a.id === id && a.kind === 'reference' && a.videoId === currentVideoId)) return currentReferenceFile;
1078   if (!pendingTakeId && takeBlob && state.data.takes.some(t => t.id === activeTakeId && t.cameraAssetId === id)) return takeBlob;
1079 }
1080 async function getAvailableMedia(id) { return sessionFile(id) || await readMedia(id); }
1081 async function refreshMediaInventory() {
1082   if (!state) return;
1083   const token = ++inventoryToken, data = state.data;
1084   const keys = await readMediaKeys();
1085   if (token !== inventoryToken || data !== state.data) return;
1086   const stored = new Set(keys.filter(key => typeof key === 'string')), session = new Set(data.assets.filter(a => sessionFile(a.id)).map(a => a.id));
1087   const rows = mediaInventory(data, stored, session), labels = { reference: '示範', take: '自己原片', vs: 'VS' };
1088   $('media-summary').textContent = 'JSON 不含影片。共 ' + rows.length + ' 份描述：本機副本 ' + rows.filter(r => r.availability === 'stored').length + '、本次連結 ' + rows.filter(r => r.availability === 'session').length + '、缺片 ' + rows.filter(r => r.availability === 'missing').length + '。本機副本仍須另行備份，瀏覽器可能清除資料。';
1089   const focused = $('media-items').contains(document.activeElement) ? document.activeElement : null;
1090   $('media-items').replaceChildren();
1091   for (const row of rows) {
1092     const li = document.createElement('li'); li.dataset.assetId = row.asset.id; li.dataset.availability = row.availability;
1093     const statusText = row.availability === 'stored' ? '已存瀏覽器副本' : row.availability === 'session' ? '本次已連結，未存瀏覽器副本' : '缺片，需要另選原檔';
1094     li.append(document.createTextNode(labels[row.asset.kind] + ' · ' + row.owner + ' · ' + statusText + (row.interrupted ? '（錄影／中斷片段，需檢查完整性）' : '') + '。描述大小 ' + (row.asset.byteLength / 1048576).toFixed(2) + ' MiB。 '));
1095     if (row.availability === 'missing') li.append(button('選片並預覽重連', () => openMediaRelink(row.asset.id, row.owner), `asset:${row.asset.id}:link`));
1096     else {
1097       li.append(button('另存影片檔', async () => {
1098         const original = await getAvailableMedia(row.asset.id); if (!original) throw new Error('原片已不在，請重新檢查清單並選片。');
1099         // 保留救援下載；無法識別容器時仍可帶走原始 bytes，不改成假的 MP4。
1100         const blob = await normalizedVideo(original).catch(() => original), link = document.createElement('a');
1101         link.href = urlFor('inventory:' + row.asset.id, blob); link.download = mediaFilename(blob, 'dance-' + row.asset.kind); link.click();
1102         status('已交給瀏覽器另存影片；請確認下載完成，JSON 本身不含影片。');
1103       }, `asset:${row.asset.id}:download`));
1104       if (row.availability === 'session') li.append(button('保存瀏覽器副本', async signal => {
1105         const blob = await getAvailableMedia(row.asset.id); if (!blob) throw new Error('原片已不在，請重新連結。');
1106         if (blob.size > 200 * 1024 * 1024) throw new Error('副本初期限 200 MiB；仍可另存影片檔。');
1107         await saveData(data => {
1108           const asset = data.assets.find(a => a.id === row.asset.id); if (!asset) throw new Error('媒體描述已失聯。');
1109           Object.assign(asset, { byteLength: blob.size, mimeType: linkedMime(asset.mimeType, blob), state: 'ready', storageKind: 'indexeddb' });
1110         }, { id: row.asset.id, blob }, signal);
1111         status('已保存副本，仍請另存影片與 JSON。');
1112       }, `asset:${row.asset.id}:save`));
1113       if (row.availability === 'stored') li.append(button('移除此瀏覽器副本…', () => showDelete('copy', row.asset.id), `asset:${row.asset.id}:remove`));
1114     }
1115     $('media-items').append(li);
1116   }
1117   if (focused) focusControl(focused);
1118 }
1119 async function openMediaRelink(assetId, owner) {
1120   if (await getAvailableMedia(assetId)) { await refreshMediaInventory(); status('這支影片已可用，原比較畫面保留。'); return; }
1121   const asset = state.data.assets.find(a => a.id === assetId); if (!asset) throw new Error('媒體描述已失聯，原資料保留。');
1122   releaseCamera(); closeMediaDialog(false);
1123   relinkDraft = { assetId, revision: state.revision, candidate: null, opener: operationTrigger };
1124   $('media-title').textContent = ({ reference: '示範', take: '自己原片', vs: 'VS' })[asset.kind] + ' · ' + owner;
1125   $('relink-file-label').textContent = asset.kind === 'vs' ? '已合成的單一 VS（不取代比較原片）' : '未合成的' + (asset.kind === 'reference' ? '老師原片' : '自己原片') + '（請勿用 VS 代替）';
1126   $('relink-checked').checked = false; $('relink-save').checked = false; $('relink-file').value = '';
1127   $('relink-summary').textContent = '請選片並預覽。備份描述 ' + (asset.byteLength / 1048576).toFixed(2) + ' MiB' + (asset.durationMs ? '、' + (asset.durationMs / 1000).toFixed(3) + ' 秒' : '') + '；本期預設只在這次連結。';
1128   $('media-dialog').showModal(); focusControl($('relink-file'));
1129 }
1130 function button(text, action, focusKey) {
1131   const el = document.createElement('button'); el.type = 'button'; el.textContent = text;
1132   if (focusKey) el.dataset.focusKey = focusKey;
1133   el.addEventListener('click', () => operation(action)); return el;
1134 }
1135 function closeDeleteDialog(abort = true) {
1136   if (!deleteDraft && !$('delete-dialog').open) return;
1137   const opener = deleteDraft?.opener;
1138   if (abort) active?.abort(); deleteDraft = null; $('delete-dialog').close();
1139   focusControl(opener || $('library'));
1140 }
1141 async function showDelete(kind, id) {
1142   if (keepMarkerDraft()) return;
1143   const plan = planDeletion(state.data, kind, id), keys = new Set(await readMediaKeys());
1144   // 新動作刪除也保留開啟來源，確認框結束後才能找回鍵盤焦點。
1145   deleteDraft = { plan, revision: state.revision, opener: operationTrigger };
1146   const names = { copy: '只移除此瀏覽器副本', take: '刪除此練習影片與比較', export: '刪除此 VS 輸出', video: '刪除整份影片筆記', move: '移除動作並解除標記關聯' };
1147   // 按 ID 各查一次，避免大型備份的三層關聯搜尋卡住刪除預覽。
1148   const artifact = kind === 'export' ? state.data.exports.find(e => e.id === id) : undefined;
1149   const relatedRecipe = artifact && state.data.recipes.find(r => r.id === artifact.recipeId);
1150   const targetTake = state.data.takes.find(t => t.id === (kind === 'take' ? id : relatedRecipe?.takeId));
1151   const videoId = kind === 'video' ? id : targetTake?.referenceVideoId || state.data.assets.find(a => a.id === id)?.videoId;
1152   const owner = state.data.videos.find(v => v.id === videoId)?.title;
1153   $('delete-title').textContent = names[kind] + (owner ? ` · ${owner}` : '');
1154   const c = plan.counts;
1155   $('delete-summary').textContent = `將移除：影片筆記 ${c.videos}、標記 ${c.markers}、練習影片 ${c.takes}、配方 ${c.recipes}、VS 輸出 ${c.exports}；本機影片副本 ${plan.mediaIds.filter(id => keys.has(id)).length} 份。${kind === 'copy' ? '保留筆記、配方與媒體描述；本次已開啟的影片可繼續使用，下次需要重連。' : '共用原片仍有其他練習使用時保留；移除的練習同時清理其殘留錄影片段。'}`;
1156   if (kind === 'move') $('delete-summary').textContent = `將移除動作「${state.data.moves.find(m => m.id === id)?.name}」，並解除 ${state.data.markers.filter(m => m.moveId === id).length} 個標記關聯。全部標記、筆記、練習、比較與影片保留。`;
1157   releaseCamera(); $('delete-dialog').showModal(); $('delete-cancel').focus();
1158 }
1159 function clearDeletedPlayer(id, keys) {
1160   const video = $(id); video.pause(); video.removeAttribute('src'); video.load();
1161   for (const key of keys) { const url = urls.get(key); if (url) URL.revokeObjectURL(url); urls.delete(key); }
1162 }
1163 $('delete-video').addEventListener('click', () => operation(() => showDelete('video', $('library').value)));
1164 $('delete-cancel').addEventListener('click', () => { closeDeleteDialog(); status('已取消移除，原資料保留。'); });
1165 $('delete-dialog').addEventListener('cancel', () => { closeDeleteDialog(); status('已取消移除，原資料保留。'); });
1166 $('delete-confirm').addEventListener('click', () => operation(async signal => {
1167   const draft = deleteDraft; if (!draft) return;
1168   if (draft.revision !== state.revision) throw new Error('預覽後資料已改變，請取消並重新檢查。');
1169   const { plan } = draft, retained = plan.kind === 'copy' ? await getAvailableMedia(plan.id) : null;
1170   const remove = async index => {
1171     // 先取得所有受影響 take 的鎖，避免別頁仍錄製／恢復時刪掉來源或分段。
1172     if (index < plan.locks.length) return withTakeLock(plan.locks[index], () => remove(index + 1));
1173     state = await writeLibrary(plan.data, draft.revision, plan.kind === 'copy' ? { id: plan.id } : undefined, signal, plan.kind === 'copy' ? undefined : plan);
1174   };
1175   await remove(0);
1176   if (retained) sessionMedia.set(plan.id, retained);
1177   for (const id of plan.mediaIds) if (plan.kind !== 'copy') {
1178     sessionMedia.delete(id);
1179     const key = `inventory:${id}`, url = urls.get(key); if (url) URL.revokeObjectURL(url); urls.delete(key);
1180   }
1181   // 只在明確刪到這份原片後撤銷救援 URL；取消、無關刪除或一般換片仍須保留。
1182   if (partialAssetId && plan.kind !== 'copy' && plan.mediaIds.includes(partialAssetId)) {
1183     const url = urls.get('partial-download'); if (url) URL.revokeObjectURL(url); urls.delete('partial-download');
1184     $('partial-download').hidden = true; $('partial-download').removeAttribute('href'); partialAssetId = null;
1185   }
1186   if (currentVideoId && !state.data.videos.some(v => v.id === currentVideoId)) {
1187     clearDeletedPlayer('reference', ['reference', 'reference-download']); $('reference-download').hidden = true;
1188     currentVideoId = null; pendingLinkId = null; referenceDurationMs = 0; currentReferenceFile = null; $('confirm-link').hidden = true;
1189     clearMarkerEditor(); $('video-title').value = '';
1190   }
1191   if ((activeTakeId && !state.data.takes.some(t => t.id === activeTakeId)) || (pendingTakeId && !state.data.takes.some(t => t.id === pendingTakeId))) {
1192     clearDeletedPlayer('self', ['self', 'take-download']); $('take-download').hidden = true;
1193     activeTakeId = null; pendingTakeId = null; takeBlob = null; selfDurationMs = 0; $('confirm-take').hidden = true;
1194   }
1195   if (outputAssetId && !state.data.assets.some(a => a.id === outputAssetId)) {
1196     clearDeletedPlayer('output', ['output-download']); $('output-download').hidden = true; output.hidden = true;
1197     outputAssetId = null; outputBlob = null; outputRecipe = undefined; $('share').disabled = true; $('output-status').textContent = '此 VS 已移除，仍可用保留的原片重新合成。';
1198   }
1199   closeDeleteDialog(false); renderLibrary();
1200   status('指定的本站資料已移除；原始檔、原相簿與其他筆記保留。');
1201 }));
1202 function renderLibrary() {
1203   const focused = document.activeElement;
1204   if (beatEditorVideoId !== currentVideoId) resetBeatEditor();
1205   renderBeatPosition();
1206 
1207   refreshMediaInventory().catch(() => { $('media-summary').textContent = '暫時無法檢查媒體，原資料保留；仍可下載 JSON。'; });
1208   const selected = $('library').value;
1209   $('library').replaceChildren(new Option('選擇影片筆記', ''));
1210   const names = videoLabels(state.data.videos);
1211   for (const video of state.data.videos) $('library').add(new Option(names.get(video.id), video.id));
1212   $('library').value = currentVideoId || pendingLinkId || selected;
1213   if (metadataVideoId !== currentVideoId) {
1214     metadataVideoId = currentVideoId;
1215     const video = currentVideo();
1216     $('video-teacher').value = video?.teacher || ''; $('video-style').value = video?.danceStyle || ''; $('video-date').value = video?.recordedDate || '';
1217   }
1218   const moveSelection = $('marker-move').value;
1219   $('marker-move').replaceChildren(new Option('不連結動作', ''));
1220   for (const move of state.data.moves) $('marker-move').add(new Option(`${move.name} · ${move.danceStyle} · ${moveStatuses[move.status]}`, move.id));
1221   $('marker-move').value = moveSelection;
1222   renderSearch();
1223   $('markers').replaceChildren(); $('takes').replaceChildren();
1224   for (const marker of state.data.markers.filter(m => m.videoId === currentVideoId).sort((a, b) => a.startMs - b.startMs)) {
1225     const li = document.createElement('li'), text = document.createElement('p');
1226     const move = state.data.moves.find(m => m.id === marker.moveId), grid = currentVideo()?.beatGrid;
1227     text.textContent = `${(marker.startMs / 1000).toFixed(3)}s${marker.endMs === undefined ? '' : `–${(marker.endMs / 1000).toFixed(3)}s`} · ${marker.title}${move ? ` · ${move.name}（${moveStatuses[move.status]}）` : ''}${grid ? ` · ${beatLabel(grid, marker.startMs)}${marker.endMs === undefined ? '' : ` → ${beatLabel(grid, marker.endMs)}`}` : ''}\n${marker.note}`;
1228 
1229     li.append(text);
1230     const outside = referenceDurationMs > 0 && (marker.endMs ?? marker.startMs) > referenceDurationMs;
1231     if (outside) text.append(document.createTextNode('（超出目前影片，請編輯修復）'));
1232     const jump = button('跳到這裡', async signal => {
1233       if (!referenceDurationMs || (marker.endMs ?? marker.startMs) > referenceDurationMs) throw new Error('請先重連足夠長度的示範，或修復超界標記。');
1234       await seek(reference, marker.startMs / 1000, signal);
1235       if (!selfDurationMs && marker.endMs !== undefined) { $('start').value = String(marker.startMs / 1000); $('end').value = String(marker.endMs / 1000); }
1236       status(marker.endMs === undefined ? '已跳到標記。' : selfDurationMs ? '已跳到標記；已有自拍，錄影片段維持原設定。' : '已跳到標記，並將區間設為下一次練習片段。');
1237     }, `marker:${marker.id}:jump`);
1238     jump.disabled = outside;
1239     li.append(jump, button('編輯', async () => editMarker(marker), `marker:${marker.id}:edit`), button('刪除標記', async signal => {
1240       if (keepMarkerDraft()) return;
1241       if (!confirm('確定刪除這個標記？其他筆記與影片不受影響。')) return;
1242       await saveData(data => { data.markers = data.markers.filter(m => m.id !== marker.id); }, undefined, signal);
1243     }, `marker:${marker.id}:delete`));
1244     $('markers').append(li);
1245   }
1246   for (const take of state.data.takes.filter(t => t.referenceVideoId === currentVideoId)) {
1247     const li = document.createElement('li');
1248     const asset = state.data.assets.find(a => a.id === take.cameraAssetId);
1249     const mediaState = sessionMedia.has(take.cameraAssetId) && asset?.state !== 'ready' ? 'session-only' : asset?.state;
1250     const label = take.state === 'failed' ? '未錄成；可重新錄製' : take.state === 'interrupted' ? mediaState === 'ready' ? '中斷片段已保存' : '中斷待恢復' : mediaState === 'missing' ? '原片待重連' : take.state === 'ready' ? mediaState === 'session-only' ? '當次開啟，原片請另存' : '已保存' : '可能錄製中／待恢復';
1251     li.append(document.createTextNode(`${take.rate}× · ${((take.referenceEndMs - take.referenceStartMs) / take.rate / 1000).toFixed(1)} 秒 · ${label} `));
1252     li.append(button('開啟自拍／恢復', signal => openTake(take, signal), `take:${take.id}:open`), button('刪除此練習與比較…', () => showDelete('take', take.id), `take:${take.id}:delete`));
1253     for (const artifact of state.data.exports.filter(e => state.data.recipes.some(r => r.id === e.recipeId && r.takeId === take.id))) {
1254       const savedRecipe = state.data.recipes.find(r => r.id === artifact.recipeId);
1255       const name = artifact.presentation ? '疊影' : 'VS';
1256       li.append(button(artifact.recipeRevision === savedRecipe?.revision ? `下載已存 ${name}` : `下載舊版 ${name}（設定已變更）`, async () => {
1257         const blob = await getAvailableMedia(artifact.assetId); if (!blob) throw new Error('找不到這個 VS，請用另存的影片或原片重新合成。');
1258         outputBlob = blob; outputAssetId = artifact.assetId; outputRecipe = undefined; download('output-download', blob, artifact.presentation ? 'dance-overlay' : 'dance-vs');
1259         $('output-download').textContent = artifact.presentation ? '下載疊影影片' : '下載 VS 影片';
1260         $('output-status').textContent = `已取回當時保存的${artifact.presentation ? '疊影影片' : 'VS'}；影片對應其原本的比較設定。`;
1261         output.src = urls.get('output-download'); output.hidden = false; $('share').disabled = false;
1262         status('已取回保存的 VS，可用下方連結下載。');
1263       }, `export:${artifact.id}:download`));
1264       li.append(button('刪除此 VS 輸出…', () => showDelete('export', artifact.id), `export:${artifact.id}:delete`));
1265     }
1266     $('takes').append(li);
1267   }
1268   if (focused instanceof HTMLElement && focused.dataset.focusKey && !focused.isConnected) focusControl(focused);
1269 }
1270 function renderSearch() {
1271   const filters = { keyword: $('library-keyword').value, danceStyle: $('library-style').value, teacher: $('library-teacher').value, from: $('library-from').value, to: $('library-to').value, status: $('library-status').value };
1272   const result = searchLibrary(state.data, filters), names = videoLabels(state.data.videos);
1273   $('search-summary').textContent = filters.from && filters.to && filters.from > filters.to ? '起日比迄日晚，請修正日期範圍。' : `找到 ${result.videos.length} 堂課程、${result.moves.length} 個動作。缺片時仍可開啟筆記及重連。`;
1274   $('search-videos').replaceChildren(); $('move-results').replaceChildren();
1275   for (const video of result.videos) {
1276     const li = document.createElement('li');
1277     li.append(document.createTextNode([names.get(video.id), video.danceStyle, video.teacher, video.recordedDate].filter(Boolean).join(' · ') + ' '), button('開啟課程', signal => openVideoNotes(video.id, signal)));
1278     $('search-videos').append(li);
1279   }
1280   for (const { move, markers } of result.moves) {
1281     const li = document.createElement('li'), heading = document.createElement('h4'), clips = document.createElement('ul');
1282     heading.textContent = `${move.name} · ${move.danceStyle} · ${moveStatuses[move.status]}`;
1283     li.append(heading, button('編輯動作／狀態', async () => {
1284       if (keepMoveDraft()) return;
1285       moveEditingId = move.id; $('move-name').value = move.name; $('move-style').value = move.danceStyle; $('move-status').value = move.status; focusControl($('move-name'));
1286     }), button('移除動作…', () => showDelete('move', move.id)));
1287     for (const marker of markers) {
1288       const clip = document.createElement('li'), text = document.createElement('p');
1289       text.textContent = `${names.get(marker.videoId)} · ${(marker.startMs / 1000).toFixed(3)}s${marker.endMs === undefined ? '' : `–${(marker.endMs / 1000).toFixed(3)}s`} · ${marker.title}\n${marker.note}`;
1290       clip.append(text, button('開啟指定片段', async signal => {
1291         if (!await openVideoNotes(marker.videoId, signal)) return;
1292         if (!referenceDurationMs) { status('筆記仍在，示範尚未連結；請選片預覽確認後，再點指定片段。'); return; }
1293         if ((marker.endMs ?? marker.startMs) > referenceDurationMs) throw new Error('這個片段超出目前原片，請重連完整影片或編輯修復。');
1294         await seek(reference, marker.startMs / 1000, signal);
1295         $('start').value = String(marker.startMs / 1000);
1296         $('end').value = String((marker.endMs ?? Math.min(marker.startMs + 15000, referenceDurationMs)) / 1000);
1297         status('已開啟動作片段，可慢速循環練習。');
1298       }), button('編輯片段／關聯', async signal => { if (await openVideoNotes(marker.videoId, signal)) editMarker(marker); }));
1299       clips.append(clip);
1300     }
1301     li.append(clips);
1302     const history = document.createElement('ul'), label = document.createElement('p');
1303     const takes = practiceHistory(state.data, markers);
1304     label.textContent = `${markers.length} 個片段 · ${takes.length} 次涵蓋片段的練習（新到舊）`;
1305     li.append(label, history);
1306     for (const take of takes) {
1307       const item = document.createElement('li'), row = state.data.recipes.find(r => r.takeId === take.id);
1308       const takeState = { ready: '可開啟（原片依保存清單）', interrupted: '中斷待檢查', failed: '未完成', recording: '錄製中／待恢復' };
1309       item.append(document.createTextNode(`${new Date(take.createdAt).toLocaleString()} · ${names.get(take.referenceVideoId)} · ${take.referenceStartMs / 1000}–${take.referenceEndMs / 1000}s · ${take.rate}× · ${takeState[take.state]}${row ? ` · 配方第 ${row.revision} 版／偏移 ${row.offsetMs / 1000}s` : ' · 無比較配方'} `));
1310       if (row) item.append(button('開啟這次比較配方', async signal => {
1311         if (!await openVideoNotes(take.referenceVideoId, signal)) return;
1312         if (!referenceDurationMs) { status('練習歷史與配方仍在，示範尚未連結；請先重連原片再開啟這次比較。'); return; }
1313         await openTake(take, signal);
1314       }));
1315       history.append(item);
1316     }
1317     $('move-results').append(li);
1318   }
1319 }
1320 for (const id of /** @type {const} */ (['library-keyword', 'library-style', 'library-teacher', 'library-from', 'library-to', 'library-status'])) $(id).addEventListener('input', () => { if (state && !active) renderSearch(); });
1321 $('library-reset').addEventListener('click', () => {
1322   for (const id of /** @type {const} */ (['library-keyword', 'library-style', 'library-teacher', 'library-from', 'library-to', 'library-status'])) $(id).value = '';
1323   renderSearch();
1324 });
1325 function keepMoveDraft() {
1326   if (!moveDirty) return false;
1327   // 切換只讀編輯也不能蓋掉輸入；失敗時不清旗標，直到明確保存或取消。
1328   status('動作欄位尚未保存，請先儲存或取消動作編輯。'); focusControl($('move-name')); return true;
1329 }
1330 $('move-form').addEventListener('input', () => { moveDirty = true; });
1331 $('cancel-move').addEventListener('click', () => { moveEditingId = null; moveDirty = false; $('move-form').reset(); focusControl($('move-name')); });
1332 $('move-form').addEventListener('submit', e => {
1333   e.preventDefault(); operation(async signal => {
1334     const name = $('move-name').value.trim(); if (!name) throw new Error('請填寫動作名稱。');
1335     await saveData(data => {
1336       const old = data.moves.find(m => m.id === moveEditingId);
1337       if (moveEditingId && !old) throw new Error('動作已移除，請保留草稿後重新新增。');
1338       const row = { id: old?.id || crypto.randomUUID(), name, danceStyle: $('move-style').value.trim(), status: /** @type {import('./types').Move['status']} */ ($('move-status').value), revision: (old?.revision || 0) + 1 };
1339       if (old) Object.assign(old, row); else data.moves.push(row);
1340     }, undefined, signal);
1341     moveEditingId = null; moveDirty = false; $('move-form').reset(); status('動作與你選擇的練習狀態已保存。');
1342   });
1343 });
1344 async function beginTake(value) {
1345   if (!currentVideoId) throw new Error('請先開啟並連結影片筆記。');
1346   /** @type {import('./types').Take} */
1347   const take = { id: crypto.randomUUID(), referenceVideoId: currentVideoId, cameraAssetId: crypto.randomUUID(), referenceStartMs: value.startMs, referenceEndMs: value.endMs, rate: value.rate, state: 'recording', createdAt: new Date().toISOString(), ...(requireCurrentVideo().beatGrid ? { beatGrid: structuredClone(requireCurrentVideo().beatGrid) } : {}) };
1348   await saveData(data => {
1349     data.assets.push({ id: take.cameraAssetId, kind: 'take', byteLength: 0, mimeType: '', mediaIncluded: false, state: 'copying', storageKind: 'indexeddb' });
1350     data.takes.push(take);
1351     data.recipes.push({ id: crypto.randomUUID(), takeId: take.id, offsetMs: 0, referenceMirror: value.referenceMirror, selfMirror: value.selfMirror, layout: 'side-by-side', audioSource: 'reference', revision: 1 });
1352   });
1353   // 建立工作記錄不代表已換片，倒數取消時必須保留原自拍與配方的關聯。
1354   return take;
1355 }
1356 /** @param {import('./types').Take} take @param {Blob} blob @param {number} durationMs @param {boolean} interrupted @param {import('./types').CaptureTiming} [captureTiming] */
1357 async function finalizeTake(take, blob, durationMs, interrupted = false, captureTiming) {
1358   await saveData(data => {
1359     const asset = data.assets.find(a => a.id === take.cameraAssetId);
1360     if (!asset) throw new Error('自拍媒體記錄失聯；原片仍可下載。');
1361     Object.assign(asset, { byteLength: blob.size, mimeType: blob.type, durationMs, state: 'ready', storageKind: 'indexeddb' });
1362     const savedTake = data.takes.find(t => t.id === take.id);
1363     if (!savedTake) throw new Error('自拍記錄失聯，未刪除任何媒體。');
1364     savedTake.state = interrupted ? 'interrupted' : 'ready';
1365     if (captureTiming) savedTake.captureTiming = captureTiming;
1366   }, { id: take.cameraAssetId, blob });
1367   await clearChunks(take.cameraAssetId).catch(() => { diagnostics.chunkCleanup = 'pending'; });
1368 }
1369 async function markStoppedTake(take, nextState) {
1370   const saved = state.data.takes.find(t => t.id === take.id);
1371   if (!saved || saved.state === 'ready' || saved.state === nextState) return;
1372   await saveData(data => {
1373     const row = data.takes.find(t => t.id === take.id), asset = data.assets.find(a => a.id === take.cameraAssetId);
1374     row.state = nextState;
1375     if (asset && !asset.durationMs) asset.state = nextState;
1376   });
1377 }
1378 function addComparison(data, value, assetId) {
1379   const take = { id: crypto.randomUUID(), referenceVideoId: currentVideoId, cameraAssetId: assetId, referenceStartMs: value.startMs, referenceEndMs: value.endMs, rate: value.rate, state: /** @type {const} */ ('ready'), createdAt: new Date().toISOString() };
1380   data.takes.push(take);
1381   data.recipes.push({ id: crypto.randomUUID(), takeId: take.id, offsetMs: value.offsetMs, referenceMirror: value.referenceMirror, selfMirror: value.selfMirror, layout: 'side-by-side', audioSource: 'reference', revision: 1 });
1382   return take;
1383 }
1384 /** @returns {import('./types').OverlayPresentation} */
1385 function overlayPresentation() {
1386   const values = overlayLayoutFields.map(([input]) => Number($(input).value) / 100);
1387   const layer = offset => ({ scale: values[offset], x: values[offset + 1], y: values[offset + 2] });
1388   return { layout: 'overlay', opacity: Number($('overlay-opacity').value) / 100, reference: layer(0), self: layer(3) };
1389 }
1390 function currentPresentation() { return $('export-layout').value === 'overlay' ? overlayPresentation() : undefined; }
1391 function recipeSignature(value) {
1392   // 相同時間與原片仍可能輸出不同外觀；調整疊影不能誤標舊片為新結果。
1393   return JSON.stringify({ referenceVideoId: currentVideoId, selfAssetId: state.data.takes.find(t => t.id === activeTakeId)?.cameraAssetId, ...value, presentation: currentPresentation(), following: following.signature() });
1394 }
1395 /** @param {import('./types').Comparison} [value] @param {AbortSignal} [signal] */
1396 async function saveCurrentRecipe(value = recipe(), signal) {
1397   // 預覽／匯出可取消自己的準備交易；changeComparison 不傳 signal，仍由獨立佇列完成保存。
1398   if (!activeTakeId) return;
1399   const take = state.data.takes.find(t => t.id === activeTakeId);
1400   if (!take || take.referenceVideoId !== currentVideoId) throw new Error('比較配對已失聯，請重新開啟。');
1401   if (take.referenceStartMs !== value.startMs || take.referenceEndMs !== value.endMs || take.rate !== value.rate) {
1402     let savedTake;
1403     // take 沒有 revision；另建紀錄才能讓已輸出的影片仍保有原本的時間意義。
1404     await saveData(data => { savedTake = addComparison(data, value, take.cameraAssetId); }, undefined, signal);
1405     activeTakeId = savedTake.id; renderLibrary(); return;
1406   }
1407   await saveData(data => {
1408     const row = data.recipes.find(r => r.takeId === activeTakeId);
1409     if (row.offsetMs !== value.offsetMs || row.referenceMirror !== value.referenceMirror || row.selfMirror !== value.selfMirror) {
1410       Object.assign(row, { offsetMs: value.offsetMs, referenceMirror: value.referenceMirror, selfMirror: value.selfMirror, revision: row.revision + 1 });
1411     }
1412   }, undefined, signal);
1413 }
1414 /** @param {Blob} blob @param {number} durationMs @param {import('./types').OverlayPresentation} [presentation] */
1415 async function persistExport(blob, durationMs, presentation) {
1416   const row = state.data.recipes.find(r => r.takeId === activeTakeId), assetId = crypto.randomUUID();
1417   if (!row) throw new Error('找不到比較配方。');
1418   await saveData(data => {
1419     data.assets.push({ id: assetId, kind: 'vs', byteLength: blob.size, mimeType: blob.type, durationMs, mediaIncluded: false, state: 'ready', storageKind: 'indexeddb' });
1420     data.exports.push({ id: crypto.randomUUID(), recipeId: row.id, recipeRevision: row.revision, assetId, mimeType: blob.type, state: 'ready', createdAt: new Date().toISOString(), ...(presentation ? { presentation } : {}) });
1421   }, { id: assetId, blob });
1422   outputAssetId = assetId;
1423 }
1424 function openTake(take, signal) { return withTakeLock(take.id, () => openTakeUnlocked(take, signal)); }
1425 async function openTakeUnlocked(take, signal) {
1426   if (!referenceDurationMs) throw new Error('請先重連示範影片，再開啟自拍。');
1427   if (takeBlob && activeTakeId !== take.id && !confirm('請先下載目前自拍。開啟另一段後，已保存的舊 take 仍保留，確定繼續？')) return;
1428   const row = state.data.recipes.find(r => r.takeId === take.id);
1429   if (!row) throw new Error('找不到此自拍的比較配方，沒有變更原畫面。');
1430   // 已持有同一 take 的恢復鎖，才可把無 owner 的工作狀態改成中斷。
1431   if (take.state === 'recording') await markStoppedTake(take, 'interrupted');
1432   releaseCamera();
1433   let blob = sessionMedia.get(take.cameraAssetId) || await readMedia(take.cameraAssetId);
1434   const recovered = !blob;
1435   if (!blob) {
1436     try { blob = await recoverChunks(take.cameraAssetId); }
1437     catch {
1438       existingMode = true;
1439       $('start').value = String(take.referenceStartMs / 1000); $('end').value = String(take.referenceEndMs / 1000); $('rate').value = String(take.rate);
1440       $('offset').value = String(row.offsetMs / 1000); $('reference-mirror').checked = row.referenceMirror; $('self-mirror').checked = row.selfMirror;
1441       selfDurationMs = 0; activeTakeId = null; takeBlob = null;
1442       self.removeAttribute('src'); self.load();
1443       pendingTakeId = take.id;
1444       status('自拍原片缺少或暫存片段不完整；原有筆記與錄影片段都保留。請用右邊「選擇練習影片」載入另存原片，預覽後確認連結。'); return;
1445     }
1446   }
1447   const durationMs = await loadCandidate(blob, self, 'self', signal, async () => {
1448     if (!recovered) return;
1449     // 中斷 MP4 可能只剩檔頭，loadeddata／尺寸仍可成功；真正呈現影格後才能保存並清 chunks。
1450     if (!self.requestVideoFrameCallback) throw new Error('此瀏覽器無法確認恢復片段的影格；錄影片段與舊原片都保留，請改用受測 Chrome。');
1451     const waiting = new AbortController(), cancel = () => waiting.abort(signal?.reason);
1452     signal?.addEventListener('abort', cancel, { once: true });
1453     if (signal?.aborted) cancel();
1454     try { await Promise.all([presentedFrame(self, waiting.signal), self.play()]); }
1455     finally { self.pause(); waiting.abort(); signal?.removeEventListener('abort', cancel); }
1456     await seek(self, 0, signal);
1457   });
1458   // 原片讀回成功才一起切換配方與下載；壞檔或取消不得把另一段設定寫進舊 take。
1459   download('take-download', blob, 'dance-self'); existingMode = true;
1460   selfDurationMs = durationMs; takeBlob = blob; activeTakeId = take.id;
1461   pendingTakeId = null; $('confirm-take').hidden = true;
1462   $('start').value = String(take.referenceStartMs / 1000); $('end').value = String(take.referenceEndMs / 1000); $('rate').value = String(take.rate);
1463   $('offset').value = String(row.offsetMs / 1000); $('reference-mirror').checked = row.referenceMirror; $('self-mirror').checked = row.selfMirror;
1464   const interrupted = recovered || take.state !== 'ready';
1465   // 只有從 chunks 組回的原片需要落盤；外部 File 與已存中斷片段不能因狀態標籤被自動複製。
1466   if (recovered) await finalizeTake(take, blob, selfDurationMs, true);
1467   updateIntersection(); draw(recipe()); status(interrupted ? '已取回可播放的中斷片段，仍標示中斷；請檢查長度並下載原片。' : '自拍與校正配方已取回。原片可另外下載。');
1468 }
1469 $('confirm-take').addEventListener('click', () => operation(async signal => {
1470   const take = state.data.takes.find(t => t.id === pendingTakeId);
1471   if (!take || take.referenceVideoId !== currentVideoId || !takeBlob || !selfDurationMs) throw new Error('請先載入要重連的自拍原片。');
1472   await withTakeLock(take.id, async () => {
1473     // 人工確認只重新連結，不把外部原片當新錄影自動複製，也不改掉原配方。
1474     if (takeBlob.size !== state.data.assets.find(a => a.id === take.cameraAssetId)?.byteLength && !confirm('此檔與原片大小不同。請確認內容與時間對齊，仍要連結？')) return;
1475     const value = { ...recipe(), selfDurationMs }; comparison(value);
1476     // 人工選回的原片一律只連結；即使原紀錄是中斷，也要另按保存才可建立瀏覽器副本。
1477     sessionMedia.set(take.cameraAssetId, takeBlob); activeTakeId = take.id; pendingTakeId = null; $('confirm-take').hidden = true;
1478     await openTakeUnlocked(take, signal);
1479   });
1480 }));
1481 async function openVideoNotes(id, signal) {
1482   if (keepMarkerDraft()) return;
1483   const video = state.data.videos.find(v => v.id === id); if (!video) throw new Error('請先選一份影片筆記。');
1484   releaseCamera(); reference.pause(); currentVideoId = id; pendingLinkId = null; pendingTakeId = null; $('confirm-take').hidden = true; selfDurationMs = 0; activeTakeId = null; takeBlob = null;
1485   $('video-title').value = video.title; $('confirm-link').hidden = true; clearMarkerEditor();
1486   const asset = state.data.assets.find(a => a.videoId === id && a.kind === 'reference');
1487   const blob = asset && (sessionMedia.get(asset.id) || await readMedia(asset.id));
1488   referenceDurationMs = 0; currentReferenceFile = null;
1489   if (blob) {
1490     referenceDurationMs = await load(reference, urlFor('reference', blob), signal); currentReferenceFile = blob; download('reference-download', blob, 'dance-reference'); updateAudioStatus();
1491     status('已開啟此瀏覽器保存的示範及筆記。');
1492   } else {
1493     const previous = urls.get('reference'); if (previous) URL.revokeObjectURL(previous); urls.delete('reference');
1494     reference.removeAttribute('src'); reference.load(); pendingLinkId = id;
1495     status('筆記仍在，示範尚未連結。請選片、預覽後確認；不會依同名自動配對。');
1496   }
1497   // 重開筆記與新選片使用相同預設；指定 take 仍由 openTakeUnlocked 還原原配方。
1498   // 重連短片必須能練完整的實際媒體；舊筆記片長／超界標記仍保留，缺片時才顯示原筆記範圍。
1499   $('start').value = '0'; $('end').value = String((referenceDurationMs || video.durationMs) / 1000); renderLibrary();
1500   return true;
1501 }

1506   if (!state.data.assets.some(a => a.videoId === linkedId && a.kind === 'reference')) await saveData(data => data.assets.push({ id: crypto.randomUUID(), videoId: linkedId, kind: 'reference', byteLength: currentReferenceFile.size, mimeType: currentReferenceFile.type, durationMs: referenceDurationMs, mediaIncluded: false }), undefined, signal);
1507   currentVideoId = pendingLinkId; pendingLinkId = null; $('confirm-link').hidden = true;
1508   const linkedAsset = state.data.assets.find(a => a.videoId === currentVideoId && a.kind === 'reference');
1509   if (!linkedAsset) throw new Error('找不到示範媒體記錄，請重新開啟筆記。');
1510   sessionMedia.set(linkedAsset.id, currentReferenceFile);
1511   $('video-title').value = requireCurrentVideo().title; renderLibrary();
1512   status(referenceDurationMs < requireCurrentVideo().durationMs ? '影片已連結；此檔較短，超界標記保留並需要修復。' : '影片已連結，筆記與配方保持原有時間。');
1513 }));
1514 $('copy-reference').addEventListener('click', () => operation(async signal => {
1515   if (!currentReferenceFile) throw new Error('請先連結示範影片。');
1516   if (currentReferenceFile.size > 200 * 1024 * 1024) throw new Error('副本初期限制 200 MiB；仍可選片練習並保存筆記。');
1517   const asset = state.data.assets.find(a => a.videoId === currentVideoId && a.kind === 'reference');
1518   if (!asset) throw new Error('示範媒體記錄不存在，請重新連結。');
1519   status('正在保存示範副本，失敗時筆記仍會保留。');
1520   // 停止也要取消副本交易，不能只停播放器後仍在背景寫入。
1521   await saveData(data => { const savedAsset = data.assets.find(a => a.id === asset.id); if (savedAsset) Object.assign(savedAsset, { state: 'ready', storageKind: 'indexeddb', byteLength: currentReferenceFile.size, mimeType: currentReferenceFile.type, durationMs: referenceDurationMs }); }, { id: asset.id, blob: currentReferenceFile }, signal); status('已保存本機示範副本；瀏覽器仍可能清除資料，請自行備份。');
1522 }));

1525   if (!asset) throw new Error('示範媒體記錄不存在，請重新連結。');
1526   await showDelete('copy', asset.id);
1527 }));
1528 $('save-title').addEventListener('click', () => operation(async signal => {

1530   await saveData(data => {

1533     for (const [field, element] of /** @type {const} */ ([['teacher', 'video-teacher'], ['danceStyle', 'video-style'], ['recordedDate', 'video-date']])) {

1539 function clearMarkerEditor() {
1540   // 換片不能沿用舊標記 ID，否則存檔會把原筆記移到另一支影片。
1541   markerEditingId = null; markerVideoId = null; $('marker-form').reset(); $('marker-form').hidden = true;
1542 }
1543 function keepMarkerDraft() {
1544   if (beatDirty) { status('拍點尚未保存，請先保存或取消拍點編輯。'); return true; }
1545   if ($('marker-form').hidden) return false;
1546   // 不讓換片或另一個編輯入口靜默吃掉草稿；使用者可明確保存或取消。
1547   status('這份標記尚未保存，請先儲存或取消編輯。'); return true;
1548 }
1549 function editMarker(marker, clickedMs = Math.round(reference.currentTime * 1000)) {
1550   if (keepMarkerDraft()) return;
1551   markerEditingId = marker?.id;
1552   markerVideoId = currentVideoId;
1553   $('marker-start').value = String((marker?.startMs ?? clickedMs) / 1000);
1554   $('marker-end').value = marker?.endMs === undefined ? '' : String(marker.endMs / 1000);
1555   $('marker-title').value = marker?.title || ''; $('marker-note').value = marker?.note || '';
1556   $('marker-move').value = marker?.moveId || '';
1557   $('marker-form').hidden = false; focusControl($('marker-title'));
1558 }
1559 $('mark').addEventListener('click', () => {
1560   // 在點擊事件內先取時間；後續打字、非同步保存或播放進度不能改寫它。
1561   const clickedMs = Math.round(reference.currentTime * 1000);
1562   if (!active && referenceDurationMs) editMarker(undefined, clickedMs);
1563 });
1564 $('cancel-marker').addEventListener('click', () => { clearMarkerEditor(); focusControl($('mark')); });
1565 $('marker-form').addEventListener('submit', e => {
1566   e.preventDefault(); operation(async signal => {
1567     const video = requireCurrentVideo();
1568     if (markerVideoId !== video.id) throw new Error('這份草稿不屬於目前影片，請取消後重新開啟編輯。');
1569     if (!$('marker-start').value.trim()) throw new Error('請填寫標記起點。');
1570     const startMs = Math.round(Number($('marker-start').value) * 1000), endMs = $('marker-end').value === '' ? undefined : Math.round(Number($('marker-end').value) * 1000);
1571     validateMarkerRange(startMs, endMs, Math.min(video.durationMs, referenceDurationMs || video.durationMs));
1572     const title = $('marker-title').value.trim(); if (!title) throw new Error('請填寫標記標題。');
1573     status('正在保存時間標記…');
1574     /** @type {import('./types').Marker} */
1575     const row = { id: markerEditingId || crypto.randomUUID(), videoId: markerVideoId, startMs, ...(endMs === undefined ? {} : { endMs }), title, note: $('marker-note').value, revision: 1, ...($('marker-move').value ? { moveId: $('marker-move').value } : {}) };
1576     await saveData(data => {
1577       const index = data.markers.findIndex(m => m.id === row.id);
1578       // 即使殘留編輯事件晚到，也不允許跨影片更新既有標記。
1579       if (markerEditingId && data.markers[index]?.videoId !== row.videoId) throw new Error('這份標記已不屬於目前影片，請重新開啟編輯。');
1580       if (index < 0) data.markers.push(row); else { row.revision = data.markers[index].revision + 1; data.markers[index] = row; }
1581     }, undefined, signal);
1582     clearMarkerEditor(); focusControl($('mark')); status('時間標記已保存；點「跳到這裡」即可回看。');
1583   });
1584 });
1585 function downloadBackup() {
1586   $('media-inventory').open = true;
1587   const data = validateBackup({ ...state.data, exportedAt: new Date().toISOString() });
1588   // 匯入上限按實際 UTF-8 bytes 計算；額外縮排會讓接近上限的合法筆記無法還原。
1589   const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(data)], { type: 'application/json' });
1590   const a = document.createElement('a'); a.href = urlFor('backup', blob); a.download = 'notes.dance-stamp.json'; a.click();
1591   status(storageUnconfirmed() ? '已交給瀏覽器另存本頁最後確認的筆記；不含未保存的表單草稿或影片。請確認下載完成，並另存原片與 VS。' : '筆記與配方備份已下載，不含影片。請另存示範、自拍與 VS。');
1592 }
1593 // 救援備份只讀記憶體，刻意繞過已封鎖的操作佇列；不把未確認的交易結果合進備份。
1594 $('backup').addEventListener('click', () => { if (storageUnconfirmed()) downloadBackup(); else operation(async () => downloadBackup()); });

1598 });

1623   // 驗證實際下載的 UTF-8 大小，避免縮排後超限而產生自己也無法匯入的分享檔。
1624   if (file.size > 10 * 1024 * 1024) throw new Error('分享檔超過 10 MiB，請減少選取的片段或正文。');
1625   shareDraft = { file, revision: state.revision }; $('share-content').textContent = content;
1626   const line = text => { const p = document.createElement('p'); p.textContent = text; $('share-readable').append(p); };
1627   // 人先看得懂實際會傳的文字，再提供完整 JSON；兩份預覽都從同一個已篩選物件產生。
1628   for (const video of data.videos) {
1629     line('影片筆記：' + video.title);
1630     if (video.teacher !== undefined) line('老師：' + video.teacher);
1631     if (video.danceStyle !== undefined) line('舞風：' + video.danceStyle);
1632     if (video.recordedDate !== undefined) line('拍攝日期：' + video.recordedDate);
1633     for (const marker of data.markers.filter(m => m.videoId === video.id)) line(`${marker.startMs / 1000}${marker.endMs === undefined ? '' : '–' + marker.endMs / 1000} 秒 · ${marker.title}\n${fields.includes('note') ? marker.note : '（不含筆記正文）'}`);
1634   }
1635   for (const asset of data.assets) if (asset.originalName !== undefined) line('原檔名：' + asset.originalName);
1636   const allowed = [...$('share-fields').querySelectorAll('label')].filter(label => label.querySelector('input')?.checked).map(label => label.textContent);
1637   $('share-summary').textContent = `筆記檔預覽：${data.videos.length} 份影片筆記、${data.markers.length} 個標記、${data.takes.length} 份練習、${data.exports.length} 份 VS 描述；${file.size} bytes，不含影片。額外欄位：${allowed.length ? allowed.join('、') : '無'}。`;
1638   try { $('share-system').hidden = !(typeof navigator.share === 'function' && navigator.canShare?.({ files: [file] })); } catch { $('share-system').hidden = true; }
1639 }));
1640 $('share-download').addEventListener('click', () => operation(async () => {
1641   if (!shareDraft || shareDraft.revision !== state.revision) { invalidateShare(); throw new Error('內容已變更，請重新預覽。'); }
1642   const a = document.createElement('a'); a.href = urlFor('share-notes', shareDraft.file); a.download = shareDraft.file.name; a.click();
1643   $('share-summary').textContent = '已交給瀏覽器下載筆記檔；請確認下載完成，再自行傳送。沒有確認對方接收結果。';
1644 }));
1645 $('share-system').addEventListener('click', async () => {
1646   if (active || shareSending || !shareDraft || shareDraft.revision !== state.revision) return;
1647   const draft = shareDraft;
1648   shareSending = true; updateControls();
1649   try {
1650     // 必須在明確點按當下交給系統，不能先 await 儲存而失去瀏覽器的使用者啟動資格。
1651     await navigator.share({ files: [draft.file] });
1652     if (shareDraft === draft) $('share-summary').textContent = '已交給系統分享；無法確認對方是否下載或匯入。仍可下載此檔。';
1653   } catch (error) {
1654     if (shareDraft === draft) $('share-summary').textContent = error instanceof Error && error.name === 'AbortError' ? '已取消系統分享，原資料保留；仍可下載。' : '系統分享未完成，原資料保留；請改用下載筆記檔。';
1655   } finally { shareSending = false; updateControls(); }
1656 });

1659 $('restore').addEventListener('change', () => operation(async signal => {

'這是另一個來源，將分開匯入，不與本機同名資料混合。' : ''}`;
1669 
1670   const count = kind => incoming.assets.filter(a => a.kind === kind).length;
1671   $('import-media').textContent = `JSON 不含影片：示範 ${count('reference')}、自己 ${count('take')}、VS ${count('vs')} 份描述。確認後請核對保存清單，缺片須另外選取並預覽確認。`;

1673   $('import-confirm').textContent = !plan.added ? '完成，沒有新資料' : plan.conflicts.length ? '另存衝突副本' : '確認匯入';

1675 }));

1679   if (draft.plan.added) { state = await writeLibrary(draft.plan.data, draft.revision, undefined, signal); renderLibrary(); }

1686 $('relink-cancel').addEventListener('click', () => closeMediaDialog());
1687 $('media-dialog').addEventListener('cancel', e => { e.preventDefault(); closeMediaDialog(); });
1688 $('relink-checked').addEventListener('change', updateControls);
1689 $('relink-file').addEventListener('change', () => operation(async signal => {
1690   const selected = $('relink-file').files?.[0]; $('relink-file').value = ''; if (!selected || !relinkDraft) return;
1691   const old = relinkDraft.candidate, video = $('relink-preview');
1692   try {
1693     const file = await normalizedVideo(selected), durationMs = await loadCandidate(file, video, 'relink-preview', signal);
1694     await mediaAction(video, 'timeupdate', () => video.play(), signal);
1695     while (video.currentTime <= 0 && !video.ended) await event(video, 'timeupdate', signal);
1696     video.pause(); await seek(video, 0, signal); signal.throwIfAborted();
1697     if (!relinkDraft) throw new DOMException('已取消', 'AbortError');
1698     $('relink-checked').checked = false;
1699     const asset = state.data.assets.find(a => a.id === relinkDraft.assetId);
1700     if (!asset) throw new Error('媒體描述已失聯，沒有變更原資料。');
1701     // 比較原片必須涵蓋已存 B；裁切候選可以預覽，但不能悄悄綁成完整老師原片。
1702     const blocked = asset.kind === 'reference' && state.data.takes.some(t => t.referenceVideoId === asset.videoId && t.referenceEndMs > durationMs);
1703     relinkDraft.candidate = { file, durationMs, blocked };
1704     const mismatch = asset.byteLength !== file.size || asset.durationMs && asset.durationMs !== durationMs;
1705     $('relink-summary').textContent = `候選 ${(durationMs / 1000).toFixed(3)} 秒、${(file.size / 1048576).toFixed(2)} MiB。${blocked ? '原片短於既有比較結尾，不能連結；請改選完整老師原片。' : mismatch ? '與備份描述不同：請核對是否裁切／轉碼；舊時間與配方保留，超界處需修復。' : '請核對內容與標記位置，不能只憑檔名判斷。'}`;
1706   } catch (error) { if (old && $('media-dialog').open) await loadCandidate(old.file, video, 'relink-preview').catch(() => {}); throw error; }
1707   finally { video.pause(); }
1708 }));
1709 $('relink-confirm').addEventListener('click', () => operation(async signal => {
1710   const draft = relinkDraft, candidate = draft?.candidate;
1711   if (!draft || !candidate || candidate.blocked || !$('relink-checked').checked) throw new Error('請先選片、實播預覽並確認。');
1712   if (state.revision !== draft.revision || await getAvailableMedia(draft.assetId)) throw new Error('此片資料已改變，原片保留；請取消後重新檢查清單。');
1713   const copy = $('relink-save').checked;
1714   if (copy && candidate.file.size > 200 * 1024 * 1024) throw new Error('副本初期限 200 MiB；取消勾選仍可只在本次連結。');
1715   await saveData(data => {
1716     const asset = data.assets.find(a => a.id === draft.assetId); if (!asset) throw new Error('媒體描述已失聯，原資料保留。');
1717     Object.assign(asset, { byteLength: candidate.file.size, mimeType: linkedMime(asset.mimeType, candidate.file), durationMs: candidate.durationMs, state: copy ? 'ready' : 'session-only', storageKind: copy ? 'indexeddb' : 'session' });
1718     if (asset.kind === 'vs') for (const artifact of data.exports.filter(e => e.assetId === asset.id)) artifact.mimeType = linkedMime(artifact.mimeType, candidate.file);
1719   }, copy ? { id: draft.assetId, blob: candidate.file } : undefined, signal);
1720   sessionMedia.set(draft.assetId, candidate.file); closeMediaDialog(false); await refreshMediaInventory();
1721   status('影片已確認連結，原比較畫面與配方保留。可另存影片，或從對應筆記開啟。');
1722 }));
1723 function clearPracticeWatch() {
1724   practiceToken++;
1725   // frame ID 只屬於原 API；暫停時也清理，避免沒有新影格而永遠掛住的 callback。
1726   if (practiceFrame !== undefined) {
1727     if (practiceFrameUsesVideo) reference.cancelVideoFrameCallback(practiceFrame);
1728     else cancelAnimationFrame(practiceFrame);
1729   }
1730   practiceFrame = undefined;
1731 }
1732 function stopPractice() {
1733   looping = false; practiceStarting = false; clearPracticeWatch(); practiceController?.abort(); practiceController = null; practiceSeek = null;
1734   reference.pause(); $('loop').textContent = '開始 A–B 循環'; updateStopControl();
1735 }
1736 function applyPracticeSettings() {
1737   const rate = Number($('practice-rate').value);
1738   // 媒體操作暫用錄影速度，結束後還給練習選項；load 與原生播放也沿用同一預設。
1739   reference.defaultPlaybackRate = rate; reference.playbackRate = rate;
1740   if ('preservesPitch' in reference) {
1741     reference.preservesPitch = true;
1742     $('pitch-status').textContent = '已啟用瀏覽器音高保持；實際聽感依瀏覽器而定。';
1743   } else $('pitch-status').textContent = '此瀏覽器未提供音高保持，變速時聲音可能變調。';
1744 }
1745 function drawPractice() {
1746   renderBeatPosition();
1747   if (!$('practice-mirror').checked || reference.readyState < 2) return;
1748   const c = $('practice-canvas'), ctx = drawingContext(c), rect = contain(reference.videoWidth, reference.videoHeight, c.width, c.height);
1749   ctx.fillStyle = '#000'; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, c.width, c.height); ctx.save(); ctx.translate(c.width, 0); ctx.scale(-1, 1); ctx.drawImage(reference, rect.x, rect.y, rect.width, rect.height); ctx.restore();
1750 }
1751 async function rewindPractice(seconds, signal) {
1752   // tick 與 ended 可能同時抵達尾端；共用進行中的 seek，不排入第二次回捲。
1753   if (practiceSeek) return practiceSeek;
1754   // 取消等待不會取消原生 seek；新 owner 先等舊跳轉結束，避免重啟時重疊。
1755   const pending = (async () => {
1756     if (reference.seeking) await mediaAction(reference, 'seeked', () => {}, signal);
1757     await seek(reference, seconds, signal);
1758   })(); practiceSeek = pending;
1759   try { await pending; } finally { if (practiceSeek === pending) practiceSeek = null; }
1760 }
1761 async function playLoopFromA(controller) {
1762   practiceStarting = true; updateStopControl();
1763   try {
1764     const { a } = practiceRange(); reference.pause();
1765     await rewindPractice(a, controller.signal); controller.signal.throwIfAborted();
1766     applyPracticeSettings();
1767     // 開始就持有可取消的 owner；晚到的 seek／play 不能在使用者停止後重新啟動。
1768     await mediaAction(reference, 'playing', () => reference.play(), controller.signal);
1769     controller.signal.throwIfAborted();
1770     if (controller === practiceController && !reference.paused) watchPractice();
1771   } finally { if (controller === practiceController) practiceStarting = false; }
1772 }
1773 function practiceRange() {
1774   const a = Number($('start').value), b = Number($('end').value);
1775   // 與配方同以整數毫秒比較；65001ms 的秒數往返誤差不能誤拒完整片段。
1776   if (!$('start').value || !$('end').value || !referenceDurationMs || !Number.isFinite(a) || !Number.isFinite(b) || a < 0 || b <= a || Math.round(b * 1000) > referenceDurationMs) throw new Error('請填合法的 A–B 起訖；不會自動交換。');
1777   return { a, b };
1778 }
1779 function watchPractice() {
1780   if (active) return;
1781   clearPracticeWatch(); practiceController ??= new AbortController();
1782   const token = practiceToken, owner = practiceController;
1783   const tick = async () => {
1784     if (token !== practiceToken) return;
1785     practiceFrame = undefined;
1786     if (active || reference.paused || document.hidden) return;
1787     drawPractice();
1788     if (looping && !reference.seeking) {
1789       try {
1790         const { a, b } = practiceRange();
1791         // 循環中改 A 也要重新定位，不能繼續播放新起點之前的畫面。
1792         if (reference.currentTime >= b || reference.currentTime < a - 0.15) await rewindPractice(a, owner.signal);
1793       } catch (caught) {
1794         if (owner !== practiceController || token !== practiceToken) return;
1795         const error = caught instanceof Error ? caught : new Error(String(caught)); if (error.name !== 'AbortError') status(error.message); stopPractice(); return;
1796       }
1797     }
1798     if (token === practiceToken && owner === practiceController && !reference.paused) schedule();
1799   };
1800   const schedule = () => {
1801     practiceFrameUsesVideo = !!reference.requestVideoFrameCallback;
1802     practiceFrame = practiceFrameUsesVideo ? reference.requestVideoFrameCallback(tick) : requestAnimationFrame(tick);
1803   };
1804   schedule();
1805 }
1806 $('practice-rate').addEventListener('change', () => { if (!active) applyPracticeSettings(); });
1807 $('practice-mirror').addEventListener('change', () => { $('practice-canvas').hidden = !$('practice-mirror').checked; if (!active) drawPractice(); });
1808 reference.addEventListener('seeked', () => { if (!active) drawPractice(); });
1809 reference.addEventListener('timeupdate', renderBeatPosition);
1810 reference.addEventListener('seeking', () => {
1811   if (!active && looping && !practiceSeek) { stopPractice(); status('已手動跳轉並停止 A–B；重新開始會從 A 校正。'); }
1812 });
1813 $('set-a').addEventListener('click', () => { if (!active && !selfDurationMs) $('start').value = reference.currentTime.toFixed(3); else status('錄後來源片段固定，請先重新選片再改 A。'); });
1814 $('set-b').addEventListener('click', () => { if (!active && !selfDurationMs) $('end').value = reference.currentTime.toFixed(3); else status('錄後來源片段固定，請先重新選片再改 B。'); });
1815 $('loop').addEventListener('click', async () => {
1816   if (active) return;
1817   if (looping) { stopPractice(); return; }
1818   clearPracticeWatch(); practiceController?.abort();
1819   const controller = new AbortController(); practiceController = controller;
1820   looping = true; $('loop').textContent = '停止 A–B 循環';
1821   try { await playLoopFromA(controller); if (controller === practiceController) status('A–B 循環練習中。'); }
1822   catch (caught) {
1823     if (controller !== practiceController) return;
1824     const error = caught instanceof Error ? caught : new Error(String(caught)); stopPractice(); if (error.name !== 'AbortError') status(error.message);
1825   }
1826 });
1827 for (const video of [reference, self, output]) video.addEventListener('error', () => {
1828   if (active) return;
1829   stop(); status('播放中斷，這個影片內容或格式無法繼續解碼。筆記與原片保留，可重新選片。');
1830 });
1831 reference.addEventListener('play', async () => {
1832   if (active) return;
1833   try {
1834     applyPracticeSettings();
1835     // 接過 Web Audio 的 video 不會自動回到原生出聲路徑；一般播放與循環都需恢復同一 context。
1836     if (audio) await prepareAudio();
1837     if (!active && !reference.paused) watchPractice();
1838   } catch (caught) { const error = caught instanceof Error ? caught : new Error(String(caught)); reference.pause(); status(error.message); }
1839 });
1840 reference.addEventListener('pause', () => {
1841   if (!active && reference.paused && !practiceStarting) {
1842     if (reference.ended && looping) clearPracticeWatch(); else stopPractice();
1843   }
1844   // 練習暫停也要釋放音訊運算；合成中的暫停由 operation 統一清理。
1845   if (!active && reference.paused && audio?.state === 'running') audio.suspend().catch(() => {});
1846 });
1847 reference.addEventListener('ended', async () => {
1848   const owner = practiceController;
1849   if (looping && !active && owner) {
1850     try { await playLoopFromA(owner); }
1851     catch { if (owner === practiceController) stopPractice(); }
1852   }
1853 });
1854 // ended 只保留上方可取消的循環 owner；重複掛舊 handler 會在停止後重新播放。
1855 function changeComparison(change) {
1856   if (storageUnconfirmed()) { showStorageRecovery(); return; }
1857   if (active || pendingTakeId) return;
1858   change(); $('scrub').value = '0'; updateIntersection();
1859   if (!activeTakeId) return;
1860   let value;
1861   try { value = recipe(); } catch { return; }
1862   const version = ++comparisonSaveVersion;
1863   // blur 先於按鈕 click；保存時若停用按鈕，使用者剛按的播放／匯出就會被吃掉。
1864   // 以同一佇列保存，媒體操作進來時等待佇列，既不漏點擊也不讓 revision 互撞。
1865   $('compare').setAttribute('aria-busy', 'true'); updateSourceTimelines(); status('正在保存比較設定…');
1866   const saving = comparisonSave.then(async () => {
1867     await saveCurrentRecipe(value);
1868     if (version === comparisonSaveVersion && !active) await align(value, 0);
1869   });
1870   // 佇列只代表前次工作已結束；單次錯誤另行回報，不能阻擋備份、另存或恢復。
1871   // 匯出／預覽仍會重新保存目前配方，實際 quota／revision 檢查沒有被略過。
1872   comparisonSave = saving.catch(() => {});
1873   saving.then(() => {
1874     if (version !== comparisonSaveVersion) return;
1875     $('compare').setAttribute('aria-busy', 'false'); updateIntersection();
1876     if (!active) status('比較設定已保存；原片與舊輸出仍保留。');
1877   }, error => {
1878     if (version !== comparisonSaveVersion) return;
1879     $('compare').setAttribute('aria-busy', 'false'); status(`${error.message} 原片與目前輸入仍保留。`);
1880     updateControls();
1881   });
1882 }
1883 for (const id of /** @type {const} */ (['start', 'end', 'rate', 'offset', 'reference-mirror', 'self-mirror'])) $(id).addEventListener('change', () => changeComparison(() => {}));
1884 for (const [id, target, video] of /** @type {const} */ ([['reference-start', 'start', reference], ['reference-end', 'end', reference], ['self-start', 'offset', self]])) $(id).addEventListener('click', () => {
1885   const time = video.currentTime.toFixed(3);
1886   changeComparison(() => { $(target).value = time; });
1887 });
1888 for (const el of $('offset-controls').querySelectorAll('button')) el.addEventListener('click', () => changeComparison(() => {
1889   $('offset').value = String((Math.round(Number($('offset').value) * 1000) + Number(el.dataset.offset)) / 1000);
1890 }));
1891 $('copy-self').addEventListener('click', () => operation(async signal => {
1892   const take = state.data.takes.find(t => t.id === activeTakeId), blob = takeBlob;
1893   if (!take || !blob) throw new Error('請先選取並連結我的影片。');
1894   if (blob.size > 200 * 1024 * 1024) throw new Error('副本初期限制 200 MiB；仍可當次比較，原片請自行保存。');
1895   await saveData(data => {
1896     const asset = data.assets.find(a => a.id === take.cameraAssetId);
1897     Object.assign(asset, { state: 'ready', storageKind: 'indexeddb', byteLength: blob.size, mimeType: blob.type, durationMs: selfDurationMs });
1898   }, { id: take.cameraAssetId, blob }, signal);
1899   status('已保存我的影片副本；JSON 仍不含影片，請另行下載備份。');
1900 }));
1901 function updateAudioStatus() {
1902   // 有些瀏覽器不提供音軌資訊；未知時不冒稱有聲，最後仍須重播輸出確認。
1903   const video = /** @type {HTMLVideoElement & {audioTracks?: {length: number}, webkitAudioDecodedByteCount?: number, mozHasAudio?: boolean}} */ (reference);
1904   const hasAudio = video.audioTracks ? video.audioTracks.length > 0 : (video.webkitAudioDecodedByteCount ?? 0) > 0 ? true : video.mozHasAudio;

web/offline.js — 100% lines
   1 /* 檔案用途：顯示真實離線完整性、容量及等待更新；不接觸私人媒體。所在層：Web 能力。主要關聯：sw.js、main.js、TECHNICAL.md。 */
   2 export function storageEstimateText(estimate) {
   3   const { usage, quota } = estimate || {};
   4   if (!Number.isFinite(usage) || !Number.isFinite(quota) || usage < 0 || quota <= 0) return '此瀏覽器未提供容量估計。';
   5   const mib = value => (value / 1048576).toFixed(1);
   6   return `此網站估計已用 ${mib(usage)} MiB／額度 ${mib(quota)} MiB，估計可用 ${mib(Math.max(0, quota - usage))} MiB；不是裝置剩餘空間或寫入保證。`;
   7 }
   8 /** @param {ServiceWorker} worker */
   9 function offlineStatus(worker) {
  10   return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
  11     const channel = new MessageChannel();
  12     const close = () => { clearTimeout(timer); channel.port1.close(); channel.port2.close(); };
  13     const timer = setTimeout(() => { close(); reject(new Error('離線檢查逾時。')); }, 5000);
  14     channel.port1.onmessage = e => { close(); resolve(e.data); };
  15     try { worker.postMessage({ type: 'CHECK_OFFLINE' }, [channel.port2]); }
  16     catch (error) { close(); reject(error); }
  17   });
  18 }
  19 /** @param {ServiceWorker} candidate */
  20 function activateUpdate(candidate) {
  21   return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
  22     const channel = new MessageChannel();
  23     const wasControlled = !!navigator.serviceWorker.controller;
  24     const close = () => { clearTimeout(timer); channel.port1.close(); channel.port2.close(); navigator.serviceWorker.removeEventListener('controllerchange', changed); candidate.removeEventListener('statechange', changed); };
  25     // 首次開站可能尚未受 SW 控制；此時候選真正 activated 即可重載，不必等不會發生的接管事件。
  26     const changed = () => { if (navigator.serviceWorker.controller === candidate || (!wasControlled && candidate.state === 'activated')) { close(); resolve(undefined); } };
  27     const timer = setTimeout(() => { close(); reject(new Error('更新未完成，請稍後再試；目前工作保留。')); }, 10000);
  28     navigator.serviceWorker.addEventListener('controllerchange', changed);
  29     candidate.addEventListener('statechange', changed);
  30     channel.port1.onmessage = event => { if (event.data?.error) { close(); reject(new Error(event.data.error)); } };
  31     try { candidate.postMessage({ type: 'ACTIVATE_UPDATE' }, [channel.port2]); }
  32     catch (error) { close(); reject(error); }
  33   });
  34 }
  35 /** @param {{offline: HTMLElement, capacity: HTMLElement, persistence: HTMLElement, updates: HTMLElement, updateNotice: HTMLElement, applyUpdate: HTMLButtonElement, canUpdate: () => boolean, refresh: HTMLButtonElement, persist: HTMLButtonElement, checkUpdate: HTMLButtonElement}} ui */
  36 export async function startOffline(ui) {
  37   let registration;
  38   let checkingUpdate = false;
  39   let applyingUpdate = false;
  40   let refreshSequence = 0;
  41   const refreshCapacity = async () => {
  42     // 同步拒絕也視為能力未知，不能阻止筆記開啟或 SW 註冊。
  43     const [estimate, persisted] = await Promise.allSettled([Promise.resolve().then(() => navigator.storage?.estimate?.()), Promise.resolve().then(() => navigator.storage?.persisted?.())]);
  44     ui.capacity.textContent = storageEstimateText(estimate.status === 'fulfilled' ? estimate.value : undefined);
  45     ui.persistence.textContent = persisted.status === 'fulfilled' && persisted.value === true ? '瀏覽器已允許持久保存；手動清除網站資料仍會刪除，請另存 JSON 及影片。' : persisted.status === 'fulfilled' && persisted.value === false ? '未獲持久保存，瀏覽器可能自動清除；請另存 JSON 及影片。' : '無法確認持久保存狀態；請另存 JSON 及影片。';
  46   };
  47   const updateText = () => {
  48     ui.updates.textContent = registration?.waiting ? '新版已備妥。先保存工作及原片，再按「立即更新」同意重新載入；本頁未保存的原片需重新選取。不按就繼續使用目前版本，不會自動重載。' : '有新版會自動提示，按「立即更新」同意後才重新載入，不會中斷錄影或匯出。';
  49     // 提示不使用阻塞錄影的原生 alert；按鈕才代表同意換版，兩處說明仍共用 waiting 訊號。
  50     ui.updateNotice.hidden = !registration?.waiting;
  51     ui.updateNotice.textContent = registration?.waiting ? ui.updates.textContent : '';
  52     ui.applyUpdate.hidden = !registration?.waiting;
  53   };
  54   ui.applyUpdate.addEventListener('click', async () => {
  55     if (applyingUpdate || !registration?.waiting) return;
  56     if (!ui.canUpdate()) { ui.updateNotice.textContent = '請先停止播放／相機、完成錄影或匯出，並保存或取消未完成編輯，再按「立即更新」。目前工作保留。'; return; }
  57     applyingUpdate = true; ui.applyUpdate.disabled = true;
  58     // 查詢其他分頁與啟用之間暫停本頁互動，避免同意後又開始新錄影或輸入草稿。
  59     const wasInert = document.body.inert; document.body.inert = true;
  60     ui.updateNotice.textContent = '正在載入新版，請稍候…';
  61     try { await activateUpdate(registration.waiting); location.reload(); }
  62     catch (error) { ui.updateNotice.textContent = error instanceof Error ? error.message : '更新未完成；目前工作保留，可再試。'; }
  63     finally { document.body.inert = wasInert; applyingUpdate = false; ui.applyUpdate.disabled = false; }
  64   });
  65   const refresh = async () => {
  66     // 啟動、worker 事件與手動重查會重疊；過期回覆或逾時不可蓋掉最後一輪的離線結論。
  67     const sequence = ++refreshSequence;
  68     await refreshCapacity();
  69     if (sequence !== refreshSequence) return;
  70     try {
  71       const worker = navigator.serviceWorker?.controller || registration?.active;
  72       if (!worker) { ui.offline.textContent = '離線資源尚未備妥；首次請保持連線，稍後重新檢查。'; return; }
  73       const result = await offlineStatus(worker);
  74       if (sequence !== refreshSequence) return;
  75       ui.offline.textContent = result.complete === true ? `已檢查應用資源完整（${result.version}），可離線重開；影片須看保存清單或重新選片。${navigator.serviceWorker.controller ? '' : '首次請先重開一次，確認本頁已受離線服務控制。'}` : '應用離線資源不完整，請保持連線；已存筆記與影片未刪除。';
  76     } catch {
  77       if (sequence !== refreshSequence) return;
  78       ui.offline.textContent = '暫時無法確認離線資源，請保持連線；原資料保留。';
  79     }
  80     updateText();
  81   };
  82   ui.refresh.addEventListener('click', () => { refresh().catch(() => {}); });
  83   ui.persist.disabled = !navigator.storage?.persist;
  84   ui.persist.addEventListener('click', async () => {
  85     ui.persist.disabled = true;
  86     try {
  87       // 僅使用者點按才請求，明確報告拒絕／例外，不把 API 存在當成已允許。
  88       const granted = await navigator.storage.persist();
  89       await refreshCapacity();
  90       ui.persistence.textContent = granted ? '瀏覽器已允許持久保存；仍請另存 JSON 及影片，手動清除不受保護。' : '瀏覽器未允許持久保存；仍可使用，請另存 JSON 及影片。';
  91     } catch { ui.persistence.textContent = '持久保存請求失敗；原資料保留，仍請另存 JSON 及影片。'; }
  92     finally { ui.persist.disabled = false; }
  93   });
  94   const checkForUpdate = async () => {
  95     // 回前景與恢復連線可能接連發生；同一輪共用檢查，失敗後仍可重試。
  96     if (!registration || checkingUpdate) return;
  97     checkingUpdate = true;
  98     try { await registration.update(); updateText(); }
  99     catch { ui.updates.textContent = '目前無法檢查新版；現有版本與資料保留，可稍後再試。'; }
 100     finally { checkingUpdate = false; }
 101   };
 102   ui.checkUpdate.addEventListener('click', checkForUpdate);
 103   await refreshCapacity();
 104   // 本機開發由 watcher 更新；若再註冊正式 SW，舊快取會把修改擋在頁面之外。
 105   if (document.documentElement?.dataset.devReload === 'true' && ['127.0.0.1', 'localhost', '[::1]'].includes(location.hostname)) {
 106     ui.offline.textContent = '本機即時更新模式暫停離線快取；筆記與影片副本仍保留。';
 107     ui.checkUpdate.disabled = true; return;
 108   }
 109   if (!('serviceWorker' in navigator)) { ui.offline.textContent = '此瀏覽器不支援應用離線重開；本機選片仍可使用。'; ui.checkUpdate.disabled = true; return; }
 110   try {
 111     registration = await navigator.serviceWorker.register('/sw.js', { updateViaCache: 'none' });
 112     const watch = worker => { worker?.addEventListener('statechange', () => { refresh().catch(() => {}); }); };
 113     watch(registration.installing);
 114     registration.addEventListener('updatefound', () => { watch(registration.installing); });
 115     navigator.serviceWorker.addEventListener('controllerchange', () => { refresh().catch(() => {}); });
 116     const checkWhenVisible = () => {
 117       // 手機背景及離線時不喚醒網路；新版仍走原本 waiting，不能為更新打斷錄影或草稿。
 118       if (navigator.onLine && document.visibilityState === 'visible') void checkForUpdate();
 119     };
 120     document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', checkWhenVisible);
 121     window.addEventListener('online', checkWhenVisible);
 122     // 從上一頁快取還原不一定改變可見狀態，pageshow 要另查，不能只等重新開站。
 123     window.addEventListener('pageshow', checkWhenVisible);
 124     window.setInterval(checkWhenVisible, 15 * 60 * 1000);
 125     await refresh();
 126     // 先完成初始狀態顯示，免得隨後的 refresh 把檢查失敗訊息蓋掉。
 127     checkWhenVisible();
 128   } catch { ui.offline.textContent = '離線資源未備妥，請保持連線；本機資料未刪除。'; }
 129 }

web/person-worker.js — 100% lines
   1 /* 檔案用途：在本機背景執行人物偵測，避免阻塞停止按鈕。所在層：專用 Web Worker。主要關聯：follow-ui.js、scripts/web-assets.ts。 */
   2 {
   3   const scope = /** @type {any} */ (globalThis);
   4   const enteredAt = performance.timeOrigin + performance.now();
   5   let detector, canvas, runtime = 'nosimd';
   6   scope.onmessage = async ({ data }) => {
   7     const bitmap = data.bitmap;
   8     let input, returned = false;
   9     // 數字沿原回覆返回；補邊與清理順序仍使用主線流程，關閉計時保持原訊息。
  10     const timing = data.measure ? { enteredAt } : null;
  11     try {
  12       if (!detector) {
  13         let began = timing ? performance.now() : 0;
  14         try { scope.importScripts('/vision-bundle.js'); }
  15         finally { if (timing) timing.scriptImportMs = performance.now() - began; }
  16         // 沿用固定 CPU／模型；能力檢查失敗視為不支援，不拿 UA 或裝置名稱猜測。
  17         let simd = false;
  18         if (!data.compatibility) try { simd = await scope.Vision.FilesetResolver.isSimdSupported(); } catch {}
  19         runtime = simd ? 'simd' : 'nosimd';
  20         if (timing) timing.runtime = runtime;
  21         const prefix = simd ? '/vision-simd' : '/vision-wasm';
  22         began = timing ? performance.now() : 0;
  23         try { detector = await scope.Vision.ObjectDetector.createFromOptions({ wasmLoaderPath: `${prefix}.js`, wasmBinaryPath: `${prefix}.wasm` }, {
  24           baseOptions: { modelAssetPath: '/person-model.tflite', delegate: 'CPU' }, runningMode: 'IMAGE', categoryAllowlist: ['person'], scoreThreshold: 0.35, maxResults: 12,
  25         }); } catch {} finally { if (timing) timing.modelInitMs = performance.now() - began; }
  26         if (!detector) {
  27           if (simd) {
  28             // Emscripten 失敗可能留下半初始化全域；交還同一格，由乾淨 Worker 僅重試一次相容版。
  29             scope.postMessage({ retryCompatibility: true, bitmap, ...(timing ? { timing: { ...timing, runtime } } : {}) }, [bitmap]); returned = true; return;
  30           }
  31           throw new Error('相容人物模型初始化失敗');
  32         }
  33       }
  34       if (timing) timing.runtime = runtime;
  35       // 狹長選框直接送方形模型會改變人體比例；先等比補邊，不擴大到旁人或降低分數門檻。
  36       const size = Math.max(bitmap.width, bitmap.height), x = (size - bitmap.width) / 2, y = (size - bitmap.height) / 2;
  37       canvas ||= new OffscreenCanvas(size, size);
  38       if (canvas.width !== size || canvas.height !== size) { canvas.width = size; canvas.height = size; }
  39       const context = canvas.getContext('2d'); if (!context) throw new Error('無法建立人物分析畫面。');
  40       context.fillStyle = '#ccc'; context.fillRect(0, 0, size, size); context.drawImage(bitmap, x, y);
  41       input = canvas.transferToImageBitmap();
  42       const began = timing ? performance.now() : 0;
  43       let results;
  44       try { results = detector.detect(input); }
  45       finally { if (timing) timing.detectMs = performance.now() - began; }
  46       const clamp = (value, max) => Math.max(0, Math.min(max, value));
  47       // 模型座標先扣掉補邊，再回傳選框內的比例；補邊裡的假框不能變成追蹤目標。
  48       const boxes = results.detections.filter(d => d.boundingBox).map(({ boundingBox: b }) => {
  49         const left = clamp(b.originX - x, bitmap.width), top = clamp(b.originY - y, bitmap.height);
  50         return { x: left / bitmap.width, y: top / bitmap.height, width: (clamp(b.originX + b.width - x, bitmap.width) - left) / bitmap.width, height: (clamp(b.originY + b.height - y, bitmap.height) - top) / bitmap.height };
  51       }).filter(b => b.width > 0 && b.height > 0);
  52       scope.postMessage({ boxes, ...(timing ? { timing } : {}) });
  53     } catch { scope.postMessage({ error: '本機人物偵測無法啟動或分析失敗，請確認應用資源完整後重試。', ...(timing ? { timing } : {}) }); }
  54     finally { input?.close(); if (!returned) bitmap.close(); }
  55   };
  56 }

web/recorder.js — 100% lines
   1 /* 檔案用途：原型的媒體等待、編碼與停止順序。所在層：瀏覽器媒體工具。主要關聯：main.js、RECORD_COMPARE.md。 */
   2 export function event(target, name, signal, timeoutMs = 15_000) {
   3   return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
   4     const finish = (error, value) => {
   5       clearTimeout(timer);
   6       target.removeEventListener(name, success);
   7       target.removeEventListener('error', failure);
   8       signal?.removeEventListener('abort', aborted);
   9       error ? reject(error) : resolve(value);
  10     };
  11     const success = value => finish(null, value);
  12     const failure = () => {
  13       const code = target.error?.code;
  14       finish(new Error(code === 4 ? '此瀏覽器無法播放這個影片格式，可改選 H.264 MP4。' : code === 3 ? '影片解碼失敗，可能內容損壞或編碼不相容。' : '媒體無法解碼或裝置發生錯誤。'));
  15     };
  16     const aborted = () => finish(new DOMException('操作已停止', 'AbortError'));
  17     const timer = setTimeout(() => finish(new Error('等待影片準備逾時，請重新選片或重試。')), timeoutMs);
  18     target.addEventListener(name, success, { once: true });
  19     target.addEventListener('error', failure, { once: true });
  20     signal?.addEventListener('abort', aborted, { once: true });
  21     if (signal?.aborted) aborted();
  22   });
  23 }
  24 
  25 /** @param {EventTarget} target @param {string} name @param {() => void | Promise<unknown>} action @param {AbortSignal} [signal] @param {number} [timeoutMs] */
  26 export async function mediaAction(target, name, action, signal, timeoutMs = 15_000) {
  27   const waiting = new AbortController();
  28   const cancel = () => waiting.abort(signal?.reason);
  29   signal?.addEventListener('abort', cancel, { once: true });
  30   if (signal?.aborted) cancel();
  31   // 啟動失敗與事件等待必須共用清理，否則 play 拒絕或 setter 拋錯會留下逾時與監聽。
  32   const pending = event(target, name, waiting.signal, timeoutMs);
  33   try { await Promise.all([pending, Promise.resolve().then(() => { waiting.signal.throwIfAborted(); return action(); })]); }
  34   finally { waiting.abort(); signal?.removeEventListener('abort', cancel); }
  35 }
  36 
  37 // 啟播承諾完成不代表兩側像素已呈現；先收原生影格通知，輸出才不會錄入啟動等待。
  38 /** @param {HTMLVideoElement} video @param {AbortSignal} signal @param {(frame: VideoFrameCallbackMetadata) => boolean} [accept] */
  39 export async function presentedFrame(video, signal, accept = _frame => true) {
  40   signal.throwIfAborted();
  41   if (!video.requestVideoFrameCallback) { await event(video, 'playing', signal); return; }
  42   return /** @type {Promise<void>} */ (new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
  43     let id, finished = false;
  44     const finish = error => {
  45       if (finished) return;
  46       finished = true; clearTimeout(timer); signal.removeEventListener('abort', aborted);
  47       if (id !== undefined) video.cancelVideoFrameCallback(id);
  48       error ? reject(error) : resolve();
  49     };
  50     const aborted = () => finish(signal.reason);
  51     const timer = setTimeout(() => finish(new Error('等待比較畫面逾時，原片保留，請重新預覽後重試。')), 15_000);
  52     const watch = () => {
  53       try {
  54         id = video.requestVideoFrameCallback((_now, frame) => {
  55           id = undefined;
  56           // 取消後排隊中的舊通知仍可能送達；不能讓不合格影格重新訂閱已結束的暖機。
  57           if (finished) return;
  58           // 暖機用來源時間確認播放確實前進；定位後則只把此通知當成呈現步驟屏障。
  59           try { if (accept(frame)) finish(); else watch(); } catch (error) { finish(error); }
  60         });
  61       } catch (error) { finish(error); }
  62     };
  63     signal.addEventListener('abort', aborted, { once: true }); watch();
  64   }));
  65 }
  66 
  67 export async function seek(video, seconds, signal, waitForFrame = false) {
  68   if (!Number.isFinite(seconds) || seconds < 0) throw new Error('跳轉時間不正確。');
  69   if (video.readyState < 1) throw new Error('影片尚未準備好，請先重新選片。');
  70   signal?.throwIfAborted();
  71   if (!waitForFrame) {
  72     if (Math.abs(video.currentTime - seconds) < 0.001 && video.readyState >= 2) return;
  73     await mediaAction(video, 'seeked', () => { video.currentTime = seconds; }, signal); return;
  74   }
  75   if (!video.requestVideoFrameCallback) throw new Error('此瀏覽器無法確認起始畫面，未開始合成；原片保留。');
  76   const waiting = new AbortController(), cancel = () => waiting.abort(signal?.reason);
  77   signal?.addEventListener('abort', cancel, { once: true });
  78   try {
  79     // 訂閱必須先於 seek；seeked 只代表播放位置完成，不能保證 Canvas 已讀到那一格。
  80     // Safari 暫停回零的唯一通知可能仍帶舊 mediaTime；再等 metadata 歸零會永久卡住。
  81     // 通知與 seeked 分別等待，完成後由呼叫端重畫 Canvas；metadata 不當作像素正確的證據。
  82     const frame = presentedFrame(video, waiting.signal);
  83     await Promise.all([frame, mediaAction(video, 'seeked', () => { video.currentTime = seconds; }, waiting.signal)]);
  84   } finally { waiting.abort(); signal?.removeEventListener('abort', cancel); }
  85 }
  86 
  87 export async function load(video, url, signal) {
  88   await mediaAction(video, 'loadeddata', () => { video.src = url; video.load(); }, signal);
  89   if (!video.videoWidth || !video.videoHeight) throw new Error('選取的檔案沒有可解碼的視訊。');
  90   // Chromium 的即時 WebM 可能沒有 duration；實際 seek 尾端，不能拿 chunks 數猜長度。
  91   if (!Number.isFinite(video.duration)) await seek(video, 1e9, signal);
  92   const durationMs = Math.round(video.duration * 1000);
  93   if (!Number.isSafeInteger(durationMs) || durationMs <= 0) throw new Error('無法讀回影片長度，請先另存原片。');
  94   await seek(video, 0, signal);
  95   return durationMs;
  96 }
  97 
  98 export function cameraError(error, facing) {
  99   switch (error?.name) {
 100     case 'NotAllowedError': case 'SecurityError': return new Error('相機權限未開啟。請在瀏覽器允許相機，再按開啟；不需要麥克風。');
 101     case 'NotFoundError': return new Error('找不到可用相機，請接上相機或換裝置後重試。');
 102     case 'OverconstrainedError': return new Error(facing === 'environment' ? '沒有可用的後鏡頭，請選前鏡頭再重試。' : '目前相機無法使用這組設定，請重新開啟或換相機。');
 103     case 'NotReadableError': case 'AbortError': return new Error('相機可能被其他程式使用或暫時中斷。請關閉其他相機程式，再按開啟重試。');
 104     default: return new Error('相機無法開啟，請確認連接與瀏覽器權限後重試。');
 105   }
 106 }
