 108 /** @param {Array<{index: number, blob: Blob}>} rows */
 109 export function assembleChunks(rows) {
 110   if (!rows.length) throw new Error('沒有可恢復的錄影片段；筆記仍保留。');
 111   const mime = rows.find(row => row.blob?.type)?.blob.type || '', family = mime.split(';')[0];
 112   // 缺首塊、缺號或混合容器不拼成「成功」；原始 chunks 留在庫中，不能靠重置修復。
 113   if (rows.some((row, index) => row.index !== index || !(row.blob instanceof Blob) || !row.blob.size || (row.blob.type && row.blob.type.split(';')[0] !== family))) throw new Error('錄影片段不完整或格式不一致；已保留原資料，不能標示成功。');
 114   return new Blob(rows.map(row => row.blob), { type: mime });
 115 }
 116 
 117 /** @param {MediaStream} stream @param {(blob: Blob) => void} onChunk @param {(timeMs: number) => void} onStart @param {{webmOnly?: boolean}} [options] */
 118 export function record(stream, onChunk = () => {}, onStart = () => {}, { webmOnly = false } = {}) {
 119   if (!globalThis.MediaRecorder) throw new Error('此瀏覽器沒有錄影能力。');
 120   // Safari VS 的 MP4 成檔仍超出覆蓋門檻；專用選項不能默退該路徑，也不能改掉相機／Chrome 順序。
 121   const candidates = webmOnly ? ['video/webm;codecs=vp8,opus'] : ['video/mp4;codecs=avc1.42E01E,mp4a.40.2', 'video/mp4', 'video/webm;codecs=vp8,opus', 'video/webm', ''];
 122   let recorder;
 123   for (const mimeType of candidates) {
 124     if (mimeType && !MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported(mimeType)) continue;
 125     try {
 126       recorder = new MediaRecorder(stream, mimeType ? { mimeType, videoBitsPerSecond: 4_000_000 } : undefined);
 127       // start() 呼叫與原生 start 事件分開觀測，不能冒充影格同步證據。
 128       recorder.addEventListener('start', () => onStart(Math.round(performance.now())), { once: true });
 129       // Safari VS 的週期切塊曾在 stop 遺失整段尾部；只此路徑一次收檔，相機／Chrome 仍逐秒落盤。
 130       if (webmOnly) recorder.start(); else recorder.start(1000);
 131       break;
 132     } catch { recorder = null; }
 133   }
 134   if (!recorder) throw new Error(webmOnly ? '此瀏覽器無法啟動 WebM（VP8／Opus）比較匯出，請改用 Chrome；原片、設定及前次 VS 保留。' : '裝置無法啟動任何候選錄影格式。');
 135   const chunks = [];
 136   let resolveDone, rejectDone, resolveStopped, rejectStopped, failure, finished = false, timer;
 137   const done = /** @type {Promise<Blob>} */ (new Promise((resolve, reject) => { resolveDone = resolve; rejectDone = reject; }));
 138   const stopped = /** @type {Promise<Blob>} */ (new Promise((resolve, reject) => { resolveStopped = resolve; rejectStopped = reject; }));
 139   const data = e => { if (e.data.size) { chunks.push(e.data); onChunk(e.data); } };
 140   const error = () => { failure = new Error('編碼失敗；原始片段保留，請降低負載後重試。'); rejectDone(failure); };
 141   const cleanup = () => {
 142     finished = true; clearTimeout(timer);
 143     recorder.removeEventListener('dataavailable', data); recorder.removeEventListener('error', error); recorder.removeEventListener('stop', stoppedEvent);
 144   };
 145   const stoppedEvent = () => {
 146     // 編碼 error 先通知 owner 停止，但仍等原生 stop 收齊最後一塊，不能提早撤銷落盤監聽。
 147     const blob = new Blob(chunks, { type: recorder.mimeType || chunks[0]?.type }); cleanup();
 148     if (blob.size) { resolveStopped(blob); failure ? rejectDone(failure) : resolveDone(blob); }
 149     else { const empty = failure || new Error('沒有錄到影片資料。'); rejectStopped(empty); rejectDone(empty); }
 150   };
 151   recorder.addEventListener('dataavailable', data); recorder.addEventListener('error', error); recorder.addEventListener('stop', stoppedEvent, { once: true });
 152   done.catch(() => {}); stopped.catch(() => {});
 153   return {
 154     done,
 155     async stop() {
 156       if (!finished && !timer) timer = setTimeout(() => { failure ??= new Error('等待錄影停止逾時；已保留原始片段。'); cleanup(); rejectStopped(failure); rejectDone(failure); }, 15_000);
 157       if (recorder.state !== 'inactive') recorder.stop();
 158       const blob = await stopped;
 159       if (failure) throw failure;
 160       return blob;
 161     },
 162   };
 163 }
 164 
 165 /** @param {Blob} file */
 166 export async function normalizedVideo(file) {
 167   if (!file.size) throw new Error('這是空檔案，請重新選取。');
 168   // 只讀固定小段檔頭補正下載容器；不讀完整原片、不轉碼，也不以此取代實際播放驗證。
 169   const bytes = new Uint8Array(await file.slice(0, 4096).arrayBuffer()), header = String.fromCharCode(...bytes);
 170   const declared = file.type.split(';')[0];
 171   let mime = header.slice(4, 8) === 'ftyp' ? header.slice(8, 12) === 'qt  ' ? 'video/quicktime' : 'video/mp4' : '';
 172   if (bytes[0] === 0x1a && bytes[1] === 0x45 && bytes[2] === 0xdf && bytes[3] === 0xa3) {
 173     if (header.includes('\x42\x82\x84webm')) mime = 'video/webm';
 174     else if (header.includes('\x42\x82\x88matroska')) mime = 'video/x-matroska';
 175   }
 176   mime ||= ['video/mp4', 'video/webm', 'video/quicktime', 'video/x-matroska'].includes(declared) ? declared : '';
 177   if (!mime) throw new Error('無法確認影片容器，請改選 H.264 MP4／MOV 或受測 WebM；原片未修改。');
 178   return declared === mime ? file : new Blob([file], { type: mime });
 179 }
 180 
 181 export function extension(mimeType) {
 182   if (mimeType.startsWith('video/mp4')) return 'mp4';
 183   if (mimeType.startsWith('video/webm')) return 'webm';
 184   if (mimeType.startsWith('video/quicktime')) return 'mov';
 185   if (mimeType.startsWith('video/x-matroska')) return 'mkv';
 186   throw new Error('未知輸出容器，無法提供正確副檔名。');
 187 }

web/storage.js — 100% lines
   1 /* 檔案用途：版本化初始化、筆記 revision 與媒體短交易。所在層：IndexedDB 儲存。主要關聯：library.js、main.js、DATA_FORMAT.md。 */
   2 import { assembleChunks } from './recorder.js';
   3 import { newLibrary, validateBackup, migrateLibrary } from './library.js';
   4 /** @type {Promise<IDBDatabase> | null} */
   5 let database = null;
   6 const stores = ['metadata', 'media', 'chunks'];
   7 // 一旦缺少交易結束收據，本頁不能靠重開連線或重試覆蓋來猜測結果；重新導航後才重新讀回。
   8 const storageFailure = new AbortController();
   9 export const storageUnconfirmedMessage = '保存狀態未確認，不能確認這次寫入已完成或已回滾。本頁已暫停儲存；請先另存筆記、原片與 VS，再重新開啟同一頁讀回。';
  10 export function storageUnconfirmed() { return storageFailure.signal.aborted; }
  11 /** @template T @param {IDBRequest<T>} req @returns {Promise<T>} */
  12 function request(req) {
  13   return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
  14     const cleanup = () => { req.onsuccess = null; req.onerror = null; storageFailure.signal.removeEventListener('abort', unknown); };
  15     const unknown = () => { cleanup(); reject(storageFailure.signal.reason); };
  16     req.onsuccess = () => { cleanup(); resolve(req.result); };
  17     req.onerror = () => { cleanup(); reject(req.error); };
  18     storageFailure.signal.addEventListener('abort', unknown, { once: true });
  19     if (storageUnconfirmed()) unknown();
  20   });
  21 }
  22 /** @param {IDBDatabase} db @param {number} oldVersion */
  23 export function upgradeSchema(db, oldVersion) {
  24   // 目前只有 0→1；未知升級必須回滾，不能猜 schema 或刪庫來繞過 migration。
  25   if (oldVersion !== 0) throw new Error('沒有可用的資料版本升級路徑，舊資料保留。');
  26   for (const name of stores) db.createObjectStore(name);
  27 }
  28 export async function openStore() {
  29   storageFailure.signal.throwIfAborted();
  30   if (database) return database;
  31   database = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
  32     let blocked = false, upgradeError;
  33     const req = indexedDB.open('dance-stamp', 1);
  34     req.onupgradeneeded = e => {
  35       try { upgradeSchema(req.result, e.oldVersion); }
  36       catch (error) { upgradeError = error; req.transaction?.abort(); }
  37     };
  38     req.onblocked = () => { blocked = true; database = null; reject(new Error('請先關閉其他 Dance Stamp 分頁，再重新開啟。')); };
  39     req.onerror = () => { database = null; reject(upgradeError || req.error); };
  40     req.onsuccess = () => {
  41       if (blocked) { req.result.close(); return; }
  42       if (!stores.every(name => req.result.objectStoreNames.contains(name))) {
  43         req.result.close(); database = null; reject(new Error('資料庫結構不完整；沒有刪除舊資料，請先保留備份。')); return;
  44       }
  45       req.result.onversionchange = () => { req.result.close(); database = null; };
  46       resolve(req.result);
  47     };
  48   });
  49   return database;
  50 }
  51 /** @param {IDBTransaction} tx @param {AbortSignal} [signal] */
  52 function completed(tx, signal) {
  53   let abort = () => {};
  54   /** @type {Promise<void>} */
  55   const done = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
  56     /** @type {ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined} */
  57     let timer;
  58     const cleanup = () => {
  59       clearTimeout(timer);
  60       tx.removeEventListener('complete', complete); tx.removeEventListener('abort', aborted); tx.removeEventListener('error', watch, true);
  61       signal?.removeEventListener('abort', abort); storageFailure.signal.removeEventListener('abort', unknown);
  62     };
  63     const complete = () => { cleanup(); resolve(); };
  64     const aborted = () => { cleanup(); reject(tx.error || new Error('資料未寫入，請保留草稿後重試。')); };
  65     const unknown = () => { cleanup(); reject(storageFailure.signal.reason); };
  66     const watch = () => {
  67       // error／取消後等一段結束通知寬限；期限只代表「未確認」，絕不代表成功或回滾。
  68       timer ??= setTimeout(() => {
  69         const error = new Error(storageUnconfirmedMessage); error.name = 'StorageUnconfirmedError';
  70         storageFailure.abort(error);
  71         // 盡力停止原生寫入；abort 返回也不是結束收據，不能改稱已回滾。
  72         try { tx.abort(); } catch { /* 未確認狀態保持到本頁重新導航。 */ }
  73         // close 只停止接受新交易，不能證明卡住的交易已結束，也不清除資料。
  74         try { tx.db.close(); } catch { /* 本頁仍以共同旗標阻止重用連線。 */ }
  75       }, 1000);
  76     };
  77     abort = () => { watch(); try { tx.abort(); } catch { /* 仍等原生收據；沒有收據就回報未確認。 */ } };
  78     tx.addEventListener('complete', complete); tx.addEventListener('abort', aborted); tx.addEventListener('error', watch, true);
  79     signal?.addEventListener('abort', abort, { once: true });
  80     storageFailure.signal.addEventListener('abort', unknown, { once: true });
  81     if (signal?.aborted) abort();
  82   });
  83   return { done, abort };
  84 }
  85 /** @returns {Promise<import('./types').LibraryState>} */
  86 export async function readLibrary() {
  87   const db = await openStore(), tx = db.transaction(['metadata', 'media']);
  88   const [saved, keys] = await Promise.all([request(tx.objectStore('metadata').get('library')), request(tx.objectStore('media').getAllKeys())]);
  89   if (!saved) return { revision: 0, data: newLibrary() };
  90   if (!Number.isSafeInteger(saved.revision) || saved.revision < 1) throw new Error('筆記版本無效；沒有重置資料。');
  91   validateBackup(saved.data);
  92   /** @type {import('./types').LibraryState} */
  93   const result = { ...saved, data: migrateLibrary(saved.data) };
  94   const present = new Set(keys);
  95   const unfinished = new Set(result.data.takes.filter(t => ['recording', 'interrupted'].includes(t.state)).map(t => t.cameraAssetId));
  96   for (const asset of result.data.assets) {
  97     asset.storageKind = present.has(asset.id) ? 'indexeddb' : 'external';
  98     asset.state = present.has(asset.id) ? 'ready' : 'missing';
  99     if (!present.has(asset.id) && unfinished.has(asset.id)) asset.state = 'interrupted';
 100   }
 101   // 讀取不改寫正在錄製的 take；另一分頁可能仍握有錄影鎖，只有明確恢復才能 finalize。
 102   return result;
 103 }
 104 /** @param {import('./types').Library} next @param {number} expectedRevision @param {{id: string, blob?: Blob}} [mediaChange] @param {AbortSignal} [signal] @param {{mediaIds: string[], chunkIds: string[]}} [cleanup] */
 105 export async function writeLibrary(next, expectedRevision, mediaChange, signal, cleanup) {
 106   validateBackup(next);
 107   if (!Number.isSafeInteger(expectedRevision) || expectedRevision < 0) throw new Error('筆記版本無效，未寫入。');
 108   if (mediaChange && !next.assets.some(a => a.id === mediaChange.id)) throw new Error('媒體沒有對應資料記錄，未寫入。');
 109   if (cleanup) {
 110     const retained = new Set(next.assets.map(a => a.id));
 111     if ([...cleanup.mediaIds, ...cleanup.chunkIds].some(id => retained.has(id))) throw new Error('清理仍有關聯的資料遭拒，原資料保留。');
 112   }
 113   const db = await openStore(); signal?.throwIfAborted();
 114   const tx = db.transaction(cleanup ? ['metadata', 'media', 'chunks'] : ['metadata', 'media'], 'readwrite');
 115   const { done, abort } = completed(tx, signal), store = tx.objectStore('metadata');
 116   // 取消匯入或重連須取消同一個原生交易，不能只關畫面後仍偷偷寫入。
 117   let failure;
 118   const req = store.get('library');
 119   req.onsuccess = () => {
 120     // revision 與 metadata／Blob 在同一交易；失敗不能留下半份配方或蓋過其他分頁。
 121     if (storageUnconfirmed()) { abort(); return; }
 122     if ((req.result?.revision || 0) !== expectedRevision) { failure = new Error('另一分頁已更新筆記。請先保留草稿，再重新開啟此頁。'); abort(); return; }
 123     try {
 124       store.put({ revision: expectedRevision + 1, data: next }, 'library');
 125       if (mediaChange) mediaChange.blob ? tx.objectStore('media').put(mediaChange.blob, mediaChange.id) : tx.objectStore('media').delete(mediaChange.id);
 126       // metadata、失去關聯的副本及 chunks 同一交易；中斷不能留下半刪的筆記。
 127       if (cleanup) {
 128         for (const id of cleanup.mediaIds) tx.objectStore('media').delete(id);
 129         for (const id of cleanup.chunkIds) tx.objectStore('chunks').delete(IDBKeyRange.bound([id, 0], [id, Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER]));
 130       }
 131     } catch (error) { failure = error; abort(); }
 132   };
 133   try { await done; } catch (error) { throw storageUnconfirmed() ? storageFailure.signal.reason : signal?.aborted ? signal.reason : failure || error; }
 134   finally { req.onsuccess = null; }
 135   return { revision: expectedRevision + 1, data: next };
 136 }
 137 /** @param {string} id @returns {Promise<Blob | undefined>} */
 138 export async function readMedia(id) { const db = await openStore(); return request(db.transaction('media').objectStore('media').get(id)); }
 139 export async function readMediaKeys() { const db = await openStore(); return request(db.transaction('media').objectStore('media').getAllKeys()); }
 140 /** @param {string} id @param {number} index @param {Blob} blob */
 141 export async function saveChunk(id, index, blob) {
 142   if (!Number.isSafeInteger(index) || index < 0 || !(blob instanceof Blob) || !blob.size) throw new Error('錄影片段的順序或內容無效，未寫入。');
 143   const db = await openStore(), tx = db.transaction('chunks', 'readwrite'), { done, abort } = completed(tx);
 144   // 同一塊不能覆寫；重複通知必須保留第一份並回報錯誤。
 145   let failure;
 146   try { tx.objectStore('chunks').add(blob, [id, index]); } catch (error) { failure = error; abort(); }
 147   try { await done; } catch (error) { throw storageUnconfirmed() ? storageFailure.signal.reason : failure || error; }
 148 }
 149 /** @param {string} id */
 150 export async function recoverChunks(id) {
 151   const db = await openStore(), store = db.transaction('chunks').objectStore('chunks'), range = IDBKeyRange.bound([id, 0], [id, Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER]);
 152   const [chunks, keys] = await Promise.all([request(store.getAll(range)), request(store.getAllKeys(range))]);
 153   const blob = assembleChunks(chunks.map((blob, index) => ({ index: Array.isArray(keys[index]) && keys[index].length === 2 ? Number(keys[index][1]) : -1, blob })));
 154   // Safari 可能讓組合 Blob 仍共用 chunks 的底層檔案；先取得獨立 bytes，清 chunks 才不會連新原片一起失效。
 155   const bytes = await blob.arrayBuffer();
 156   if (bytes.byteLength !== blob.size) throw new Error('錄影片段讀回長度不符；原片段仍保留，請重試。');
 157   return new Blob([bytes], { type: blob.type });
 158 }
 159 /** @param {string} id */
 160 export async function clearChunks(id) {
 161   const db = await openStore(), tx = db.transaction('chunks', 'readwrite'), { done, abort } = completed(tx);
 162   let failure;
 163   try { tx.objectStore('chunks').delete(IDBKeyRange.bound([id, 0], [id, Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER])); } catch (error) { failure = error; abort(); }
 164   try { await done; } catch (error) { throw storageUnconfirmed() ? storageFailure.signal.reason : failure || error; }
 165 }
 166 /** @template T @param {string} id @param {() => Promise<T>} action @returns {Promise<T>} */
 167 export async function withTakeLock(id, action) {
 168   if (!navigator.locks) throw new Error('此瀏覽器無法安全處理錄影恢復，請改用受測的桌面 Chrome；筆記與播放器仍可使用。');
 169   return navigator.locks.request(`dance-stamp:take:${id}`, { ifAvailable: true }, lock => {
 170     if (!lock) throw new Error('另一個分頁仍在錄影或恢復這個 take，請先停止該分頁。');
 171     return action();
 172   });
 173 }

web/sw.js — 100% lines
   1 /* 檔案用途：只快取靜態應用資源，讓已載入的 Web 版可離線重開。所在層：Service Worker。主要關聯：build-web.ts、main.js。 */
   2 /** @type {ServiceWorkerGlobalScope} */
   3 const worker = /** @type {ServiceWorkerGlobalScope} */ (/** @type {unknown} */ (self));
   4 const version = '__BUILD__', cacheName = `dance-stamp-web-${version}`;
   5 /** @type {Record<string, string>} */
   6 const manifest = /* __MANIFEST__ */ {};
   7 const files = Object.keys(manifest);
   8 async function matches(response, path) {
   9   if (!response?.ok) return false;
  10   const hash = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', await response.clone().arrayBuffer());
  11   return [...new Uint8Array(hash)].map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('') === manifest[path];
  12 }
  13 worker.addEventListener('install', e => {
  14   // 安裝不自動 skipWaiting／claim；整批驗證後才等待，失敗只移除這個未完成的新版 cache。
  15   e.waitUntil((async () => {
  16     try {
  17       const cache = await caches.open(cacheName);
  18       for (const path of files) {
  19         const response = await fetch(new Request(path, { cache: 'reload' }));
  20         if (!await matches(response, path)) throw new Error('應用資源不完整，舊版保留。');
  21         await cache.put(path, response);
  22       }
  23     } catch (error) { await caches.delete(cacheName); throw error; }
  24   })());
  25 });
  26 worker.addEventListener('activate', e => e.waitUntil(caches.keys().then(keys => Promise.all(keys.filter(k => k.startsWith('dance-stamp-web-') && k !== cacheName).map(k => caches.delete(k))))));
  27 worker.addEventListener('fetch', e => {
  28   const url = new URL(e.request.url);
  29   // 影片、筆記、其他 origin 及非 GET 不進 SW；媒體保存在獨立的本機儲存層。
  30   if (e.request.method !== 'GET' || url.origin !== worker.location.origin || !files.includes(url.pathname)) return;
  31   e.respondWith((async () => {
  32     /** @type {Cache | undefined} */
  33     let cache;
  34     try {
  35       cache = await caches.open(cacheName);
  36       const cached = await cache.match(url.pathname);
  37       if (cached && await matches(cached, url.pathname)) return cached;
  38     } catch { /* 快取暫時無法讀取時仍可在線取回，但不能略過下面的版本驗證。 */ }
  39     // 舊版缺檔時不能從新版主機混入不相容模組；符合本版雜湊才可補回。
  40     const response = await fetch(e.request);
  41     if (!await matches(response, url.pathname)) return new Response('應用版本不完整，請先備份並關閉所有本站分頁後重開。', { status: 503 });
  42     // 容量不足不能讓正確的在線頁面也失效；未補存仍由離線檢查誠實回報不完整。
  43     try { await cache?.put(url.pathname, response.clone()); } catch { /* 本次回應可用，不代表已保存副本。 */ }
  44     return response;
  45   })());
  46 });
  47 worker.addEventListener('message', e => {
  48   if (e.data?.type === 'ACTIVATE_UPDATE' && e.ports[0]) {
  49     e.waitUntil((async () => {
  50       try {
  51         // 同意只涵蓋發起的分頁；其他舊頁可能正在錄影或有草稿，不能一併強制接管。
  52         const pages = await worker.clients.matchAll({ type: 'window', includeUncontrolled: true });
  53         if (pages.length !== 1 || !e.source || !('id' in e.source) || pages[0].id !== e.source.id) throw new Error('其他本站分頁仍開著。請先保存並關閉其他分頁，再按「立即更新」；目前工作保留。');
  54         await worker.skipWaiting();
  55       } catch (error) { e.ports[0].postMessage({ error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : '更新未完成，請稍後再試。' }); }
  56     })());
  57     return;
  58   }
  59   if (e.data?.type !== 'CHECK_OFFLINE' || !e.ports[0]) return;
  60   e.waitUntil((async () => {
  61     try {
  62       const cache = await caches.open(cacheName), missing = [];
  63       // API 存在不能當成離線就緒；被清掉或損壞的實際資源也要顯示缺少。
  64       for (const path of files) if (!await matches(await cache.match(path), path)) missing.push(path);
  65       e.ports[0].postMessage({ version, complete: files.length > 0 && missing.length === 0, missing });
  66     } catch { e.ports[0].postMessage({ version, complete: false }); }
  67   })());
  68 });

web/timeline.js — 100% lines
   1 /* 檔案用途：驗證 M0 比較配方與共同時間。所在層：純媒體邏輯。主要關聯：RECORD_COMPARE.md、main.js。 */
   2 // 比較、備份與 Safari 音訊共用同一上限，避免長片在選取後才被其他流程拒絕。
   3 export const MAX_COMPARISON_MS = 300_000;
   4 /** @param {number} startMs @param {number | undefined} endMs @param {number} durationMs */
   5 export function validateMarkerRange(startMs, endMs, durationMs) {
   6   // 表單與備份共用毫秒邊界，避免重連或匯入後出現另一套合法區間。
   7   if (!Number.isSafeInteger(durationMs) || durationMs <= 0
   8     || !Number.isSafeInteger(startMs) || startMs < 0
   9     || (endMs !== undefined && (!Number.isSafeInteger(endMs) || endMs < 0))) throw new Error('標記時間必須是非負整數毫秒。');
  10   if (endMs !== undefined && endMs <= startMs) throw new Error('標記終點必須晚於起點，不會自動交換。');
  11   if ((endMs ?? startMs) > durationMs) throw new Error('標記超出影片長度。');
  12 }
  13 
  14 export function comparison(recipe) {
  15   const { startMs, endMs, rate, offsetMs, selfDurationMs } = recipe;
  16   if (![startMs, endMs, offsetMs, selfDurationMs].every(Number.isSafeInteger)
  17     || startMs < 0 || endMs <= startMs || selfDurationMs <= 0 || ![0.5, 0.75, 1].includes(rate)) {
  18     throw new Error('片段、速度或自拍長度無效。');
  19   }
  20   const practiceMs = (endMs - startMs) / rate;
  21   if (practiceMs > MAX_COMPARISON_MS) throw new Error('請縮短片段：放慢後每次比較最多 5 分鐘。');
  22   // 正偏移是略過自拍開頭；負偏移必須連來源音訊一起裁切，否則會有聲畫錯位。
  23   const lowerMs = Math.max(0, -offsetMs);
  24   const upperMs = Math.min(practiceMs, selfDurationMs - offsetMs);
  25   if (upperMs <= lowerMs) throw new Error('兩支影片沒有可比較的共同區間。');
  26   return { lowerMs, upperMs, durationMs: upperMs - lowerMs };
  27 }
  28 
  29 export function captureDuration({ startMs, endMs, rate }) {
  30   const { durationMs } = comparison({ startMs, endMs, rate, offsetMs: 0, selfDurationMs: MAX_COMPARISON_MS });
  31   // 相機仍維持原驗收範圍；不能把長比較截成 60 秒後誤當成合法的新錄影。
  32   if (durationMs < 15_000 || durationMs > 60_000) throw new Error('請調整片段或速度，讓實際錄影時間介於 15 到 60 秒。');
  33   return durationMs;
  34 }
  35 
  36 export function positions(recipe, elapsedMs) {
  37   const { lowerMs, durationMs } = comparison(recipe);
  38   if (!Number.isFinite(elapsedMs) || elapsedMs < 0 || elapsedMs > durationMs) throw new Error('時間超出共同區間。');
  39   const timeMs = lowerMs + elapsedMs;
  40   return { referenceMs: recipe.startMs + recipe.rate * timeMs, selfMs: timeMs + recipe.offsetMs };
  41 }
  42 
  43 export function contain(width, height, boxWidth, boxHeight) {
  44   if (![width, height, boxWidth, boxHeight].every(n => Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0)) throw new Error('影片尺寸無效。');
  45   const scale = Math.min(boxWidth / width, boxHeight / height);
  46   return { width: width * scale, height: height * scale, x: (boxWidth - width * scale) / 2, y: (boxHeight - height * scale) / 2 };
  47 }
